### [1] [Surprisingly normal? GW241011 and GW241110 in the context of hierarchical merger classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40774)
*Cailin Plunkett,Michael Zevin,Salvatore Vitale*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 论文提出"可分类分布（classifiable distribution）"概念，超越可探测性评估事件能否被正确归入特定族群，并以层级合并候选体（hierarchical merger candidates）GW241011与GW241110的意外低质量为契机，分析参数空间中先验上能否支持可靠测量的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辨别双黑洞合并的形成通道（formation channel）是引力波天体物理（gravitational-wave astrophysics）的核心目标，但测量不精确与理论预言不确定性使单个事件归类困难；测量精度随参数空间变化，单纯基于推断性质的分类指标忽视了这一差异。

Method: 以GW241011和GW241110的低质量为切入点，构建"可分类分布（classifiable distribution）"以刻画事件可被正确表征为某一族群成员的条件；并通过模拟层级合并体分析内禀与外禀参数对分类识别的影响。

Result: 质量比（mass ratio）是层级合并识别中最大的内禀驱动因素，轨道倾角（orbital inclination）与信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）是关键外禀因素；GW241011与GW241110的分类指标与类似模拟层级合并体一致。

Conclusion: 模型选择（model selection）与分类必须置于测量能力的语境中理解，尤其当引力波事件目录（catalog）中涌现越来越多性质看似异常的事件时。

Abstract: Uncovering the origins of merging binary black holes is a key goal in gravitational-wave astrophysics, but identifying the formation channel of a particular binary is complicated by imprecise measurements and uncertain theoretical predictions. While classification often involves computing metrics from an event's inferred properties, measurement precision varies across parameter space. Using the perhaps-surprising low masses of the hierarchical merger candidates GW241011 and GW241110 as a backdrop, we consider the classifiable distribution, which steps beyond detectability to assess what events may be correctly characterized as part of a population. Interpreting classification outcomes, and assessing the falsifiability of theoretical model predictions, are benefit by understanding the regions of parameter space that a priori enable---and do not enable---confident measurement. For hierarchical mergers, we demonstrate that mass ratio is the largest intrinsic driver of identification, while orbital inclination and signal-to-noise ratio are the key extrinsic factors. Further, we show GW241011 and GW241110 achieve classification metrics consistent with similar simulated hierarchical mergers. Model selection and classification require the context of measurement capacity, especially as catalogs offer growing numbers of events with seemingly unusual properties.

</details>

### [2] [Tunable Three-Body Interactions in Microwave-Shielded Polar Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41003)
*Hanwei Yang,Tijs Karman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 微波屏蔽（microwave shielding）在使超冷极性分子（ultracold polar molecules）获得碰撞稳定性的同时，会产生与屏蔽相互作用本身量级相当的真实非可加三体相互作用（nonadditive three-body interactions）；其大小、符号与几何依赖可通过微波参数与取向灵活调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波屏蔽被用于获得碰撞稳定的超冷极性分子体系，但屏蔽场本身是否会引入额外多体相互作用此前未被明确纳入考量；本文旨在指出这一效应并强调其在量子模拟（quantum simulation）中的必要性。

Method: 基于对微波屏蔽下超冷极性分子相互作用的分析，考察非可加三体相互作用的量级、符号与几何依赖，并探讨其随微波参数与分子取向的可调控性，以及典型光晶格（optical lattice）中超极性分子（ultrapolar molecules）的相互作用强度。

Result: 非可加三体相互作用与屏蔽相互作用量级相当；其强度、符号和几何依赖性可由微波参数与取向灵活控制；在典型光晶格中，超极性分子的相互作用强度可达十千赫兹（ten kilohertz）量级。

Conclusion: 该非可加三体相互作用可稳定新颖的多体相（many-body phases），在基于微波屏蔽分子的量子模拟中应予以考虑。

Abstract: We show that microwave shielding, which enables collisionally stable ultracold polar molecules, also leads to genuine nonadditive three-body interactions comparable to the shielding interaction itself. Their magnitude, sign, and geometrical dependence can be flexibly controlled through microwave parameters and orientation. Interaction strengths can reach the ten kilohertz-scale for ultrapolar molecules in typical optical lattices. The nonadditive three-body interactions can stabilize novel many-body phases, and should be accounted for in quantum simulation with microwave shielded molecules.

</details>


### [3] [Minimal model of sticky collisions in modulated optical traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41075)
*Hubert J. Jóźwiak,Tijs Karman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出一个最小量子散射模型（minimal quantum scattering model），将碰撞视为调制光阱中发生的相干过程，说明长程势的量子反射（quantum reflection）根本改变了损失动力学，从而消解了近期实验与理论预测粘附时间（sticking time）之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷双碱分子（ultracold bialkali molecules）的碰撞损失被认为源于碰撞复合物的光激发，但近期调制光阱（modulated optical trap）测量显示存活率异常偏低，与理论预测及早期实验相冲突，需要新的理论框架解释这一张力。

Method: 构建一个最小量子散射模型，把碰撞处理为在调制阱存在下发生的相干过程（coherent process），并纳入长程势的量子反射效应。

Result: 量子反射被证明从根本上改变损失动力学（loss dynamics），消除了近期实验与预测粘附时间之间的矛盾；模型给出当前唯象模型（phenomenological model）所不具备的可检验预测。

Conclusion: 该框架预测损失强烈依赖碰撞能量（collision energy）、初始分子态（initial molecular state）与长程相互作用（long-range interactions），为区分机制提供了新的实验判据。

Abstract: Collisional loss of ultracold bialkali molecules is predicted to occur through photo-excitation of collision complexes. However, recent measurements in modulated optical traps report an unexpected lack of excess survival, at odds with theoretical predictions and earlier experiments. We develop a minimal quantum scattering model that treats collisions as a coherent process occurring in the presence of the modulated trap. We demonstrate that quantum reflection from the long-range potential fundamentally alters loss dynamics, removing the tension between recent experiments and predicted sticking times. This framework provides new testable predictions such as a strong dependence on collision energy, initial molecular state, and long-range interactions, that are absent in the currently used phenomenological model.

</details>


### [4] [Pair-Condensation Peak in Radio-Frequency Dimer-Projection Spectroscopy of a Unitary Fermi Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41770)
*Zhixiu Peng,Zhenhua Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出点二聚体近似（point-dimer approximation），将射频二聚体投影谱（radio-frequency dimer-projection spectroscopy）表示为初始多体态的配对关联，并预言谱中存在准粒子连续谱与配对凝聚峰两类特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频二聚体投影谱已成为探测强相互作用费米气体（strongly interacting Fermi gases）的有力手段，但其频率分辨谱尚未被充分研究，其谱结构的微观起源不明确。

Method: 利用分子尺度与多体尺度之间的分离，发展点二聚体近似（point-dimer approximation），直接将射频响应表达为初始多体态中的配对关联（pair correlations）；并在BCS平均场理论（BCS mean-field theory）框架下推导频率依赖谱。

Result: 谱呈现两类定性不同的特征：由费米子配对（fermionic pairing）支配的宽准粒子连续谱（quasiparticle continuum），以及由凝聚库珀对（condensed Cooper pairs）相干投影到末态二聚体产生的尖锐配对凝聚峰（pair-condensation peak）；连续谱具有由超流能隙（superfluid gap）决定的阈值。

Conclusion: 该理论确立了射频二聚体投影谱作为短程关联（short-range correlations）、费米子配对与长程相干性（long-range coherence）的统一探针。

Abstract: Radio-frequency dimer-projection spectroscopy has recently emerged as a powerful probe of strongly interacting Fermi gases, yet its frequency-resolved spectrum remains largely unexplored. Here we show that the spectrum exhibits two qualitatively distinct features: a broad quasiparticle continuum governed by fermionic pairing and a sharp pair-condensation peak arising from the coherent projection of condensed Cooper pairs onto the final-state dimer. To reveal this physics, we develop a point-dimer approximation that exploits the separation between the molecular and many-body length scales and expresses the radio-frequency response directly in terms of pair correlations in the initial many-body state. Within the BCS mean-field theory, we derive the frequency-dependent spectrum, identify the microscopic transfer processes underlying its structure, and show that the continuum possesses a threshold determined by the superfluid gap. Our theory establishes radio-frequency dimer-projection spectroscopy as a unified probe of short-range correlations, fermionic pairing, and long-range coherence in strongly interacting Fermi gases.

</details>

### [5] [Mpemba effect as an entropy-production race:Finite-amplitude bounds on crossing times in Markovian relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40704)
*Mesfin Taye*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将两种初态的弛豫视为“熵产生竞赛（entropy-production race）”，把相对熵（relative entropy）首次相交时刻用归一化熵产生率之差的累积积分表示，并给出严格的双向时间界。在三态可逆强模型中得到 $0.1659\le t_M\le0.1674$，区间宽度小于 $1\%$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是解释为何两个在共同最终马尔可夫生成元（Markov generator）下演化的典型初态可表现出姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）：它们激发不同的弛豫模式，导致相对熵出现交叉。作者希望为这一交叉时刻提供可计算的严格界，而非仅依赖小偏差展开或数值观测。

Method: 把弛豫建模为熵产生竞赛，利用精确平衡关系 $-\dot D=\dot e_p$，将相交条件写为归一化熵产生率（$r_\alpha=\dot e_p^\alpha/D_\alpha$）差的累积积分等于初始对数亏损（logarithmic deficit）$\ln[D_H(0)/D_C(0)]$。结合全局熵衰减不等式（entropy-decay inequalities）、修正对数 Sobolev 常数（modified logarithmic Sobolev constant）与谱间隙（spectral gap）的比较、相对熵比较不等式及马尔可夫收缩性（Markov contraction），给出有限振幅的交叉时间界，并用外向舍入区间算术（outward-rounded interval arithmetic）独立验证。

Result: 对精确可解的三态强可逆制备，得到 $0.1659\le t_M\le0.1674$（模型时间单位），区间窄于 $1\%$，且包含 $t_M=0.1662209$；保守的全局速率比较只给出 $t_M\ge0.042$。较热初态起始偏离平衡 $6.12$ 倍，却在 $t=0.233$ 达到 $D=10^{-5}$，而较冷初态需 $t=0.844$；平均能量（mean energy）的交叉晚于相对熵。慢本征函数（slowest eigenfunction）的非零三阶矩（cubic moment）足以使修正对数 Sobolev 常数严格低于谱间隙。

Conclusion: 结果把姆潘巴型交叉时刻转化为可严格界定的熵产生率失衡问题，并在有限状态、指定距离度量下给出高精度区间。对固定的驱动生成元，非绝热熵产生（nonadiabatic entropy production）支配同一平衡关系。作者明确限定结论仅适用于有限状态弛豫，不用于预测水的冻结时间。

Abstract: Two canonical preparations evolving under a common final Markov generator can exhibit the Mpemba effect because they excite different relaxation modes. We bound the first crossing of their relative entropies by treating relaxation as an entropy-production race. The exact balance $-\dot D=\dot e_p$ implies that a meeting occurs when the accumulated \emph{difference} of normalized entropy-production rates, $\int_0^t(r_H-r_C)\,\mathrm ds$ with $r_α=\dot e_p^α/D_α$, equals the initial logarithmic deficit $\ln[D_H(0)/D_C(0)]$. Global entropy-decay inequalities yield a lower crossing-time bound when the entropy-decay constant is bounded from below and the hot rate from above; we supply both certificates. A nonzero cubic moment of a slowest eigenfunction is sufficient for the modified logarithmic Sobolev constant to lie strictly below the spectral gap. Combining established relative-entropy comparison inequalities with Markov contraction and the actual mode amplitudes gives finite-amplitude crossing bounds without a small-deviation expansion. For an exactly strong, reversible three-state preparation these give $0.1659\le t_M\le0.1674$ in model time units, independently certified by outward-rounded interval arithmetic. This interval is narrower than $1\%$ and contains $t_M=0.1662209$. The conservative global-rate comparison used here gives only $t_M\ge0.042$. The hotter preparation starts $6.12$ times farther from equilibrium, yet it reaches $D=10^{-5}$ at $t=0.233$, compared with $t=0.844$ for the colder one. Mean energy crosses later than relative entropy. For a fixed driven generator, nonadiabatic entropy production governs the same balance. These results concern finite-state relaxation in a specified distance and do not predict water-freezing times.

</details>


### [6] [Theory of Convective Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40862)
*Žiga Krajnik,Takato Yoshimura,Alvise Bastianello,Enej Ilievski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一维无自相互作用、含相互作用声模（sound modes）系统的扩散尺度流体力学（hydrodynamic）理论，揭示除常规扩散外的两种由声模碰撞产生的额外扩散通道，并给出对对流扩散（convective diffusion）的精确关联函数与普适非高斯分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散尺度动力学描述主要基于由快衰减非流体力学模式噪声驱动的常规扩散（normal diffusion），而对声模之间碰撞所导致的额外扩散机制缺乏统一的流体力学刻画，尤其缺乏对可积系统（integrable systems）中反常涨落的系统性理解。

Method: 采用流体力学理论构建方法，在一维无自相互作用、含相互作用声模的系统中推导扩散尺度动力学：将混沌系统（chaotic systems）中的高斯弛豫过程分解为常规扩散、表现为有效乘性时间噪声（multiplicative temporal noise）的对流扩散，以及由周围流体力学模式产生的有效加性噪声（additive noise）三类通道；并把广义流体力学（generalized hydrodynamics）描述的可积系统作为对流扩散的纯实现纳入框架。

Result: 针对对流扩散，得到高阶动力学关联函数（higher-point dynamical correlation functions）的精确闭式表达式，并与常规扩散的结构进行对比；进一步表明在对流扩散中，每个声模在其共动参考系（comoving frame）内的电荷转移呈现普适的非高斯分布（non-Gaussian distribution）。

Conclusion: 声模碰撞构成扩散尺度上独立于微观噪声的扩散通道，其中对流扩散对应可积系统的纯实现；其普适非高斯电荷转移分布确立了反常涨落（anomalous fluctuations）作为可积系统扩散尺度动力学的特征性质。

Abstract: We obtain a hydrodynamic theory of diffusive-scale dynamics in one-dimensional systems with interacting sound modes in the absence of self-interactions. Chaotic systems generically feature three distinct Gaussian relaxation processes: besides normal diffusion, driven by microscopic noise stemming from fast-decaying non-hydrodynamic modes, collisions between sound modes lead to two additional channels: convective diffusion manifesting as an effective multiplicative temporal noise, and an effective additive noise generated by the surrounding hydrodynamic modes. Our theory also includes, as a subclass, integrable systems described by generalized hydrodynamics, representing a pure realization of convective diffusion. Focusing on convective diffusion, we obtain an exact closed-form expression for the higher-point dynamical correlation functions and contrast their structure with those of normal diffusion. Additionally, we show that in convective diffusion each sound mode features a universal non-Gaussian distribution of charge transfer in its comoving frame, thereby establishing anomalous fluctuations as a characteristic feature of diffusive-scale dynamics in integrable systems.

</details>


### [7] [Faster-than-power-law decay of order parameter correlations in the two-dimensional RP$^2$ model: a rigorous result by vibe-proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41256)
*Masaki Oshikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作针对二维方格上的RP$^2$与RP$^3$模型，由AI（ChatGPT 6 Astra）给出严格证明：任意有限逆温度$\beta\ge0$下，序参量两点关联函数的绝对值上界为$C_\beta\exp\{-c_\beta[\log(2+r)]^2\}$，即快于任意幂律衰减；证明已在Lean中形式化验证并公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RP$^2$模型以指向矢（director）替代Heisenberg自旋，允许由$\pi_1(RP^2)=Z_2$分类的点涡旋缺陷，文献中长期猜测其可能产生类似O(2)（XY）模型的BKT转变或具有幂律关联的临界相/临界点，但因问题困难，相图始终无定论。

Method: 采用严格数学证明（由人工智能Artificial Intelligence，具体为ChatGPT 6 Astra给出），并将证明在Lean证明助手中形式化（formalized）与机器验证；同时辅以基于大距离下单参数有效理论拟设（one-parameter effective theory ansatz）的启发式论证。

Result: 证明了对二维方格上RP$^2$与RP$^3$模型，任意有限逆温度$\beta\ge0$的两点序参量关联函数绝对值满足次指数型上界$C_\beta\exp\{-c_\beta[\log(2+r)]^2\}$，即衰减快于任意$r$的幂次；形式化代码公开于doi:10.5281/zenodo.23548847。

Conclusion: 严格关联界与启发式有效理论论证共同提示（但未证明）$Z_2$涡旋在任何非零温度下均处于解绑（unbound）状态，即不存在类似BKT的临界相；该结论的严格性限于关联衰减界，涡旋行为的判定仍属猜想。

Abstract: We consider the RP$^2$ model on the two-dimensional square lattice with nearest-neighbor interactions. It is obtained by replacing the spin in the standard classical Heisenberg model with a director, which does not distinguish two opposite directions. Unlike the standard classical Heisenberg model or the O(3) nonlinear sigma model, the RP$^2$ (and its cousin RP$^3$) model allows point vortex defects classified by $π_1(RP^2)= Z_2$ in two dimensions. It is tempting to expect the $Z_2$ vortices to induce a phase transition analogous to the well-established Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition in the O(2) model (XY model) in two dimensions. Indeed, the possibilities of a critical phase or a critical point with power-law correlations of the order parameter in the RP$^2$ and RP$^3$ models have been discussed in the literature. However, the challenging nature of the problem has prevented a definitive conclusion on the phase diagram so far. In this paper, we report a rigorous mathematical proof by Artificial Intelligence (ChatGPT 6 Astra) that the absolute value of the two-point correlation function of order parameters at distance $r$ in the RP$^2$ and RP$^3$ models on the two-dimensional square lattice is bounded above by $C_β\exp{(-c_β[\log(2+r)]^2)}$ for any finite inverse temperature $β\ge0$, with positive constants $C_β$ and $c_β$ depending only on $β$. Namely, the correlation function decays faster than any power of $r$. The proof is formalized and verified in Lean; the formalization is publicly available at doi:10.5281/zenodo.23548847 . A heuristic argument based on a one-parameter effective theory ansatz at large distances combined with the rigorous correlation bound suggests (but does not prove) that the $Z_2$ vortices are always unbound at any nonzero temperature.

</details>


### [8] [Coordination three heals, coordination four is hard: the antiferromagnetic Potts model with a costly third state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41277)
*Jeehoon Choi,Suhwan Yoo,Chaeyeon Kim,Hee Young Kwon,Changyeon Won*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究证明反铁磁三态 Potts 模型（three-state Potts model）的基态问题难度仅由最大配位数（maximum coordination）Δ 决定：Δ≤3 时基态能量可化为 Ising 基态受挫键数 fr 的简单表达式并多项式可解，平面 Δ=4 时在 0≤D₃<2 区间 NP 完全，D₃≥2 时多项式可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三态 Potts 模型在第三个态带有单离子能量代价（single-ion energy cost）D₃ 时，其基态问题的计算复杂性缺乏统一刻画，尤其在不同配位数（coordination）格点上难度边界不清，且与自旋-1 Blume–Capel 反铁磁体（antiferromagnet）的对应关系需要澄清。

Result: Δ≤3（除 K₄）时 E₀(D₃)=min(D₃,1)·fr，可在平面格点上多项式时间计算，等价于平面次三次（subcubic）图上独立奇圈横截（independent odd cycle transversal）问题多项式可解；平面 Δ=4 时，对每个固定的 0≤D₃<2 判定基态能量为 NP 完全（NP-complete），D₃≥2 为多项式；阈值 D₃=⌊Δ/2⌋ 对每个 Δ≥3 是紧的，并重现三角格点 D₃*=3。

On lattices with coordination at most three the third state heals frustrated bonds one for one: every graph of maximum degree three other than $K_4$ has an independent set of sites that meets every odd cycle and whose size equals the number $\operatorname{fr}$ of frustrated bonds of the Ising ground state. This identity also follows from known results on the chromatic vertex stability of graphs; we give a constructive proof. Hence $E_0 (D_3)=\min(D_3,1) \operatorname{fr}$ for all $D_3$, computable in polynomial time on planar lattices. In graph-theoretic terms, independent odd cycle transversal is solvable in polynomial time on planar subcubic graphs. On planar lattices with maximum coordination four, in contrast, deciding the ground-state energy is NP-complete for every fixed $0\le D_3<2$ and polynomial for $D_3\ge2$. The boundary is set by a local principle: a site of coordination $d$ in the third state relieves at most $\lfloor d/2\rfloor$ frustrated bonds, so the third state is useless once $D_3\ge\lfloorΔ/2\rfloor$; this threshold is sharp for every $Δ\ge3$ and reproduces the triangular-lattice point $D_3^*=3$ of the equivalent spin-1 Blume--Capel antiferromagnet. From a single Ising ground state the proof builds an exact ground state in polynomial time, which for quasicrystalline, foam-like and defective lattices with thousands of sites takes seconds to minutes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌多体系统与随机能量模型（random energy model）中，缩放后SFF的对数在晚期呈现自平均性和反射-Gumbel分布这两项稳健性质；作者希望检验这些性质在非混沌、非相互作用系统中的适用边界。

Conclusion: 反射-Gumbel统计在非相互作用系统中普遍失效，但可积性本身并不排除该统计；关键区别在于相互作用的有无，这为区分不同类型的多体动力学提供了谱统计判据。

Moreover, it is no longer self-averaging, as its mean and standard deviation are of the same order. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动弹性流形（driven elastic manifold）的钉扎转变由非平衡临界不动点（nonequilibrium critical fixed point）支配，但真实界面常含内部自由度；核心问题是这些内部模式究竟改变钉扎普适性（universality），还是仅影响非普适性质。

Abstract: Driven elastic manifolds exhibit a depinning transition whose large-scale behavior is governed by a nonequilibrium critical fixed point. The threshold, however, shifts. The shift is governed by the elastic crossover length of the internal mode rather than by multistability, and can have either sign. An infinite-range mean-field treatment misses this mechanism. With the physical two-partial elastic kernel and disorder calibrated against measured strengths, width fluctuations raise the athermal critical resolved shear stress by 10-35% over that of a rigid dissociated dislocation. </details>

*Mesfin Taye*

TL;DR: 该论文针对同一热马尔可夫生成元（thermal Markov generator）下两个初态的相对熵（relative entropy）非单调行为，给出有限时间交叉（finite-time crossing）的充分条件与显式界限，并用数值与区间算术（interval arithmetic）验证其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识中热化通常被视为相对熵单调衰减，但相对熵排序在有限时间内可能反转；论文旨在给出可判定、可量化的交叉判据，而非依赖精确慢模（slow mode）抵消。

Abstract: Two preparations evolving under the same thermal Markov generator can reverse their ordering in relative entropy even when the hotter state retains a nonzero slow-mode component. In a selected eight-state example, retaining two nonstationary modes certifies a crossing before 1.55163, improving a certified slow-sector endpoint of 2.53951; the located crossing is near 1.26943. A reflecting diffusion in an asymmetric potential supplies a separate physical example, with systematic mesh refinement. Independent interval arithmetic certifies selected spectral bounds, finite-grid first crossings, and an entire preparation-temperature rectangle in a three-state benchmark. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠阻尼随机过程的最优输运协议求解在控制理论（control theory）中困难但重要；问题高度非线性，包含刻画 Landauer 比特擦除（Landauer bit-erasure）的边界条件，一般不存在解析解。

Conclusion: 机器学习直接方法可有效处理带非线性边界条件的最优输运问题，为包括 Landauer 比特擦除在内的情形提供数值求解途径，并可由过阻尼微扰展开提供独立校验。

Abstract: Finding optimal transport protocols for underdamped stochastic processes is a difficult yet important problem in control theory. In this paper we develop a direct method, based on machine learning, to find the optimal solution of the problem in the presence of non-linear boundary conditions, including those modelling the Landauer bit-erasure problem. The dynamics is steered from a single-peak to a double-peak distribution in position space, by only acting on a controllable external potential. The problem is highly non-linear, and analytical solutions are not available in general. We benchmark our numerical results with a perturbative expansion, which is expected to hold in a suitable overdamped limit.

</details>

For Weeks--Chandler--Andersen potential, the nonintegrable core makes both the Fourier transform of the potential and the self-interaction correction term in the Dean--Kawasaki field theory ill-defined. </details>

Abstract: We derive a general expression for the thermal conductivity of simple fluids within a thermodynamic transport theory (TTT) in which the Onsager coefficients of an interacting system are related to those of an ideal reference system through the ratio of the determinants of the Hessians of the relevant thermodynamic potential. The transported quantity is identified with an effective energy density proportional to the rms energy fluctuations of a small fluid cell. The result expresses the thermal conductivity as the product of its dilute-gas value and a function of equilibrium thermodynamic properties, namely the specific heats at constant density and at constant $ρT$ and the temperature derivative of the latter, with no adjustable parameters. For hard spheres, it reduces to a previously verified expression in terms of the thermodynamic factor. The theory is tested against molecular dynamics results for the Lennard-Jones (LJ) and the truncated and shifted Lennard-Jones (LJTS) fluids in the supercritical region, for densities up to $ρ^*=0.8$, using equations of state based on perturbation theory. For the LJTS fluid, the mean absolute percentage error is below 8\% for all the temperatures considered. For the LJ fluid, the predictions are compared with those of the revised Enskog theory for Mie fluids, using the same equation of state: TTT gives smaller errors at almost all temperatures, with a significant improvement at low temperatures, where the errors are reduced approximately by a factor between two and three.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为无序会减慢弛豫（如玻璃与无序磁体），但这一直觉是否普遍成立缺乏一般性判据。作者希望厘清无序对渐近弛豫速率（asymptotic relaxation rate）的影响究竟由哪些机制与几何条件决定。

Method: 研究对象为简谐背景势加淬火随机高斯力场（含保守与非保守分量）中的布朗粒子（Brownian particle）。方法上结合弱无序微扰理论（weak-disorder perturbation theory）、随机矩阵理论（random-matrix theory）与 Fokker-Planck 方程的数值求解。

Conclusion: 无序对渐近弛豫速度的影响是依赖于几何与随机力类型的多面图景，而非单一方向的减慢效应。所揭示的有效刚化与能级吸引（含非厄米算子（non-Hermitian operator）的能级吸引）机制可能适用于当前设定之外的更广泛复杂系统。

Abstract: Disorder is commonly expected to slow down relaxation, as in glasses and disordered magnets. Here we show that this need not be the case in general, by analysing the asymptotic relaxation rate of a Brownian particle in a harmonic background potential and a quenched-random Gaussian force field with conservative and non-conservative components. Combining weak-disorder perturbation theory, random-matrix theory, and numerical solutions of the Fokker-Planck equation, we show that the outcome depends on the geometry of the background and on the properties of the random forces. We identify two distinct mechanisms that shape the relaxation speed: an effective stiffening of the background by the disorder, as previously reported in one dimension, and interactions (repulsive and attractive) between the levels of the Fokker-Planck operator. Through level attraction, a common feature of non-Hermitian operators, non-conservative driving forces pull the slowest mode towards faster ones, which facilitates faster relaxation. In anisotropic backgrounds, both effects occur at second order in the disorder strength, enabling either slower or faster relaxation. In isotropic backgrounds, level interactions dominate and the disorder affects the degenerate slowest mode already at first order. The shift is then given by the left-most eigenvalue of a Gaussian random matrix and relaxation is generically slowed down. In backgrounds whose anisotropy is comparable to the disorder strength, level attraction is most effective by merging the slowest mode with its neighbour into an oscillating complex-conjugate pair. In this case, relaxation becomes faster on average once the random forces are sufficiently non-conservative. Our results draw a nuanced picture of how disorder affects the asymptotic relaxation speed in complex systems and uncover general mechanisms that are likely to apply beyond the current setting.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重粒子衰变产生的多喷注末态存在组合问题（combinatorial problem），必须（至少隐含地）将喷注分配给其母粒子，因此需要一种能有效处理喷注指派假设的分类方法。

Conclusion: 由于仅编码的衰变链与具体末态相关，该构造可推广到其他拓扑（topology），并在 CMS 多项搜寻中使用；实现已公开。其以极小参数和训练开销取得与大型网络相当的性能，显示出结构先验编码的有效性。

Abstract: Final states with several jets from the decays of heavy particles pose a combinatorial problem, since the jets must be assigned, at least implicitly, to their parent particles. We describe the Combinatorial Residual Network (CoReNet), a classifier that encodes the assumed decay chain of the final state in its architecture. CoReNet builds its event representation level by level along the decay hierarchy, evaluates every jet assignment hypothesis with weights shared across objects, and combines the hypotheses with learned weights using residual networks. The exchange symmetries of the decay are built into the construction, the size of the network does not depend on the number of hypotheses, and the network is trained with the classification loss alone, without generator-level jet assignments. The architecture was developed for the $b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ final state and has been used in several CMS searches. On fast-simulation samples of Higgs boson pair production in the $b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ final state against QCD multijet production, CoReNet with 1508 trainable parameters performs well above kinematic baselines and trains in minutes on a single GPU. Its hypothesis weights select the truth-matched jet pairing in most of the signal events in which it is defined, although no assignment target is used in training. Since only the encoded decay chain is specific to the final state, the construction carries over to other topologies, which we test with $HH \to b\bar{b}W^+W^-$ against semileptonic $t\bar{t}$. There, encoding the decay chain of the background as well as that of the signal further improves the classification, at a cost of about $10^3$ parameters. In both final states its performance is comparable to that of a state-of-the-art transformer-based network more than two orders of magnitude larger. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/cms-cmu/CoReNet.

Abstract: A search for heavy neutral leptons (HNLs) in the decay of W bosons is performed in final states with three muons, with one originating promptly from the primary interaction vertex and the other two stemming from a common secondary vertex. Abstract: We present the Reconstruct Everything Model (REM), an end-to-end multitask model for sparse, multimodal sensor data, demonstrated in high-energy physics. Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of quantum systems whose evolution is governed by linear generators of fixed unitarily invariant norm. For this broad class of evolutions, we derive a universal time-optimal generator of the dynamics converting one given state into another. It provides a time-optimal non-Hermitian Hamiltonian for pure state conversion. We then use this general result to derive quantum speed limits for mixed-state evolutions governed by Lindbladians and non-Hermitian Hamiltonians. Abstract: We show that, under certain conditions, generalised contextuality, in the sense introduced by Spekkens, cannot be created from the vacuum of a quantum field. Second, in the limit of zero energy gap in the spectrum of the free detector Hamiltonian. Our results are at the opposite side of what has been recently obtained in the literature regarding the harvesting of Kochen-Specker contextuality. Our results demonstrate that the existing notions of contextuality can be independent of one another and that contextuality harvesting depends on which precise notion is used.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态极限下的久保磁化率（Kubo susceptibility）描述孤立量子系统的绝热响应，其能级占据数不变，一般不同于占据数重新热化的等温响应。两者缺乏统一描述，故有必要建立可同时容纳二者的动力学框架。

</details>

</details>


### [30] [Distilling Knowledge from Search: Decoder Model Factory for Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41398)

TL;DR: GraphMP 是一种基于学习的消息传递（message passing）解码器，面向量子低密度奇偶校验码（quantum low-density parity-check codes），可在不同码族、码长、物理错误率与噪声模型间免微调迁移，并通过“解码器模型工厂”与模型引导的束搜索（beam search）持续迭代提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 LDPC 码的解码需要在解码图（decoding graph）规模增长时保持可扩展性，并降低逻辑错误率；同时现有解码器难以在不同码族、码长与噪声条件下泛化，缺乏可随硬件演化持续适配的解码方案。

Result: 所提解码器可在不微调的情况下跨码族、码尺寸、物理错误率与噪声模型迁移；模型引导搜索与束搜索共同降低逻辑错误率；多代收集与蒸馏可跨代提升解码器，并使其策略随噪声变化而自适应调整。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种量子—经典耦合架构（quantum–classical architecture），由快速解码保护量子信息、较慢学习利用运行经验更新模型两个交互循环构成；长期目标是构建可扩展的解码器模型工厂，持续产出高性能、低延迟（low-latency）解码器并适应演化的量子硬件。

Abstract: We introduce GraphMP, a learning-based message-passing decoder for quantum low-density parity-check codes that transfers without fine-tuning across code families, code sizes, physical error rates, and noise models. Repeated collection and distillation improve the decoder across generations and adapt its strategy as the noise changes. These results motivate a coupled quantum--classical architecture with two interacting cycles: rapid decoding protects quantum information, while slower learning renews the models from operating experience. The long-term vision is a scalable decoder model factory that produces high-performance, low-latency decoders and continually adapts them to evolving quantum hardware.

</details>


### [31] [Helper-assisted quantum sensing with low dressed-state leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41404)
*Shirwan Abdullah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究仅读出辅助量子比特（helper qubit）的双量子比特传感协议（two-qubit sensing protocol），推导激励受限下的信息上限，并通过驱动开放系统模型仿真重复操作，评估其在有限测量记录下的估计性能与偏置抑制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免直接读出传感器（sensor）可减少测量反作用，但其信息代价与实用条件尚不明确，故需量化辅助读出方案的信息上限与实际可行性。

Method: 采用标定的加载与卸载（loading and unloading）将横向信号编码到辅助比特相位并抑制泄漏（leakage）；推导单传感器在激励低于给定预算时的信息界（information bound），并在含不确定耦合与有限时长重置（reset）的驱动开放系统模型（driven open-system model）中仿真重复操作。

Result: 在测试参数下，有限测量记录即可支持估计且无需频繁重置；零耦合时的辅助翻转（helper flip）结合耦合符号反转可抑制整条记录共有的静态辅助偏移（offset），保留信号，代价约为等时长普通序列无偏移信息量的27%；缓变噪声下按正确噪声律对每周期似然（likelihood）取平均可消除大部分附加偏置，错误的噪声律则会恶化估计。

Conclusion: 理想控制并重复非破坏性验证（nondemolition verification）的直接读出传感器具有更高的信息率；规避直接读出是否具有实际收益，取决于具体器件的扰动模型（disturbance model）。

Abstract: We study a two-qubit sensing protocol in which only a helper qubit is read out. Calibrated loading and unloading encode a transverse signal in the helper's phase, keep leakage out of a selected dressed-state band small, and return the sensor close to its idle ground state. We derive an information bound for a single sensor whose excitation stays below a prescribed budget, and simulate repeated operation with an uncertain coupling and finite-duration resets in a driven open-system model. At the tested settings, finite measurement records support estimation with infrequent resets. A helper flip at zero coupling, combined with a reversal of the coupling sign, suppresses a static helper offset shared across a record while retaining the signal, at a cost of about 27% of the no-offset information of a plain sequence of equal duration. Under slowly varying noise, averaging each cycle's likelihood over the correct noise law removes most of the added bias, and a wrong noise law can worsen the estimate. A selected directly read sensor with ideal controls and repeated nondemolition verification has the higher information rate. Any practical benefit of avoiding direct readout therefore requires a device-specific disturbance model.

</details>


### [32] [eCAS: An Orbital Embedding Method for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41517)
*Anna Brünisholz,Markus Reiher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出轨道嵌入方法 eCAS，通过构造多个可独立求解的局域化活性空间（active space）的多-CAS 问题，规避现有量子嵌入方法对近未来量子计算机的苛刻要求，并给出相对简单的梯度表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来容错量子计算机可计算原子体系的电子结构（electronic structure），但受体系尺寸限制；二次量子化电子哈密顿量（second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian）中电子-电子相互作用项数目随规模不利增长，需借助嵌入理论（embedding theories）。现有量子嵌入方法要求测量约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrices）或反复求值，对近未来量子计算机限制过强。

Method: eCAS 将体系划分为杂质（impurity）与（更大的）环境（environment），构造多-CAS 问题，其活性空间（active space）局域化且可用任意量子算法彼此独立求解。它利用 Schmidt 分解（Schmidt decomposition）构造初始杂质轨道与浴轨道（bath orbitals），并基于互相关判据（mutual correlation criterion）以局域环境轨道加以扩展。

Result: eCAS 避开了测量约化密度矩阵与重复求值等限制，并可导出相对简单的梯度（gradient）表达式；互相关判据使 eCAS 具备系统性可改进性（systematically improvable），且无需任何迭代确定的量。

Conclusion: eCAS 是一种轨道嵌入（orbital embedding）方法，通过局域化活性空间与互相关判据实现系统可改进性，降低对近未来量子硬件的需求，并为梯度计算提供便利。

Abstract: Future fault-tolerant quantum computers will allow us to calculate electronic structures of atomistic systems. However, these systems will be intrinsically limited in size, requiring embedding theories for many systems because of the unfavorable scaling of the number of terms describing the electron-electron interaction in the second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian. Quantum embedding theories address this challenge by partitioning a quantum system into a region of interest, the impurity, and its (larger) complement, the environment. However, currently available quantum embedding methods place restrictive demands on near-future quantum computers, such as the need to measure reduced density matrices or the need for repeated evaluations. Here, we introduce the new orbital embedding method eCAS that steers clear of these pitfalls by constructing a multi-CAS problem, the active spaces of which are localized and can be solved independently of each other with any quantum algorithm. This also allows for the development of comparatively simple gradient expressions. eCAS leverages the Schmidt decomposition to construct initial impurity and bath orbitals and augments them with localized environment orbitals, based on a mutual correlation criterion. The mutual correlation criterion makes eCAS systematically improvable without the need for any iteratively determined quantities.

</details>


### [33] [Control-Noise Trade-offs in Industrially Integrated ${}^{28}\mathrm{Si}/\mathrm{SiGe}$ Spin-1/2 Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41890)
*Leon C. Camenzind,Ik Kyeong Jin,Yi-Hsien Wu,Akito Noiri,Kenta Takeda,Takashi Nakajima,Takashi Kobayashi,Giordano Scappucci,Seigo Tarucha*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将硅自旋量子比特（silicon spin qubits）扩展至容错处理器（fault-tolerant processors）需要半导体代工（foundry）集成，但工业制造与高密度器件集成如何影响量子比特控制与噪声尚不明确，本文旨在厘清这一问题。

In the present Intel devices, the micromagnets are integrated inline with the back-end-of-line (BEOL) contact routing, with their separation from the quantum well set through the vertical stack design. Relative to the reference, their larger separation contributes to weaker longitudinal and transverse magnetic-field gradients. The weaker longitudinal gradient suppresses the conversion of charge noise into qubit-frequency fluctuations, whereas the weaker transverse gradient reduces electrically driven spin-resonance efficiency and makes drive-dependent errors relevant at lower Rabi frequencies. Additional BEOL routing and metal levels required for two-dimensional arrays make such placement decisions increasingly consequential when scaling. Abstract: We show that topological edge states can be realized in a simple superconducting circuit composed of an inductively shunted flux qubit. This mapping is confirmed by numerical simulations of the full superconducting circuit. We argue that chiral symmetry still holds for the edge states of the extended model around a chiral point in parameter space. Close to this point, we numerically find that edge states in our superconducting circuit are topologically protected against perturbations that obey chiral symmetry, as is the case for charge noise. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

For a family of completely integrable kicked-top systems, a homotopy classification of adiabatic cycles identifies which adiabatic cycles induce action variable holonomy and adiabatic torus pumping. A physical example is also provided.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We analyze scaling limits in algebraic quantum field theory using continuous bundles of C*-algebras. Different conditions of continuity on such bundles encode different strengths of approximation of the theory by its scaling limit. As a proof of concept, we consider the small-distance scaling limit of the theory of a massive free scalar field, which corresponds to a massless free scalar field. We provide a number of examples of local algebras of observables for which the corresponding bundle of C*-algebras representing the scaling limit is merely lower semicontinuous. Further, we construct an algebra of nonlocal observables for which the corresponding bundle of C*-algebras representing the scaling limit is uniformly continuous.

</details>

Abstract: Weyl points are quasimomentum-energy pairs in the band structure of three-dimensional periodic media at which two dispersion surfaces touch conically. They first arose in a model introduced by H. Weyl in 1929, but were first observed only recently in condensed matter physics and photonics experiments. Weyl points have been explored theoretically via numerical computation and via tight binding (discrete) models. In this article we present the first analytical treatment of Weyl points in a continuum PDE-type model. Starting with a Schrödinger operator whose electric potential is periodic with respect to the body-centered cubic (BCC) lattice, we introduce a small BCC-periodic magnetic vector potential that is equivariant with respect to a specific abelian group of symmetries. For magnetic Schrödinger operators of this type, we prove (i) that a pair of Weyl points bifurcates from a threefold degeneracy in the band structure of the underlying non-magnetic operator, and (ii) that these Weyl points are stable against small self-adjoint BCC-periodic perturbations of both the electric and magnetic vector potentials.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Connes spectral distance as a quantum distance measure and its applications in discriminating quantum states and calculating quantum entanglement. The corresponding optimal elements can always be chosen to commute with the difference matrix $Δ$, and then the optimal element and $Δ$ can be simultaneously diagonalized. Abstract: Chiral symmetry breaking is a defining feature of life, visible at all scales from molecules to organisms. Yet how microscopic chirality propagates to shape the macroscopic properties of living matter remains poorly understood. Here, using a new experimental system of spinning microalgae, we show that a living chiral fluid behaves fundamentally differently from an externally driven one. As their density increases, cells self-assemble hydrodynamically into small rotating clusters which merge into a percolating chiral fluid. Strikingly, microscopic and macroscopic vorticity have opposite signs: individual cells all spin clockwise, yet clusters orbit counterclockwise. Combining experiments, theory and simulations, we show that both chirality inversion and percolation are direct consequences of the cells' internally driven nature. We then show how percolation reshapes transport, with even and odd diffusivities responding differently to the emerging network topology.

Result: 观测到极向速度场与垂直向列颗粒排列共存,体系中存在具有径向颗粒轴的整数+1速度涡旋(vortices)与-1鞍点缺陷(saddle defects);带相反"电荷"的缺陷沿畴壁(domain walls)迁移,并随极畴(polar domains)合并而湮灭。

Conclusion: 推进方向与颗粒形状的相对取向被确定为调控缺陷织构与集体有序的控制参数,表明横向推进可改变致密椭球昆克辊的拓扑序(topological ordering)。

Abstract: Transverse propulsion changes topological ordering in dense ellipsoidal Quincke rollers. Under an alternating-current electric field, a polar velocity field coexists with perpendicular nematic particle alignment. The system supports integer +1 velocity vortices with radial particle axes and -1 saddle defects. Oppositely charged defects migrate along domain walls and annihilate as polar domains merge. These results identify propulsion direction relative to particle shape as a control parameter for defect textures and collective ordering.

</details>


### [51] [Microswimmers lose by swimming selfishly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41965)
*Albert Alonso*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究用可微分斯托克斯求解器（differentiable Stokes solver）优化软体微游动器（microswimmer）单独与成对的膜形状变化，发现流体动力学耦合使结伴游动成为社会困境（social dilemma），其结局取决于游动器彼此重视对方收益的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低雷诺数（low Reynolds number）下微游动器即使相隔较远也通过自身产生的流场相互耦合，因此每个游动器的最佳推进方式取决于邻居，使“结伴游动”成为一个博弈问题。研究旨在揭示这种流体动力学耦合如何塑造个体与集体的最优策略。

Method: 采用可微分斯托克斯求解器（differentiable Stokes solver），从随机的初始膜运动出发，分别优化单个软体游动器及成对游动器的形状变化，并比较单独最优划动（stroke）与相互响应后的策略差异。

Result: 单个游动器收敛为行波膜运动（traveling membrane wave）。成对时，一方的划动对邻居的影响远大于邻居对其自身最优划动的影响：纵向排列时该影响随间距 ℓ 按 1/ℓ 衰减，而响应激励按 1/ℓ² 衰减。自私游动器（selfish swimmers）只响应弱激励，错失的合作增益按 1/ℓ² 衰减，其自私适应使双方处境劣于各自保持独游划动的情形。

Conclusion: 互助型游动器（related swimmers）可恢复该合作增益，而竞争者（competitors）则付出代价相互阻碍，二者均遵循文中解析推导的规律。仅流体动力学本身即使结伴游动构成社会困境（social dilemma），其结果取决于游动器对彼此收益的重视程度。

Abstract: At low Reynolds number, microswimmers are coupled by the flows they create, even when they are apart. The best way for each swimmer to push itself forward then depends on its neighbors, turning swimming together into a game. To study this, we optimize the shape changes of soft swimmers, alone and in pairs, with a differentiable Stokes solver. From random initial membrane motions, a lone swimmer converges to a traveling membrane wave. In a pair, the stroke of a swimmer affects its neighbor far more than the neighbor affects its own best stroke. For swimmers in line, this influence decays as $1/\ell$ with their separation $\ell$, whereas the incentive to respond decays as $1/\ell^2$. Selfish swimmers, which maximize their own payoff, respond only to the weak incentive. The cooperative gain they miss decays only as $1/\ell^2$, and their selfish adaptations leave the pair worse off than if both had kept their solitary strokes. Related swimmers, which also value the payoff of their neighbor, recover this gain, whereas competitors pay to hinder each other, both following a law that we derive analytically. Hydrodynamics alone thus makes swimming in company a social dilemma, whose outcome depends on how much the swimmers value each other.

</details>


### [52] [Drying kinematics selects open honeycomb fold networks on solvent-extracted viscoelastic thin films and gels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42074)
*Fan Zhao,Surjyasish Mitra,Sunandan Tandon,Zoya Leonenko,Boxin Zhao,Sushanta K. Mitra*

TL;DR: 溶剂萃取与干燥使基底支撑的黏弹性薄膜和凝胶形成三臂表面褶皱的准周期网络。实验与理论结合揭示褶皱波长和臂长随干燥膜厚以5/8幂律缩放、对弹性模量依赖极弱，并受弹性毛细阻滞（elastocapillary arrest）限制振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基底支撑黏弹性薄膜与凝胶在溶剂萃取和干燥过程中形成的三臂表面褶皱（three-armed surface folds）准周期网络，旨在阐明其形成机制、特征长度尺度与几何组织的物理来源。

Method: 采用实验与理论相结合的方法，考察固定（pinned）薄膜中溶胀诱导压缩驱动表层硬化、扩散增厚的表皮在排液底层之上发生局域化褶皱的过程。

Result: 图案波长与褶皱臂长均随干燥膜厚（dried-film thickness）呈指数为5/8的幂律缩放，且对弹性模量（elastic modulus）依赖极弱；褶皱在三角晶格（triangular lattice）上形成120度结点，构成开放蜂窝网络（open honeycomb network），其节距满足 p = √3(aL + g)，g为结点间隙。

Conclusion: 褶皱仅容纳不足0.1%的储存压缩应变，表明限制其振幅的是弹性毛细阻滞（elastocapillary arrest）而非应变松弛；研究确立了控制干燥软固体（drying soft solids）图案选择的几何、动力学与界面约束。

Abstract: Solvent extraction and drying of substrate-supported viscoelastic films and gels produce quasiperiodic networks of three-armed surface folds. In this Letter, we examine the physical mechanisms governing their formation, length scales, and geometric organization. We observed that swelling-induced compression in the pinned film drives localized folding of a stiffening, diffusively thickening surface skin over a drained underlayer. Combining experiments and theory, we reveal that both the pattern wavelength and fold-arm length scale with the dried-film thickness following a power-law with an exponent 5/8, with remarkably weak dependence on elastic modulus. The folds form 120-degree junctions on a triangular lattice, defining an open honeycomb network whose pitch satisfies p = sqrt(3)(aL + g), where g is the junction gap. Notably, the folds accommodate less than 0.1% of the stored compressive strain, implicating elastocapillary arrest, rather than strain relaxation, in limiting their amplitude. These findings establish distinct geometric, kinetic, and interfacial constraints governing pattern selection in drying soft solids.

</details>

### [53] [Nuclear-geometry-tagging events in high-energy Ne--Ne collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40724)
*Joachim Carlo Kristian Bjerg Hansen,Emil Gorm Dahlbæk Nielsen*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文通过蒙卡 Glauber 模拟，考察能否用偏心率 ε2 与横向致密度 d⊥ 识别 20Ne 的 body–body 与 tip–tip 构型；Fisher 线性判别在保留 30% 目标类时，body–body 纯度约 0.16–0.20，tip–tip 约 0.54–0.57。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子-核子相互作用产生形变与核团簇等集体性质，超相对论核-核碰撞可借初态几何反映入射核结构；低能理论预言 20Ne 具有 α+16O 保龄球瓶状强形变，且该核已在 LHC 碰撞，因此需研究最大化核结构敏感度的观测量与方法。

Method: 采用几何敏感观测量——偏心率（eccentricity）ε2 与横向致密度（transverse compactness）d⊥，在蒙特卡洛 Glauber（Monte-Carlo Glauber）模拟中检验 20Ne 的 body–body 与 tip–tip 构型可分辨性；构型来自核格点有效场论（NLEFT）与从头算投影生成坐标法（PGCM），并用 Fisher 线性判别（Fisher linear discriminant）基于 ε2–d⊥ 相关性进行筛选。

Result: 在验证样本中取阈值以保留目标类 30% 时，双变量 Fisher 选择在 NLEFT（PGCM）下对 body–body 事件纯度为 0.201（0.156），对 tip–tip 事件纯度为 0.573（0.540）。

Conclusion: 基于 ε2–d⊥ 相关性的 Fisher 选择对 tip–tip 构型具有中等纯度，对 body–body 构型纯度较低；两类构型的可识别性存在明显不对称，提示仅凭这两个几何观测量难以高效筛选 body–body 事件。

Abstract: Nucleon-nucleon interactions within atomic nuclei give rise to collective properties such as deformation and nuclear clustering. Ultrarelativistic nucleon-nucleon collisions offer a novel way to probe the many-body correlations of the incoming nuclei, as the nuclear structure is reflected in the initial geometry of the produced system. State-of-the-art low-energy nuclear theory calculations predict highly deformed $^{20}$Ne nuclei with a characteristic $α$+$^{16}$O bowling-pin-like shape. Since such nuclei have recently been collided at the LHC, investigating methods to maximise the sensitivity to the nuclear structure is pertinent. In this paper, we investigate whether body--body and tip--tip configurations of $^{20}$Ne nuclei can be identified in Monte--Carlo Glauber simulations using geometry-sensitive observables: the eccentricity, $\varepsilon_2$, and the transverse compactness, $d_\perp$. The nuclear configurations are obtained from nuclear lattice effective field theory calculations within the framework of Nuclear Lattice Effective Field Theory (NLEFT) and $\textit{ab initio}$ Projected Generator Coordinate Method (PGCM). We utilise a Fisher linear discriminant to select body--body and tip--tip configurations based on the $\varepsilon_2$-$d_\perp$ correlation. With classifier thresholds chosen to retain $30\%$ of the target class in a validation sample, the two-variable Fisher selection obtains purities in NLEFT (PGCM) of $0.201$ ($0.156$) and $0.573$ ($0.540$) for body--body and tip--tip events, respectively.

</details>

*A. B. Larionov*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文在理论上研究 15 GeV/c 质子束诱导的氘核无π介子破裂（pionless breakup），运动学取氘核静止系中两个出射质子快、中子慢的情形，并预期色透明（color transparency）对氘核矢量与张量分析本领有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察色透明（color transparency）这一量子色动力学（QCD）效应能否在氘核破裂反应的极化观测量中显现。

Method: 采用理论计算，针对 15 GeV/c 入射质子引发的氘核无π介子破裂（pionless breakup）过程，选取两质子快、中子慢的特定运动学区域进行分析。

Result: 预期色透明（color transparency）对氘核的矢量分析本领（vector analyzing power）与张量分析本领（tensor analyzing power）产生显著影响。

Conclusion: 氘核破裂反应的极化分析本领可作为探测色透明（color transparency）效应的敏感观测量。

Abstract: The pionless breakup of a deuteron induced by 15 GeV/$c$ beam proton is considered theoretically in the kinematics
  where both outgoing protons are fast and the neutron is slow in the rest frame of the deuteron.
  Significant influence of color transparency on the deuteron vector and tensor analyzing powers is expected.

</details>

### [55] [SKFitter: Symmetry resolved Slater Koster tight-binding models from first principles for s, p, d and f electrons, spin orbit coupling and magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40729)
*Sundeep Kapila,Bhaskaran Muralidharan,Satadeep Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SKFitter 是一个开源 Python 框架，可从 DFT 计算构建并优化对称性分辨的 Slater-Koster 紧束缚模型，支持 s 到 f 轨道、SOC 与磁交换，在八类材料上实现近费米面 9–41 meV 的能带误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理构建精确且物理可解释的紧束缚（tight binding, TB）哈密顿量仍然困难，尤其在存在复杂轨道杂化、自旋轨道耦合（spin orbit coupling, SOC）与磁序的材料中。

Method: 该框架直接基于 Quantum ESPRESSO 或 VASP 的 DFT 结果，进行对称性分辨（symmetry resolved）的 Slater-Koster 参数化，允许对称允许轨道组之间、不同近邻壳层上独立的两中心跃迁积分，并支持正交与非正交基、在位 SOC 与位点分辨磁交换作用。

Result: 在八种代表性材料上，对称性分辨模型的费米能级 1 eV 内能带误差为 9 至 41 meV；成功重现 Pt 的 SHC 特征、Fe 的 AHC 对 SOC 的依赖以及 MnTe 的动量依赖交变磁自旋劈裂；对 EuMn2Bi2，投影引导的轨道细化将近费米面误差由 120 降至 32 meV。

Conclusion: SKFitter 提供了灵活、可复现的框架，可用于构建对称一致、物理可解释的复杂材料紧束缚模型，并可进一步计算反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity, AHC）、自旋霍尔电导（spin Hall conductivity, SHC）与拓扑不变量。

Abstract: Constructing accurate and physically interpretable tight binding (TB) Hamiltonians from first principles calculations remains challenging, particularly for materials exhibiting complex orbital hybridization, spin orbit coupling (SOC), and magnetic order. SKFitter is an open-source Python framework for constructing and optimizing symmetry resolved Slater Koster TB models directly from density functional theory (DFT) calculations performed using Quantum ESPRESSO or VASP. The framework supports s to f orbitals, incorporates on site SOC and site resolved magnetic exchange interactions, and accommodates both orthogonal and nonorthogonal basis. A central feature is the symmetry resolved parameterization of orbital manifolds, which allows independent two centre hopping integrals between symmetry allowed orbital groups and across distinct neighbour shells. SKFitter also identifies missing orbital contributions and guide systematic basis set refinement and optimizes parameters through weights. The resulting Hamiltonian can be used to evaluate anomalous (AHC) and spin Hall conductivities (SHC), and topological invariants. We demonstrate the framework using eight representative materials spanning semimetals, semiconductors, transition metals, f-electron compounds, and magnetic systems. Symmetry resolved models achieve band energy errors of 9 to 41 meV within 1 eV of the Fermi level. The fitted Hamiltonians reproduce characteristic features of the SHC response of Pt, the SOC dependence of the AHC of Fe, and the momentum dependent altermagnetic spin splitting of MnTe without any altermagnet specific interaction. For EuMn2Bi2, projection guided orbital refinement reduces the near Fermi level fitting error from 120 to 32 meV. Together, these results establish SKFitter as a flexible and reproducible framework for developing symmetry consistent, physically interpretable TB models of complex materials.

</details>


### [56] [Thermal Stability and Structural Relaxation Kinetics of Commercially Pure Titanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40737)
*Y. Semerenko,N. Galtsov,D. Hurova,T. Bednarchuk,P. Zinoviev,V. Zoryansky,V. Moskalenko,R. Smolianets,A. Smirnov,Y. Pohribna,N. Aksenova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究考察深冷轧制（cryogenic rolling）商业纯钛在退火过程中的结构弛豫（structural relaxation），制备出平均晶粒约50 nm的纳米结构，晶粒长大动力学符合Lifshitz-Slyozov-Wagner模型（生长指数约4），激活能约97 kJ/mol，对应α-Ti晶界扩散典型值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深冷塑性变形所产生的高度非平衡微观结构在后续退火中的结构弛豫行为及其温度依赖性。

Method: 在100 K下深冷轧制至真应变1.20获得纳米结构，随后在425 K、525 K、725 K和945 K退火，采用电子显微术（electron microscopy）与X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）表征微观结构演化。

Result: 深冷轧制引起显著晶粒碎化，亚结构以30-50 nm的相干散射区（coherent scattering regions, CSRs）为主；退火驱动晶粒长大，525 K时略有增大，725 K时加速粗化，945 K时完全再结晶。

Conclusion: 晶粒长大动力学遵循经典Lifshitz-Slyozov-Wagner模型，生长指数约4，反映三叉晶界（triple junctions）与缺陷控制的晶界扩散作用；激活能约97 kJ/mol与α-Ti晶界扩散典型值相符，证实深冷变形产生高度非平衡微观结构，其弛豫强烈依赖温度。

Abstract: This work explores the structural relaxation of cryorolled commercially pure titanium subjected to subsequent annealing.Nanostructured specimens with an average grain size of ~50 nm were produced by plastic deformation via cryogenic rolling at 100 K to a true strain of 1.20, followed by annealing at 425 K, 525 K, 725 K, and 945 K. Microstructural evolution was examined using electron microscopy and X-ray diffraction.
  Cryogenic rolling was found to induce pronounced grain fragmentation, with coherent scattering regions (CSRs) of 30-50 nm dominating the substructure. X-ray diffraction analysis revealed that annealing drives crystallite growth: a slight increase at 525 K, accelerated coarsening at 725 K, and complete recrystallization at 945 K. The kinetics of grain growth follow the classical Lifshitz-Slyozov-Wagner model, with a growth exponent of ~4, reflecting the role of triple junctions and defect-controlled boundary diffusion. The activation energy, estimated at ~97 kJ/mol, corresponds to typical values for grain boundary diffusion in α-Ti, confirming that cryogenic deformation produces a highly non-equilibrium microstructure whose relaxation is strongly temperature dependent.

</details>


### [57] [Spectral properties of orthorhombic KSbOSiO$_4$ crystals: studies of infrared, visible and ultraviolet radiation absorption, Raman scattering, cathodoluminescence and photoluminescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40739)
*Vladimir A. Yuryev,Tatyana V. Yuryeva,Ekaterina A. Morozova,Irina F. Kadikova,Polina A. Edelbekova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对正交相 KSbOSiO₄（KSS）晶体开展多光谱研究，测定其红外吸收与漫反射、拉曼、近红外至紫外吸收以及阴极发光与光致发光光谱，分析谱带组成并确定其允许的直接与间接带隙，为历史艺术玻璃与釉的谱学分析及 KTP 族晶体研究提供基础数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KSS 微晶常出现于历史与艺术玻璃中，在波希米亚绿松石色种子珠玻璃中被认为参与内应力驱动的腐蚀过程，导致 19 世纪珠绣藏品劣化；而 KSS 作为光学材料研究已久，且与 KTiOPO₄（KTP）同构并具介电常数异常，故需系统光谱数据以支撑玻璃文物的正确谱学解析与 KTP 族晶体研究。

Method: 采用多种光谱技术对正交相 KSS 晶体进行表征：测量 50–4000 cm⁻¹ 区间的红外吸收光谱（IR absorption）与漫反射光谱（diffuse reflection）、拉曼光谱（Raman spectrum）、近红外至紫外区间的吸收光谱，以及阴极发光（cathodoluminescence）与光致发光（photoluminescence）光谱。

Result: 对光谱带的各组成峰（constituent peaks）进行了分析，并将相关性质汇总于表格；同时确定了 KSS 允许的直接带隙（direct band gap）与间接带隙（indirect band gap）。

Conclusion: 所获数据既可用于历史与艺术玻璃及釉（制作过程中有时会析出该物质）的正确光谱分析，也有助于对 KTP 族晶体开展更深入研究。

Abstract: Crystals of orthorhombic KSbOSiO$_4$ (KSS) are often found to synthesize in glass of historical and artistic articles. For instance, these crystallites in the glass of turquoise Bohemian seed beads are known to play a significant role in the process of internal-stress driven corrosion of the glass causing deterioration and destruction of bead embroidered museum exhibits, especially articles made in the early 19th century. In art materials science, KSS has been known since the mid-2010s only for its destructive effects on historical glass objects, but as an optical material it has long been studied. KSS belongs to the group of compounds isomorphic to KTiOPO$_4$ (KTP) and, like other compounds from this group, has anomalies in dielectric constant. The article presents the results of a study of orthorhombic KSS crystals using a number of spectroscopic techniques. IR absorption and diffuse reflection spectra of KSS in the spectral range from 50 to 4000~cm$^{-1}$, its Raman spectrum, its absorption spectrum in the range from near infrared to ultraviolet as well as its cathodoluminescence and photoluminescence spectra are investigated. The constituent peaks of the spectral bands are analyzed and their properties are summarized in tables; the allowed direct and indirect band gaps of KSS are determined. The presented data are necessary both for the correct spectral analysis of historical and artistic glass and enamels, during the production of which this substance was sometimes synthesized in glass, and for a more in-depth study of crystals of the KTP group.

</details>


### [58] [Kibble-Zurek dynamics beyond the thermodynamic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40755)
*Max Haas,Guy C. Moore,Isaac M. Craig,Kristin A. Persson,Jiali He,Michael E. Hjelmstad,Dennis Meier,Sinéad M. Griffin,Jan Schultheiß*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将Kibble-Zurek机制（Kibble-Zurek mechanism, KZM）从热力学极限推广到由尺寸分布有限子系统组成的集合体系，并在多晶模型系统中验证：测得的拓扑涡旋密度呈指数约为单晶一半的幂律，该指数可由晶粒尺寸分布平均定量解释，表明有限尺寸效应会重组而非抑制KZ标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KZM的普适标度预言基于均匀系统的热力学极限（thermodynamic limit），而真实材料本质上是有限且非均匀的，因此需要回答非平衡普适性如何在理想极限之外存续。论文旨在解决有限尺寸与非均匀性对KZ标度的影响这一开放问题。

Method: 建立KZM的集合有限尺寸推广（ensemble-finite-size extension），描述具有分布尺寸的独立有限子系统集合中的临界动力学。以经历对称破缺转变（symmetry-breaking transition）的多晶模型系统作为检验对象，并将结果与实验测得的晶粒尺寸分布（grain-size distribution）平均相联系。

Result: 拓扑涡旋（topological vortex）密度表现出表观幂律（apparent power law），其指数约为单晶（single crystal）情形的一半。该降低的指数由对实验测得晶粒尺寸分布的平均定量给出，且未对测得的涡旋密度进行拟合。

### [61] [Intertwined ferromagnetic and altermagnetic orders in Cr-doped MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40870)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁性与交错磁性均破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），但二者在晶体对称性下的变换性质不同，禁止其序参量间存在直接双线性耦合；两种磁序能否在热力学上保持独立却又强烈相互影响，仍是开放问题。

Result: 当交错磁性先建立时，铁磁转变变为渡越（crossover）；当铁磁性先建立时，交错磁性的出现强烈抑制面外铁磁剩磁（out-of-plane ferromagnetic remanence）。自旋轨道耦合使铁磁矩能够控制交错磁畴流形（altermagnetic domain manifold），其面外与面内分量分别偏置时间反演（Z2）与取向（Z3）扇区。

Conclusion: 两种对称性不同的磁序可各自保持独立的序化倾向，同时保持强耦合；该结果为交错磁畴态（altermagnetic domain states）的内部调控提供了途径。

### [62] [10 kV $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Sub-Bandgap Triggered Impurity Photoconductive Semiconductor Switches (i-PCSSs): Lessons for Optimal Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41049)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ga₂O₃光电导开关（photoconductive semiconductor switch, PCSS）适合高压工作，但超过带隙（above-bandgap）触发需要深紫外激发，因此探索亚带隙（sub-bandgap）近红外触发途径具有吸引力。

Conclusion: 结果证明了近红外光导开关的可行性，同时揭示了杂质态（impurity states）光导阈值方面存在的关键权衡（trade-offs）。

Abstract: $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ photoconductive semiconductor switches (PCSSs) are attractive for high-voltage operation, but above-bandgap triggering requires deep-ultraviolet excitation. Here, we investigate a 650-um-thick, 0.01% Fe-doped $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ impurity PCSS with a 5-mm lateral gap under continuous-wave (CW) 808-nm sub-bandgap excitation at biases up to 10 kV ( 20 kV cm^-1). The ON/OFF ratio increases with optical power density but exhibits a nonmonotonic bias dependence, reaching approximately 15-16 near 5-7 kV. At 10 kV, increased OFF-state conduction and non-ideal transient behavior reduce the switching contrast. ### [63] [Unifying dynamical electron-electron and electron-phonon interactions from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41091)
*Jae-Mo Lihm,Malte Rösner,Cyrus E. Dreyer,Massimiliano Stengel,Samuel Poncé*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 DFPT+A 降维（downfolding）方法，以静态 DFPT 响应为基础叠加 Wannier 函数描述的低能活性空间的动态屏蔽，低成本高精度重现 TDDFPT，并用于计算动量与频率依赖的电子-电子、电子-声子耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由载流子材料中的电子-声子相互作用会被低能电子激发动态屏蔽，但第一性原理计算几乎都采用绝热近似（adiabatic approximation）；在金属与掺杂半导体中直接用含时密度泛函微扰理论（TDDFPT）做完全动态处理代价高且数值上脆弱。

Result: 该方法以 TDDFPT 成本的一小部分准确重现 TDDFPT；在空穴掺杂单层 hBN 中，载流子等离激元与纵向光学声子杂化使低能推迟相互作用随载流子密度非单调变化，绝热近似会遗漏该效应；在金属性单层 TaS2 中非绝热效应较弱，仅源于带内电子-空穴连续谱与近邻平行费米速度的嵌套；并验证了常用的 screened-screened 近似用于声子谱函数。

Conclusion: 本工作为使用动态屏蔽相互作用的第一性原理谱函数、输运与超导研究开辟了路径。

Abstract: Electron-phonon interactions in materials with free carriers are dynamically screened by low-energy electronic excitations, yet first-principles calculations almost always treat this screening in the adiabatic approximation. A fully dynamical treatment of metals and doped semiconductors within time-dependent density functional perturbation theory (TDDFPT) is expensive and numerically delicate. Here, we introduce DFPT+A downfolding ("A" for active space), which starts from the static density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) response of a computationally convenient base system and adds the dynamical screening of a low-energy active space, described by Wannier functions. The required statically screened electron-electron interaction is computed using DFPT directly in the basis of Wannier-function pairs and truncated in a computationally efficient and systematically improvable way. The method handles entangled bands and accurately reproduces TDDFPT at a fraction of its cost. We use it to compute the total dynamical electron-electron interaction, including direct Coulomb and phonon-mediated interactions with dynamically screened electron-electron and electron-phonon couplings, all on equal footing. In hole-doped monolayer hBN, the hybridization of the carrier plasmon with the longitudinal optical phonon makes the low-energy retarded interaction nonmonotonic in the carrier density, an effect missed by the adiabatic approximation. In metallic monolayer TaS2, nonadiabatic effects are weak and occur only due to the intraband electron-hole continuum and the nesting of nearly parallel Fermi velocities. We also validate the widely used screened-screened approximation for the phonon spectral function. This work opens the way to first-principles studies of spectral functions, transport, and superconductivity with dynamically screened interactions.

</details>


### [64] [Agentic Global Optimization of Site-Disordered Crystalline Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41327)
*Martin Hoffmann Petersen,Zekun Ren,Kedar Hippalgaonkar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于代理模型（surrogate model）的智能搜索框架，用于高效定位位点无序晶体（site-disordered crystals）的全局最低能量有序构型，其命中率与效率均显著优于传统方法，并可扩展至多子晶格无序体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位点无序晶体中多种元素部分占据同一原子位点，需先确定显式有序构型才能进行原子级模拟；候选排序数随超胞尺寸组合爆炸，且每个候选都需昂贵的结构弛豫（structural relaxation），使全局最小有序构型搜索成为高成本组合问题。

Method: 构建智能体式搜索（agentic search）框架，由拟合已有评估构型的代理模型在利用（exploitation）、不确定性（uncertainty）与新颖性（novelty）之间权衡来选择下一候选；在统一框架内实现四种代理模型——ExtraTrees 集成、贝叶斯岭回归（Bayesian ridge regression）、稀疏 ARD 回归与受 ALiEGNN 启发的图集成模型，并与随机游走（random walk）、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo）及遗传算法（genetic algorithm）三种传统方法对比。

Result: 在覆盖 12 种晶体结构、14 个测试案例（以穷举全局最小值为基准真值 ground truth）的基准测试中，代理模型方法在 94% 的运行中达到全局最小，传统方法为 55%；相对最佳传统方法，评估次数中位数降低 4.3 倍。贝叶斯岭回归在全部运行中成功且评估次数最少。

Conclusion: 将该框架应用于 Na2/3FePO4（同时含 Na/空位构型无序与 Fe2+/Fe3+ 电荷无序）时，在代理表示中编码电荷态后可复现实验报道的有序构型，表明框架可推广至多子晶格无序（multi-sublattice disorder）体系。

Abstract: Site-disordered crystals, in which several elements partially occupy the same atomic site, cannot be simulated atomistically without first resolving an explicit ordering, and the number of possible orderings grows combinatorially with supercell size. Because each candidate requires an expensive structural relaxation, locating the global-minimum ordering is a costly combinatorial search. Here, we present an agentic search in which a surrogate model, fitted to previously evaluated configurations, selects each subsequent candidate by balancing exploitation, uncertainty, and novelty. Four surrogates are implemented within a shared framework: an ExtraTrees ensemble, Bayesian ridge regression, sparse ARD regression, and an ALiEGNN-inspired graph ensemble. These are benchmarked against three traditional methods: random walk, Markov chain Monte Carlo, and a genetic algorithm across 14 test cases spanning 12 crystal structures, with exhaustively enumerated global minima as ground truth. The agentic surrogates reach the global minimum in 94% of runs, compared with 55% for the traditional methods, with a median 4.3-fold reduction in evaluations relative to the best traditional method. Bayesian ridge regression succeeds in all runs with the fewest evaluations. </details>


### [65] [Strong-Field-Driven Non-Linear Vibronic Coupling in an Asymmetric Donor-Acceptor Oligomer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41462)
*Jannik Theile,Michele Guerrini,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用实时含时密度泛函理论结合 Ehrenfest 动力学，研究部分氟化受体-给体-受体寡聚物在弱至强光-物质耦合区的电子-核响应，揭示核运动如何改变场驱动电荷转移的选择性与时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核运动（nuclear motion）与分子内电荷转移（intramolecular charge transfer）的耦合在超越微扰区（beyond the perturbative regime）的光-物质相互作用（light-matter interaction）中仍属未解问题，需在弱、中、强耦合区建立系统基准。

Method: 采用实时含时密度泛函理论（real-time time-dependent density functional theory）结合 Ehrenfest 动力学（Ehrenfest dynamics），以不同强度的共振脉冲激发具有结构不对称性（源于受体单元部分氟化）的受体-给体-受体寡聚物（acceptor-donor-acceptor oligomer），并对比固定核与可动核两种情形，辅以模式分辨动能分析（mode-resolved kinetic-energy analysis）。

Result: 固定核时，场驱动电荷优先由给体流向氟化受体，强度增大使该选择性下降；核可动时早期空间响应定性不变，但出现明显的空间不对称电荷再分布。强场区核运动仍保持电荷优先聚集于氟化受体的倾向，但所需时间约为固定核情形的两倍；随场强增加，激光诱导核响应由选择性键伸缩转向集体低频模式（collective low-frequency modes）。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了供体/受体寡聚物（donor/acceptor oligomer）中振动电子动力学（vibronic dynamics）与强场耦合的相互作用，并为相关体系提供评估基准。

Abstract: Understanding how nuclear motion couples to intramolecular charge transfer beyond the perturbative regime of light--matter interaction remains an open problem. We tackle this problem by investigating the electron-nuclear response of an acceptor-donor-acceptor oligomer with structural asymmetry due to the partial fluorination of an acceptor unit. Adopting real-time time-dependent density functional theory combined with Ehrenfest dynamics, we excite the systems with a resonant pulse of varying intensity, spanning the weak-, intermediate-, and strong-coupling regimes. When nuclei are clamped, field-driven charge preferentially flows from the donor to the fluorinated acceptor, with selectivity reduced upon increasing intensity. With moving ions, the early-time spatial response remains qualitatively unchanged, despite a distinct, spatially asymmetric charge redistribution. In the strong-field regime, nuclear motion preserves the preference for field-driven charge accumulation on the fluorinated acceptor, although the process takes approximately twice the time as in the fixed-nuclei scenario. Mode-resolved kinetic-energy analysis reveals that, with increasing field strength, the laser-induced nuclear response shifts from selected bond stretchings to collective low-frequency modes. These results provide a benchmark for assessing the interplay between vibronic dynamics and strong-field coupling in donor/acceptor oligomers.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明III-V族四面体半导体金属终止与非金属终止极性表面（metal- and nonmetal-terminated polar surfaces）的性质，涉及GaN、GaP、GaAs与GaSb等多种材料的清洁极性表面。

Method: 采用从头算（ab initio）方法，计算包括纤锌矿GaN的(0001)/(000-1)、闪锌矿GaP、GaAs、GaSb的(111)/(-1-1-1)表面，获得终止原子的晶体结构与电子态（位置、能量与占据情况）。

Result: 所有非金属终止表面均无重构，其断键态（broken-bond states）位于价带顶（VBM）附近并钉扎费米能级；Ga终止表面呈现(4×4)重构，终止原子按电子计数规则（ECR）以6:10比例分为sp3与sp2杂化，断键态分裂为占据组与空组，费米能级位于二者之间、靠近导带底（CBM）。

Conclusion: III-V族半导体极性表面的电子学特征由终止原子类型主导：非金属终止面因断键态钉扎于VBM附近，而Ga终止面经(4×4)重构与电荷重分布实现费米能级位于占据与空断键态之间、接近CBM。

Abstract: Ab initio calculations were used to elucidate the properties of the polar surfaces of III-V tetrahedral semiconductors, i.e., metal- and nonmetal-terminated. The systems considered were clean wurtzite gallium nitride (i.e. (0001) and (000-1) GaN surfaces), zinc blende gallium phosphide (i.e. (111) and (-1-1-1) GaP), gallium arsenide (i.e. (111) and (-1-1-1) GaAs), and (111) and (-1-1-1) GaSb surfaces. The calculated data include the crystallographic structure and the electronic states of the termination atoms on both sides, i.e. their positions, energies, and occupations. All nonmetal-terminated surfaces exhibit no reconstruction, and their broken-bond states are located in the vicinity of the valence band maxima (VBM), pinning the Fermi level. The Ga-terminated surface exhibited (4 x 4) reconstruction related to the division of termination atoms into sp3 and sp2 hybridized in proportions of 6 to 10, based on charge redistribution according to the electron counting rule (ECR). Thus, the broken-bond state is separated into two sets, occupied and empty, with the Fermi level located between them in the vicinity of the conduction band minimum (CBM).

</details>


### [67] [Universal model of the growth of III-V tetrahedral semiconductors on polar surfaces: pyramids and glue atoms. II. Adsorption of nonmetal atoms (N, P, As, and Sb) on Ga-terminated surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41571)
*Pawel Strak,Stanisław Krukowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
