<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解四面体半导体Ga终止表面上非金属吸附物（nonmetal adsorbates）的吸附行为与稳定性，为晶体生长模拟（growth simulations）中选择合适的表面构型提供依据。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab initio）计算，研究对象包括纤锌矿（wurtzite）GaN(0001)与闪锌矿（zinc blende）GaP(111)、GaAs(111)、GaSb(111)表面。考察情形涵盖单个非金属原子吸附与全覆盖（full coverage，θ=1），分析了稳定吸附位、能量、能带图（band diagrams）、量子态能量及轨道重叠等电子性质。

Result: 低覆盖度下GaP、GaAs、GaSb的吸附原子位于H3位，高覆盖度下转为顶位（on-top）；高氮覆盖时形成弱附着的N₂吸附分子（admolecules）。蒸发稳定性分析显示吸附原子通过高能垒（ΔE>3 eV）稳定表层Ga原子，在H3位覆盖下能垒进一步升至ΔE>4 eV。

Conclusion: 非金属吸附原子可有效抑制表层Ga原子的脱附（detachment），因此生长模拟应采用被顶位非金属原子覆盖的未重构（unreconstructed）Ga终止表面。

Abstract: Ab initio calculations were used to simulate the adsorption processes on Ga-terminated surfaces of tetrahedral semiconductors: GaN, GaP, GaAs and GaSb. The considered adsorbates are nonmetal species, i.e., nitrogen, phosphorus, arsenic, and antimony on wurtzite gallium nitride, i.e., GaN(0001) surfaces, zinc blende gallium phosphide, i.e., GaP(111), gallium arsenide, GaAs(111), and gallium antimonide, GaSb (111) surfaces. The cases investigated include single adsorbed nonmetal atoms and full coverage, i.e. theta=1 of these surfaces. The calculated results include the stable sites and the energies of the single adsorbed atoms: located at H3 sites at low coverage and in on-top positions at high coverage for GaP, GaAs and GaSb. At high nitrogen coverage, the N2 admolecules are formed, weakly attached to the surface. The calculated electronic properties of the single-adatom systems include the band diagrams of the slabs in real and momentum space, the energies of the quantum states. In addition, the overlaps of the quantum states of the nonmetal adatoms and the nearest topmost surface Ga atoms are given. The full coverage data include the atomic configurations and the bond energies of the adsorbed layers. The electronic properties include the above-mentioned data for single adatoms, together with additional properties related to interactions between adsorbed atoms. The evaporation stability analysis confirms that the adatoms stabilize the topmost Ga atoms against detachment because of the high energy barrier of DE > 3 eV which is further increased to DE > 4 eV by coverage located at H3 sites. Thus, growth simulations should employ unreconstructed Ga-terminated surfaces covered by nonmetal atoms located at on-top positions.

</details>


### [68] [Mirror-symmetry engineering of altermagnetism based on monolayer hexagonal lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41589)
*Han-Bing Li,Zhi-Gang Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于镜面对称性（mirror symmetry）的设计原则，用于在单层六方晶格中实现交错磁（altermagnetism, AM），并以多孔石墨炔（holey graphyne, HGY）为代表性体系进行验证，发现 N–H 结构可形成稳定的交错磁基态，自旋劈裂达 148.2 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁具有自旋极化能带与零净磁化，但单层六方晶格中实现 AM 受到晶格对称性的强约束，因此需要普适的设计原则与候选材料体系。

Method: 提出基于镜面对称性的设计原则与实施方案，在单层六方晶格 HGY 的镜面对称轴上引入氢化非金属单元 X–H（X = B、C、N、P、S）并扩展至 N–F，筛选出由镜面反映相联系的两个反平行自旋子晶格构型。

Result: N–H 结构形成稳定的交错磁基态，表现出动量依赖的自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting），最大可达 148.2 meV；磁性源于 π–π 超交换相互作用（π–π superexchange interactions），H/F 官能化通过调控局域电荷分布稳定本征 AM。

Conclusion: 该工作为轻元素交错磁材料（light-element altermagnetic materials）的设计提供了思路与可实施方案。

Abstract: Altermagnetism (AM) is a novel collinear antiferromagnetic phase characterized by spin-polarized bands and zero net magnetization. However, realizing AM in monolayer hexagonal lattices is strongly constrained by lattice symmetry. Here, we propose a mirror-symmetry-based design principle and implementation scheme for realizing AM based on monolayer hexagonal lattices and validate it using holey graphyne (HGY) as a representative system. By examining hydrogenated nonmetal units X$-$H (X = B, C, N, P, and S) introduced along the mirror-symmetry axis and extending the analysis to N$-$F, we identify the configurations that form two antiparallel spin sublattices related by mirror reflection. The N$-$H structure forms a stable altermagnetic ground state, with a momentum-dependent spin splitting up to 148.2 meV. The magnetism originates from $π-π$ superexchange interactions, and H/F functionalization stabilizes intrinsic AM by regulating the local charge distribution. Our work offers insights into the design of light-element altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [69] [Microscopic structure at the morphotropic phase boundary in lead-barium meta-niobat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41601)
*Marco Fornari,Virginia Carnevali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理电子结构计算与从头算分子动力学，研究Pb1-xBaxNb2O6中Ba取代对阳离子占位、局域畸变与极化的影响，揭示正交-四方相变的结构起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铅钡铌酸盐（lead-barium niobate）在正交与四方相之间的准同型相界（morphotropic phase boundary）附近表现出增强的功能性质，但其微观结构机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用第一性原理电子结构计算（first-principles electronic-structure calculations）结合从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics），并显式考虑空位有序（vacancy ordering）及钨青铜（tungsten-bronze）骨架中不等价A位笼。

Result: 随Ba含量增加，五角通道中Pb离子面内协同偏心（off-centering）逐渐减弱，Nb与A位阳离子的面外位移增强，自发极化（spontaneous polarization）由[110]转向[001]；转变发生在x≈0.6附近，与实验相图定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作给出Pb1-xBaxNb2O6准同型相界的微观解释，并为钨青铜铁电体建立了清晰的结构-极化关系（structure-polarization relationship）。

Abstract: Lead-barium niobate Pb$_{1-x}$Ba$_x$Nb$_2$O$_6$ is a tetragonal tungsten-bronze ferroelectric that exhibits enhanced functional properties near a morphotropic phase boundary separating orthorhombic and tetragonal phases. Using first-principles electronic-structure calculations combined with ab initio molecular dynamics, we investigate how Ba substitution modifies cation-site occupancy, local distortions, and polarization behavior across the solid solution. By explicitly accounting for vacancy ordering and the inequivalent $A$-site cages intrinsic to the tungsten-bronze framework, we identify the structural origin of the orthorhombic-to-tetragonal transition. Increasing Ba content progressively weakens the cooperative in-plane off-centering of Pb ions in the pentagonal channels and promotes out-of-plane displacements of Nb and $A$-site cations, leading to a rotation of the spontaneous polarization from [110] to [001]. The transition occurs near $x \approx 0.6$, in quantitative agreement with experimental phase diagrams. These results provide a microscopic interpretation of the morphotropic phase boundary in Pb$_{1-x}$Ba$_x$Nb$_2$O$_6$ and establish a clear structure-polarization relationship for tungsten-bronze ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [70] [Spontaneous Circular Dichroism and Memory Effect from Surface Defects in Altermagnetic MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41797)
*Benjamin F. Mead,Isaiah Gray,Seng Huat Lee,Michael Chilcote,Zi-Jie Yan,Sijie Xu,Aparna Jayakumar,Emilia Morosan,Jörn W. F. Venderbos,Cui-Zu Chang,Zhiqiang Mao,Pengcheng Dai,Matthew Brahlek,Liang Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过光学磁圆二色性（magnetic circular dichroism）测量，发现MnTe块体单晶在250 K以下存在磁滞与记忆效应，但抛光样品与薄膜中均未出现，表明该磁滞源于表面缺陷；结合输运数据说明MnTe中的反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）很可能非本征来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性（altermagnetism）是零磁化但宏观破坏时间反演对称性的新磁性类别；六方MnTe作为g波交替磁体，其交替磁性与反常霍尔效应之间的关系仍存争议，需要厘清该效应的物理来源。

Method: 采用光学磁圆二色性（magnetic circular dichroism）测量MnTe块体单晶的磁响应，并对抛光后的块体晶体与薄膜样品进行对照测量，再与输运测得的反常霍尔效应作温度依赖性与符号比较。

Result: 块体单晶在250 K以下观察到磁滞与记忆效应，而抛光块体与薄膜中无磁滞；磁圆二色性与反常霍尔效应呈现相似的温度依赖并在符号上发生变化。

Conclusion: 块体MnTe中观测到的磁圆二色性磁滞来源于表面缺陷；磁圆二色性与反常霍尔效应的相似温度依赖及符号变化表明，输运中的反常霍尔效应很可能不是本征（intrinsic）的。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has emerged as a new class of magnetism with vanishing magnetization and macroscopic time reversal symmetry breaking. Hexagonal MnTe is a g-wave altermagnet with a 307 K Néel temperature, large spin splitting, and an anomalous Hall effect. However, the relationship between altermagnetism and the anomalous Hall effect in MnTe remains debated. Here, using optical magnetic circular dichroism, we observe hysteresis and a memory effect in a MnTe bulk crystal below 250 K. No hysteresis is seen after polishing the bulk crystal and in thin films. We conclude that the hysteresis in magnetic circular dichroism in the bulk crystal is from surface defects. The similar temperature dependence and a sign change between circular dichroism and the anomalous Hall effect indicate that the origin of the anomalous Hall effect in transport is likely not intrinsic.

</details>


### [71] [Full-Potential Korringa Kohn Rostoker Study of Structural and Mechanical Properties in SiGeSn Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41838)
*Wasim Raja Mondal,Swarnava Ghosh,Ka-Ming Tam,Markus Eisenbach,Yang Wang,Hanna Terletska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Accurate first-principles descriptions of open, covalently bonded crystal structures remain challenging within conventional Korringa--Kohn--Rostoker (KKR) approaches owing to the spherical approximations commonly employed for the effective crystal potential. Here, we systematically assess the recently developed full-potential KKR (KKR-FP) implementation in the open-source MuST package and demonstrate its applicability to diamond-structured group-IV semiconductors and SiGeSn alloys. Benchmark calculations for diamond-structured Si reveal substantial limitations of the conventional muffin-tin and atomic sphere approximations, whereas KKR-FP accurately reproduces the experimental equilibrium lattice parameter and bulk modulus, yielding 5.433 and 94.9 GPa. The full-potential treatment also recovers the semiconducting character of Si. Further calculations for elemental Ge and Sn establish the applicability of the approach across group-IV materials. Extending this framework to SiGeSn alloys, we investigate their composition-dependent equilibrium lattice parameters, bulk moduli, and mixing enthalpies. The calculated lattice parameters exhibit positive deviations from Vegard's law, while the bulk modulus decreases substantially with changes in alloy composition. Moreover, positive mixing enthalpies of 180.92 and 346.32 meV/atom are obtained for Si0.5Ge0.375Sn0.125 and Si0.375Ge0.25Sn0.375, respectively, indicating an energetic penalty for homogeneous alloy formation relative to the selected elemental reference states. These findings highlight the importance of nonspherical potential contributions in accurately describing open-structured semiconductors and establish KKR-FP within MuST as a promising framework for investigating the structural properties and mixing energetics of compositionally complex group-IV alloys

</details>


### [72] [Magnetic control of reflectionless states and an intrinsic-mode exceptional point in a magnon-phonon coupling system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41851)
*Mingyuan Ma,Jiongjie Wang,Chong Chen,Weiqi Du,Liyang Liao,Yanzhang Cao,Peisen Liu,Boyuan Xiao,Deqi Tao,Shixuan Liang,Sulei Fu,Jiang Xiao,Cheng Song,Feng Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于FeGaB与32°YX-LiNbO3异质结的片上磁子-声子系统（magnon-phonon system）实现了反射零点（reflectionless states, RLs）与奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）的二维磁调控：磁场大小调节失谐、磁场角度调节耦合强度，并以统一单端口输入-输出模型解释复S11响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无反射态与奇异点是非厄米系统（non-Hermitian systems）的标志性奇异响应，在传感、波动控制与能量操控方面具有潜力，但在紧凑集成平台上同时实现并独立调控二者仍具挑战性。

Method: 采用FeGaB-32°YX-LiNbO3异质结构构建片上磁子-声子系统；通过磁场大小调控磁子-声子失谐（detuning），通过旋转磁场调控磁子-声子耦合强度；结合实验测量的复S11谱与统一单端口输入-输出模型（one-port input-output model）进行分析。

Result: 磁场大小使低损耗反射零点跨越实频轴，产生两个近零反射、可场调控的无反射态；实验测得复S11在反射零点奇点附近形成闭合围线，累积-2π相位绕转（phase winding）。旋转磁场使本征磁子-声子模式谱穿越奇异点，该点由实部与虚部本征值分裂的互换（eigenvalue splitting）加以识别。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个磁可编程的非厄米磁子-声子平台，可用于集成波动控制与紧凑型矢量磁传感（vector magnetic sensing），并表明无反射态与奇异点可由磁场的大小与角度两个维度独立调控。

Abstract: Reflectionless states (RLs) and exceptional points (EPs) are hallmark singular responses of non-Hermitian systems, with potential applications in sensing, wave control, and energy manipulation. Yet, realizing and independently tuning these states in a compact integrated platform remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate two-dimensional magnetic control of RLs and EPs in an on-chip magnon-phonon system based on a FeGaB-32 degree-YX-LiNbO3 heterostructure. The magnetic-field magnitude tunes the magnon-phonon detuning, driving a low-loss reflection zero across the real-frequency axis and producing two field-tunable RLs with near-vanishing reflection. A closed contour of the experimentally measured complex S11 around the reflection-zero singularity accumulates a -2pi phase winding. Independently, rotating the magnetic field tunes the magnon-phonon coupling strength, driving the intrinsic magnon-phonon eigenmode spectrum across an exceptional point, identified by the exchange between real-part and imaginary-part eigenvalue splitting. A unified one-port input-output model connects the measured complex S11 response to the field-driven dynamics of the reflection zeros and the angle-tuned intrinsic magnon-phonon eigenmodes. These results establish a magnetically programmable non-Hermitian magnon-phonon platform for integrated wave control and compact vector magnetic sensing.

</details>


### [73] [Generative Design and Graph Neural Network Screening of Novel Bimetallic Alloy Catalysts for the Hydrogen Evolution Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41932)
*Vipin Kurian Elavunkel,Sylvain Le Tonquesse,Hadrien Vroylandt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个耦合扩散生成模型（MatterGen）、机器学习力场（MatterSim）与图神经网络（GNN）预测的计算流程，用于发现现有吸附数据库未覆盖的双金属析氢反应（HER）催化剂，并通过DFT验证筛选出四种近热中性候选合金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找地球储量丰富的析氢反应（hydrogen evolution reaction, HER）催化剂是可扩展绿色制氢的核心挑战，而现有吸附数据库在结构与化学计量比上的覆盖有限，难以支撑对新型双金属催化剂的系统探索。

Method: 以标注最低氢吸附能量的块体合金结构微调MatterGen并导向近零吸附能生成候选；经表面能筛选确定稳定低指数晶面并构建显式slab模型，用MEGNet与ALIGNN两个GNN代理模型进行一致性筛选，辅以MatterSim能量差三角验证。

Result: 一致性筛选得到近热中性双金属候选短名单，其中八个经平面波DFT验证，Pd5Au、Pt5Rh、Cu5Pt3与Pd13Ru3四种合金的氢吸附能落在热中性窗口内，八个合金均通过声子计算确认动力学稳定。

Conclusion: 生成的稳定性、模型间一致性与DFT验证相结合的多级筛选流程可有效拓展数据库之外的催化剂化学空间，四种经DFT验证的双金属合金可作为后续实验跟进的HER候选材料。

Abstract: Identifying earth-abundant catalysts for the hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) is a central challenge in scalable green hydrogen production. We present a computational pipeline that couples diffusion-based generative modelling, machine-learning force-field relaxation, and graph neural network (GNN) property prediction to discover bimetallic HER catalysts beyond the structures and stoichiometries covered by existing adsorption databases. A generative model (MatterGen) is fine-tuned on bulk alloy structures labelled with their minimum hydrogen adsorption energy and steered towards adsorption energies near zero. From the generated pool we retain the stable structures that are novel bimetallic alloys: binary compositions built from the training-set metals but at stoichiometries or structures absent from that database. Each candidate's low-index facets are ranked by a machine-learning-force-field (MatterSim) surface-energy screen, and only the most stable terminations are converted into explicit slab models with hydrogen adsorbed at all high-symmetry surface sites and relaxed with the same force field. Two GNN surrogates (MEGNet and ALIGNN), trained on a high-throughput density functional theory (DFT) dataset, then screen the resulting slab candidates. Because the two models encode the local environment through different inductive biases, we retain only the alloys on which both agree, reducing the influence of any single model. This consensus screening, combined with a MatterSim energy-difference triangulation, resulted in a shortlist of near-thermoneutral bimetallic candidates. Eight are validated with plane-wave DFT, and four of them, Pd5Au, Pt5Rh, Cu5Pt3, and Pd13Ru3, bind hydrogen within the thermoneutral window. All eight alloys are dynamically stable by phonon calculations. These four alloys are highlighted as DFT-validated bimetallic HER candidates for experimental follow-up.

</details>


### [74] [Shock Loading Accesses Reaction Pathways beyond Accelerated Thermolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41955)
*Mingjie Wen,Jiahe Han,Zihan Zhou,Hao Wang,Xueyong Guo,Qingzhao Chu,Dongping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究构建了可用于C/H/N/O含能材料（energetic materials）的反应性机器学习势（reactive machine-learning potential）EMFF-MACE，在同一势能面上对20种材料开展热分解（thermolysis）模拟、对10种材料开展冲击加载（shock loading）模拟，揭示两种加载历史下的化学状态与局部结构显著分异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热分解与冲击加载虽可产生相似的主要稳定产物，但其化学演化与反应路径不同，缺乏在同一势能面上进行系统比较的手段，因此需要统一势函数以区分加载历史对分解结构状态与后续反应路径的影响。

Method: 开发EMFF-MACE反应性机器学习势，覆盖C/H/N/O含能材料，支持20种材料的热分解模拟与10种材料的冲击模拟，并在匹配的归一化反应进度（normalized reaction progress）下进行化学状态比较，结合局部环境与反应网络（reaction network）分析。

Result: 平均反应状态相似度（reaction-state similarity）从20%进度时的94.0%降至轨迹终点时的25.9%；47.1–92.6%的冲击局部环境落在对应材料热分解参考域之外。结构分析显示冲击下分子间重组增强，并出现额外的缔合与重排分支。

Conclusion: 加载历史（loading history）不仅影响热分解速率，更决定了分解过程中可及的结构状态与后续反应路径，尽管早期分解化学部分共享。

Abstract: Thermolysis and shock loading can produce similar major stable products in energetic materials, despite different chemical evolution and reaction pathways. Here, we develop EMFF-MACE, a reactive machine-learning potential for C/H/N/O energetic materials, enabling thermolysis simulations of 20 materials and shock simulations of 10 on a common potential energy surface. Comparisons at matched normalized reaction progress reveal divergent chemical states and local structural environments. Mean reaction-state similarity decreases from 94.0% at 20% progress to 25.9% at trajectory endpoints, while 47.1-92.6% of shock local environments lie outside corresponding material-specific thermolysis reference domains. Structural and reaction-network analyses reveal enhanced intermolecular reorganization and additional association and rearrangement branches under shock loading, despite partially shared early decomposition chemistry. These findings show that loading history influences the structural states and subsequent reaction pathways accessed during decomposition, rather than merely the rate of thermolysis.

</details>


### [75] [Tensor cluster expansion as an efficient surrogate for multi-ACE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41969)
*Jacob Jeffries,Enrique Martinez,Eva Zarkadoula*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将格点上的张量团簇展开（tensor cluster expansion, TCE）推广至任意关联阶数，并证明其广义特征张成 MACE 特征的一个子空间，从而可用更廉价的 TCE 表示对 MACE 特征进行化学多样性预筛，实现合金化过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）单层有限温度振动性质的可行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合金化过渡金属二硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenide, TMD）单层中，预测随化学有序度变化的有限温度热力学性质需要能描述化学多样性的构型筛选；而直接使用多-原子团簇展开（multi-ACE, MACE）等昂贵表示进行训练与筛选代价较高，因此需要更廉价的替代特征。

Method: 将格点张量团簇展开（tensor cluster expansion, TCE）框架推广到任意关联阶数，论证其广义特征构成 MACE 特征的子空间；结合最大熵子集采样（maximum-entropy subset sampling），以 TCE 特征作为廉价代理特征从 MACE 特征中经验性地恢复化学多样性，并用两组子集微调 MACE 势。

Result: 利用微调后的 MACE 势预测了不同化学有序度下合金化 TMD 单层的温度依赖振动性质；结果表明 TCE 特征可作为廉价代理特征预筛化学丰富构型，使原子团簇展开（atomic cluster expansion, ACE）势的微调更高效。

Conclusion: TCE 特征为基于 MACE 特征的构型预筛提供了低成本途径，从而在普通硬件上实现对有限温度合金热力学的可行预测，提升了 ACE 势微调的计算效率。

Abstract: In this work, we generalize an on-lattice tensor cluster expansion (TCE) framework to an arbitrary correlation order. We show that these generalized tensor cluster expansion features span a subspace of MACE features, suggesting that TCE features can sufficiently pre-screen MACE features for chemical diversity in alloyed transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers. We show that, using maximum-entropy subset sampling, we can empirically recover chemical diversity from MACE features using a cheaper TCE representation. We then fine-tune a multi-ACE (MACE) potential with both subsets, and use this potential to predict temperature-dependent vibrational properties in alloyed TMD monolayers with varying chemical ordering. In our approach, TCE features can be used as cheap surrogate features for pre-screening chemically rich configurations using MACE features, allowing for the more efficient finetuning of atomic cluster expansion (ACE) potentials, thus achieving a tractable prediction of finite-temperature alloy thermodynamics on modest hardware.

</details>


### [76] [Generation and spectroscopic characterization of continuous-wave 148.4-nm light in strontium tetraborate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42055)
*Ming Guan,Yang Wang,Heng Tu,Kaifgeng Zhou,Zhengrong Xiao,Zhong Zuo,Songpo Xu,Shaogang Yu,Xin Tong,Linqiang Hua,Xiaojun Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过四硼酸锶中的随机准相位匹配产生148.4 nm连续波光源，并用紧凑棱镜光谱仪完成表征；室温输出1.9 nW，最佳晶体温度下达5.7 nW，且转换效率对温度呈强烈结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态核钟（nuclear clock）需以148.4 nm连续波（continuous-wave, CW）辐射驱动²²⁹Th同质异能跃迁（isomeric transition），而296.8 nm倍频是最直接的技术路线，因此需要实用化的148.4 nm光源及其表征手段。

Method: 在四硼酸锶（strontium tetraborate）中利用随机准相位匹配（random quasi-phase matching）产生CW 148.4 nm光；自制紧凑棱镜光谱仪以CaF₂棱镜分离谐波与泵浦，由日盲光电倍增管（solar-blind photomultiplier）旋转角读取波长并给出绝对功率标定。

Result: 光谱仪分辨率为δλ≈0.6 nm并能分辨两条谱线；室温下148.4 nm输出1.9 nW，晶体最佳温度时升至5.7 nW；转换效率随温度强烈起伏，在30至50摄氏度间出现多个显著极大值，相邻极值仅相差几度。

Conclusion: 结果表明温度微扰即可使输出在极大与极小间切换，为固态²²⁹Th核钟用148.4 nm光源的优化与稳定化提供实用指导。

Abstract: Continuous-wave (CW) radiation at 148.4~nm is required to drive the $^{229}$Th isomeric transition of a solid-state nuclear clock, and frequency doubling of 296.8-nm light is its most direct source. We generate CW 148.4-nm light by random quasi-phase matching in strontium tetraborate and characterize it with a compact prism spectrometer built for the purpose. The spectrometer separates the harmonic from the pump on a CaF$_2$ prism, reads the wavelength from the rotation angle of a solar-blind photomultiplier, resolves the two lines with $δλ\approx 0.6~nm$, and yields an absolute power calibration. At room temperature the source delivers $1.9~nW$ at 148.4~nm, rising to 5.7 nW at the optimum crystal temperature. The conversion efficiency is also strongly structured in temperature: between 30 degree and 50 degree it shows several pronounced maxima, with neighbouring extrema only a few degrees apart, so that a small temperature excursion moves the output from a maximum to a minimum. These results provide practical guidance for optimizing and stabilizing 148.4-nm sources for solid-state $^{229}$Th nuclear clocks.

</details>


### [77] [Current-driven transient dynamics of magnetic-octupole domain walls in noncollinear antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42065)
*Myoung-Woo Yoo,Axel Hoffmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用多极微磁框架研究Mn₃Sn中自旋轨道矩驱动的八极畴壁瞬态动力学，揭示电流脉冲后畴壁运动由八极畴旋转弛豫主导，时间尺度约33 ps，并发现复合180°畴壁存在额外的慢弛豫通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流驱动的非共线反铁磁体磁八极畴壁有望用于快速自旋电子器件，但其瞬态动力学尚未被充分理解。

Method: 采用多极微磁框架（multipole micromagnetic framework）模拟Mn₃Sn中自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque）驱动的畴壁运动，并结合均匀畴解析模型与空间非均匀织构模拟进行分析。

Result: 三个对称相关的60°畴壁在电流驱动下表现出等效动力学（不同于场驱动情形）；代表性60°畴壁的脉冲后运动由周围八极畴电流诱导旋转的弛豫主导，特征时间约33 ps，可由均匀畴解析模型定量描述；复合180°畴壁还表现出与其组成畴壁相对运动相关的额外慢弛豫，且脉冲后响应随交换刚度（exchange stiffness）与磁各向异性系统变化。

Conclusion: 研究识别出不同的畴与内坐标弛豫通道，并确立了控制反铁磁自旋电子器件中磁八极畴壁脉冲定位的内禀时间尺度。

Abstract: Current-driven magnetic-octupole domain walls in noncollinear antiferromagnets are promising for fast spintronic devices, yet their transient dynamics are not yet fully understood. Here, we investigate spin-orbit-torque-driven domain-wall motion in Mn$_\text{3}$Sn using a multipole micromagnetic framework. The three symmetry-related 60° walls exhibit equivalent current-driven dynamics, unlike their field-driven motion. For a representative 60° wall, the post-pulse motion is governed by relaxation of the current-induced rotation of the surrounding octupole domains, with a characteristic time of approximately 33 ps quantitatively captured by a uniform-domain analytic model. By resolving spatially nonuniform texture dynamics beyond this analytic description, the simulations further reveal an additional slow relaxation in a composite 180° wall associated with the relative motion of its constituent walls. The post-pulse response also varies systematically with the exchange stiffness and magnetic anisotropies. These results identify distinct domain and internal-coordinate relaxation channels and establish the intrinsic time scales governing pulse-controlled positioning of magnetic-octupole domain walls in antiferromagnetic spintronic devices.

</details>


### [78] [Quantum 3xplorer: AI-Native Engine for Next-Generation Quantum Materials Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42108)
*Vsevolod Biryukov,Sergey V. Barabash,Pranab Kumar Das,Jack Deslippe,Mauro Del Ben,Felipe H. da Jornada,Yuming Shi,Johannes Voss,Zhenglu Li,Piero Altoe,Kamal Choudhary,Ruth Pachter,Steven G. Louie,Marvin L. Cohen,Timur Bazhirov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出Quantum 3xplorer（Q3），一个由AI编码代理在领域科学家监督下从零编写的AI原生量子模拟引擎（AI-native quantum simulation engine），并端到端实现平面波赝势DFT作为首个验证案例。Q3复现参考实现、单核运行更快、可扩展至GPU超级计算机，且开发周期由数年缩短至数周，结果以模式校验数据标准输出、便于AI直接使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子结构（electronic-structure）代码多为数十年前的软件架构，导致新物理模型的实现、验证与在新硬件上的部署缓慢且成本高昂，难以支撑AI驱动的材料发现对数据的需求。

Method: 采用AI代理编写、科学家设定物理与验证准则的人机协作范式：由AI编码代理（AI coding agents）从零构建引擎，并以平面波赝势DFT（plane-wave pseudopotential DFT）为首个端到端验证案例，覆盖模守恒、超软与投影缀加波（projector-augmented-wave）形式、自旋极化、DFT+U及色散修正，并含力、应力、结构弛豫与电子结构分析。

Result: Q3复现了已有参考实现（reference implementations），在代表性基准（benchmarks）中单核运行更快，并可在GPU加速超级计算机（GPU-accelerated supercomputers）上扩展；开发周期为周级而非年级。其分层、物理优先（physics-first）架构将抽象量子力学概念与数值表示分离，输入输出遵循经模式校验（schema-validated）的数据标准，并以Apache 2.0开源。

Conclusion: Q3指向计算量子科学范式的转变：科学家定义物理、构建模型并设定验证标准，AI代理负责编写、测试与优化代码，使从新理论到可信高性能实现的数十年路径可能缩短至数天，从而显著加速科学发现。

Abstract: Quantum-mechanical approaches, such as density functional theory (DFT), underpin much of modern computational materials science and are the primary source of data for AI-driven discovery. Yet the established electronic-structure codes today represent decades-old software architectures, making new physical models slow and costly to implement, validate, and deploy on emerging hardware. We present Quantum 3xplorer (Q3, pronounced "Quantum Explorer"), an AI-native quantum simulation engine written from scratch by AI coding agents under the direction and supervision of domain scientists. As a first and demanding validation case, we implement plane-wave pseudopotential DFT end to end: norm-conserving, ultrasoft, and projector-augmented-wave formalisms, spin polarization, DFT+U, and dispersion corrections, together with forces, stresses, structural relaxation, and electronic-structure analysis. Q3 reproduces established reference implementations, runs faster on a single core in representative benchmarks, and scales across GPU-accelerated supercomputers. It was developed in weeks rather than years. Its layered, physics-first architecture separates abstract quantum-mechanical concepts from their numerical representations, making its framework extendable beyond plane waves to other basis sets and levels of theory. Its inputs and outputs follow structured, schema-validated data standards, making every result AI-ready. Q3 is open source under Apache 2.0 and built to be reused, extended, and improved by the research community. Q3 points to a paradigm shift in how computational quantum science is done. Scientists define the physics, formulate the models, and set the criteria for validation; AI agents write, test, and optimize the code. The decades-long path from a new theory to a trusted, high-performance implementation can thus collapse to days, significantly accelerating scientific discovery.

</details>

### [79] [Physics-Grounded Simulation Enables Zero-Shot Molecular and Reaction Analysis in Scanning Tunneling Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40666)
*Yuanqin He,Zhiyu Wang,Yang Bao,Tao Lin,Hai Bi*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 提出一种仿真辅助的零样本分析框架（zero-shot analysis framework），仅需新体系的前驱体信息，即可从扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）图像中定量识别分子产物与反应路径，检测器在实验图像上取得0.92的微平均F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当新化学体系缺乏标注图像（labeled images）时，从STM图像定量识别分子产物与反应路径十分困难，现有方法依赖目标体系的实验图像或标注数据。

Method: 框架耦合两个组件：NanoSim基于预测的反应产物与采样表面场景生成物理可信的合成图像（physics-grounded synthetic images）；NanoAnalysis将分子检测结果转化为产物种群、空间排布及机理阐释。训练过程中不使用目标体系的实验图像或标注。

Result: 在代表性表面反应（on-surface reaction）上，检测器于实验图像取得微平均F1（micro-averaged F1）为0.92；检测分子分析揭示退火依赖的种群变化与反应位点偏好，且与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 该框架实现零样本分析能力；其模块化结构（modular organization）允许独立使用其他体系的异构数据改进各组件，为持续泛化（generalization）提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Quantitative identification of molecular products and reaction pathways from scanning tunneling microscopy images is difficult when labeled images are unavailable for a new chemical system. We develop a simulation-assisted framework for zero-shot analysis that requires only precursor information for a new target system, with no experimental images or annotations from that system used during model training. The framework couples two functional components: NanoSim generates physics-grounded synthetic images from predicted reaction products and sampled surface scenes, while NanoAnalysis translates molecular detections into product populations, spatial arrangements, and mechanistic interpretations. Applied to a representative on-surface reaction, the detector achieves a micro-averaged F1 score of 0.92 on experimental images. Analysis of the detected molecules reveals annealing-dependent population changes and reaction-site preferences consistent with experimental observations. Beyond this capability, its modular organization allows heterogeneous data from other systems to be used to improve each component independently, providing a scalable route for continued generalization.

</details>

### [80] [Field-resolved observation and quantification of the instantaneous Kerr response in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42098)
*Haifeng Lin,Hannes Kempf,Hinduja Bhuvanendran,Aitor De Andrés,Markus Ludwig,Nicolò Maccaferri*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱（field-resolved degenerate pump-probe spectroscopy）在中红外5 μm波段直接测量锗（Ge）的非线性相位响应，获得瞬时克尔效应主导的结论，并给出非线性折射率 n₂ = (0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学克尔效应（optical Kerr effect）是非线性光子学与中红外技术的基础，但传统基于强度的方法（如 z-scan）只能间接推断非线性相移，且无法分辨非线性响应的时间演化，难以区分瞬时克尔非线性与自由载流子等延迟贡献。

Method: 采用场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱，在5 μm波长下测量锗透射探针电场随泵浦-探测延迟（pump-probe delay）的变化，直接提取泵浦诱导的相移及其时间演化，同时保留对吸收变化的敏感性。

Result: 观察到交叉相位调制（cross-phase modulation）随泵浦强度包络变化，并在泵浦与探针不再重叠时消失，符合瞬时克尔响应；相移随泵浦强度线性变化，得到 n₂ = (0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。

Conclusion: 在其实验条件下，瞬时克尔效应是所测非线性相移的主导贡献；场分辨泵浦-探测光谱可作为直接、时间分辨地确定非线性折射率并区分瞬时与延迟光学响应的方法。

Abstract: The optical Kerr effect, whereby the refractive index of a material changes with light intensity, underpins nonlinear photonics and emerging mid-infrared technologies. Accurate determination of the nonlinear refractive index is therefore essential, yet conventional intensity-based techniques infer the nonlinear phase shift indirectly from changes in beam propagation or optical spectra. Moreover, methods such as the $z$-scan cannot resolve the temporal evolution of the nonlinear response, making it difficult to distinguish instantaneous Kerr nonlinearities from delayed contributions, including free-carrier effects. Here, we investigate the nonlinear optical response of germanium at $5 μ\mathrm{m}$ using field-resolved degenerate pump-probe spectroscopy. By measuring the transmitted probe electric field as a function of pump-probe delay, we directly retrieve the pump-induced phase shift and its temporal evolution while retaining sensitivity to changes in absorption. We observe cross-phase modulation that follows the pump intensity envelope and vanishes when the pump and probe no longer overlap, consistent with an instantaneous Kerr response. The phase shift scales linearly with pump intensity, yielding a nonlinear refractive index of $n_2=(0.87\pm0.16)\times10^{-17} \mathrm{m}^2/\mathrm{W}$. Our results establish the instantaneous Kerr effect as the dominant contribution to the measured nonlinear phase shift under our experimental conditions and demonstrate field-resolved pump-probe spectroscopy as a direct, time-resolved method for determining the nonlinear refractive index and distinguishing instantaneous from delayed optical responses.

</details>

### [81] [Pure annihilation decay $B_c^+ \to ST$ with improved perturbative QCD formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41078)
*Sheng-Yu Wang,Xin Liu,Zhen-Jun Xiao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 首次在标准模型（standard model）框架下研究纯湮灭衰变（pure annihilation decay）$B_c^+ \to ST$，采用改进的微扰QCD（improved perturbative QCD, iPQCD）方法在强耦合领头阶计算分支比，得到CKM favored道约$10^{-6}$量级的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯湮灭衰变$B_c^+ \to ST$此前未被研究，而轻标量（$1^3P_0$）与张量（$1^3P_2$）终态涉及轻标量的内部结构（$q\bar q$赋值与两方案分类）及QCD动力学，值得系统探讨。

Abstract: We study the pure annihilation decay $B_c^+ \to ST$ for the first time in the standard model with $S$ and $T$ representing the states of the light scalar ($1^3\!P_0$) and tensor ($1^3\!P_2$) correspondingly. Utilizing the assignment $q\bar q$ for light scalars with two-scenarios classification, we calculate the branching ratios of $B_c^+ \to ST$ with the improved perturbative QCD (iPQCD) formalism at leading order in the strong coupling $α_s$. The large branching ratios around $10^{-6}$ within uncertainties in the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa-favored channels, such as $B_c^+ \to a_0(1450) a_2(1320)$, $B_c^+ \to a_0(980, 1450)^+ f_2(1270)$, $B_c^+ \to K_0^*(700, 1430) K_2^*(1430)$, and $B_c^+ \to f_0(500, 1370, 1500) a_2(1320)^+$, are obtained in the iPQCD formalism, which would be tested at the Large Hadron Collider-beauty detector in the (near) future. The relevant tests to these theoretical predictions of large branching ratios associated with typical relative ratios could provide important opportunities for understanding the QCD dynamics of the involved modes or light scalars, as well as for exploring the reliability of the iPQCD formalism. Moreover, the direct charge-parity violating asymmetries in the decay $B_c^+ \to ST$ are naturally zero, since only tree-diagram contributions are involved.

Abstract: Since the turn of the century it becomes evident that the naive quark model is not capable to describe the rich phenomenology of hadrons, especially in the doubly heavy sector. For a large number of those states a molecular structure appears to be a natural description. In these proceedings I review the theory of hadronic molecules, how they can be described systematically in effective field theories and what imprint this structure leaves in observables. On two examples I explain non-trivial predictions emerging from the molecular hypothesis that can be tested in future experiments and lattice QCD calculations, promising deep insights into the inner workings of QCD.

</details>

Conclusion: 研究估算了序参量场被困于假真空（false vacuum）期间经历的e折数（e-folds），为宇宙学提供重要信息；引力在超冷一级相变中的作用具有可观测检验性。

Abstract: Theories with radiative mass generation and radiative symmetry breaking (RSB) lead to first-order phase transitions (FOPTs) and the production of gravitational waves (GWs) as well as primordial black holes if supercooling lasts long enough. Here we investigate the role of gravity in supercooled FOPTs that occur in the RSB scenario. We use a model-independent approach that is valid when a quantity (which we explicitly determine and is computable once the model is specified) is small enough. Eventually, the curvature of spacetime becomes relevant if the FOPT does not complete in the case in which gravity is ignored. We also include the effect on the FOPT of the non-minimal couplings with coefficients of zero mass dimension. Depending on such non-minimal couplings, gravity can enhance or reduce the region of parameter space where the phase transition completes. Observable consequences of the role of gravity on the FOPT that can be probed by current and future GW detectors are then worked out and we explore the possibility that the background of GWs detected by pulsar timing arrays is produced by FOPTs in the RSB scenario. We also estimate the number of e-folds that elapse when the field playing the role of the order parameter is trapped in the false vacuum, which provides important information for cosmology.

</details>

Conclusion: 双流体致密天体的类别构成与稳定性由暗物质粒子质量与自相互作用强度共同决定，强自相互作用可产生带延展暗晕的稳定暗物质主导分支，并可使重子物质表面引力红移超过2，提示此类构型在观测上具有潜在可区分特征。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium structure and stability against radial oscillations of a variety of two-fluid compact objects containing ordinary baryonic matter admixed with cold dark matter coupled through the background spacetime. For the ordinary fluid, we employ a QCD equation of state compatible with chiral effective field theory at nuclear densities (supplemented by the BPS and Negele-Vautherin models at extremely low densities) and with perturbative QCD in the high-density regime while also satisfying also current astrophysical data. For the dark fluid, we assume self-interacting fermionic matter with particle masses of $m_f=$ 2, 5, 10, and 100 GeV and two self-interaction strengths of $y=0.1$ and $10^3$. By scanning a large range of central pressures for both fluids, we identify the presence of dark compact planets, dark white dwarfs, dark neutron stars and the so-called dark oysters. Importantly, these objects are not only in two-fluid hydrostatic equilibrium, but they are also stable against two-fluid radial oscillations. We find that the relative representation of each class in the radially stable sample is strongly sensitive to the combinations of $m_f$ and $y$, with some combinations entirely suppressing some of these object classes within the sampled domain while favoring others. In addition to these classes, strong self-repulsion supports stable, dark-matter-dominated branches with compact ordinary-matter cores and extended dark halos, approaching the self-interacting fermion-star limit. Finally, we calculate the gravitational redshift at the ordinary-matter surface, $z_{\rm OM}$, including the contribution of extended dark-matter halos for which we found radially stable configurations with $z_{\rm OM}>2$.

TL;DR: 本文以全息（holography, AdS/CFT）、弱耦合动理学理论（kinetic theory）与多种流体力学（hydrodynamics）框架，研究共形系统在一维周期性膨胀压缩驱动下的剪切响应与平衡化过程，揭示线性区存在普适循环吸引子（cyclic attractor）及强耦合下非流体模导致的振铃效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动下的非平衡响应与平衡化机制在动理学与强耦合理论中可能存在本质差异，需系统比较不同耦合强度理论对剪切驱动响应的预测，尤其是循环吸引子的普适性及其流体力学描述的有效性。

Method: 采用全息（AdS/CFT）、弱耦合动理学理论（弛豫时间近似、标量φ⁴理论、QCD有效动理学的Yang–Mills部分）及流体力学（Navier–Stokes、MIS、rBRSSS）三类方法，对振幅A与频率ω可变的周期驱动进行线性响应解析分析与数值计算。

Result: 线性区（A≪1）系统趋于循环吸引子，该吸引子在各动理学理论间普适且可由MIS理论准确描述：慢驱动（ωτ_π≪1）退化为Navier–Stokes极限，快驱动下剪切应力饱和至自由流（free-streaming）值；强耦合全息系统中非流体准正规模（quasinormal modes）激发产生特征振铃（ringing），且对二阶流体力学的曲率项敏感。

Conclusion: 全息与动理学理论在趋近循环吸引子的方式上的差异源于二者非流体谱（non-hydrodynamic spectra）不同；长时间尺度上非线性粘性加热（与振幅平方成正比）升高温度、降低ωτ_π，导致吸引子缓慢漂移并最终在极晚时收敛至Navier–Stokes流体力学。

Abstract: We study equilibration and hydrodynamic response in conformal systems undergoing periodic expansion and contraction along one spatial direction. Using strongly coupled holography (AdS/CFT), weakly coupled kinetic theories (relaxation-time approximation, scalar $φ^4$ theory and the Yang--Mills sector of QCD effective kinetic theory) and hydrodynamics (Navier--Stokes, Müller--Israel--Stewart (MIS) and rBRSSS), we investigate the shear response to a periodic drive of varying amplitude $A$ and frequency $ω$, both analytically in linear response and numerically. We find that, at the linear level, $A\ll 1$, periodically driven systems approach a cyclic attractor that in general differs from Navier--Stokes behaviour. This attractor is universal among kinetic theories and accurately described by MIS theory: for slow driving, $ωτ_π\ll 1$, where $τ_π$ is the shear relaxation time, it approaches the Navier--Stokes limit, whereas for fast driving the shear stress saturates at its non-interacting (free-streaming) value. In strongly coupled holographic systems, the periodic drive excites non-hydrodynamic quasinormal modes, which produce a characteristic ringing in the approach to the cyclic attractor, in contrast to the purely exponential approach found in kinetic theories. This difference is a direct consequence of the distinct non-hydrodynamic spectra of the two classes of theories. Moreover, unlike in kinetic theory, the holographic response is sensitive to the curvature terms of second-order hydrodynamics. Over longer times, non-linear viscous heating, which is quadratic in the drive amplitude, raises the temperature and thereby lowers $ωτ_π$, since $τ_π\sim 1/T$. This induces a slow drift of the cyclic attractor, which eventually converges towards Navier--Stokes hydrodynamics at asymptotically late times.

</details>

Our theoretical results suggest a catalytic effect from the presence of the background magnetic field, that may enhance the neutral pion production by several orders of magnitude at very small energies $s \lesssim 1~\text{GeV}^2$, while the effect is negligible at larger energies $s\gtrsim 10~\text{GeV}^2$. In contrast, the $η$ and $η'$ production rates are nearly insensitive to the magnetic field presence
  (as compared to vacuum).

It is shown that the phase transition dynamics of Abelian-Higgs vortices, namely the pair creation of vortex and antivortex under thermal fluctuations, can be significantly modified in the oscillating axion background.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Self-diffusion in crystallized Coulomb (Yukawa) plasmas proceeds by the thermally activated formation of vacancy-interstitial pairs and the migration of these defects through the lattice, commonly known as Frenkel pairs in the materials literature. Molecular dynamics has recently resolved an Eyring law for diffusion in unstrained crystals, which we generalize to deformed crystals in this work. We simulate sheared Yukawa crystals and show that a static elastic strain exponentially enhances diffusion due to the additional elastic energy stored in the region that participates in Frenkel pair formation relative to the unstrained crystal: $D(\varepsilon)=D_0(Γ^{*})\exp[V^{*}σ/k_{\rm B}T]$, with $σ=\tfrac12μ\varepsilon^{2}$ the elastic energy density stored by the shear strain $\varepsilon$ and $V^{*}$ the activation volume. We find a single activation volume $N_{\rm act} = n_iV^{*}\simeq31$ suffices for typical astrophysical screenings with a reduction at higher screening. This suggests Frenkel pair formation requires only a brief coordination of the nearest neighbors at a thermal maximum in oscillation. Stress-enhanced diffusion supplies a microphysical mechanism for creep in strained neutron star crusts and crystallizing white dwarf cores.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁约束聚变中，载流表面及其附近磁场评估是常见计算瓶颈；当目标点逼近源表面时，Biot–Savart 与 virtual-casing 核趋于奇异，标准 Gauss–Legendre 与上采样梯形求积（quadrature）精度严重下降。

Method: 针对矢量 Biot–Savart 与 virtual-casing（Stratton–Chu）场建立球谐 QBX（quadrature-by-expansion）格式，将磁场表示为正则立体谐波（regular-solid-harmonic）矢量势的解析旋度；并以 header-only、性能可移植的 Kokkos 库实现，支持 CPU 与 GPU 后端及目标特定与局部修正加速。

Result: 在 Malhotra 等旋转椭圆（rotating-ellipse）基准上，QBX 在近表面区域保持 2–5×10^{-6} 的相对误差且成本与距离无关；而上采样梯形求积出现 O(1) 误差，节点数需按 (h/d)^2 增长。方法进一步在 Wendelstein 7-X 实际位形上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该构造在每个展开阶数下均给出无散度离散场、提供解析导数，并实现截断、求积与几何误差的先验分离，为聚变位形中近表面磁场评估提供了高效且可扩展的数值方案。

Abstract: Accurate magnetic-field evaluation on and near current-carrying surfaces is a recurring computational bottleneck in magnetic-confinement fusion. As targets approach the source surface, Biot--Savart and virtual-casing kernels become singular, causing standard Gauss--Legendre and upsampled trapezoidal quadratures to lose accuracy. We introduce AMPERe, a quadrature-by-expansion (QBX) evaluator for vector magnetostatic kernels on general three-dimensional, non-axisymmetric tokamak and stellarator geometries. The method develops a spherical-harmonic QBX formulation for the vector Biot--Savart and virtual-casing (Stratton--Chu) fields and expresses the resulting magnetic field as the analytic curl of a regular-solid-harmonic vector potential. This construction yields a divergence-free discrete field at every expansion order, provides analytic derivatives, and enables an a priori separation of truncation, quadrature, and geometric errors. On the rotating-ellipse benchmark of Malhotra et al. [1], QBX maintains a relative error of 2--5 x 10^{-6} throughout the near-surface regime at distance-independent cost, whereas upsampled trapezoidal quadrature exhibits O(1) errors and requires node counts scaling as (h/d)^2. AMPERe is implemented as a header-only, performance-portable Kokkos library supporting CPU and GPU backends, with target-specific and local-correction accelerations validated against the baseline. The method is further demonstrated on realistic Wendelstein 7-X configurations, including sheet-current and virtual-casing field evaluations.

</details>


### [90] [Characterization of Phase Transitions with the Annealed Importance Sampling Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41500)
*A. Prat Pou,E. Romero,J. Marti,F. Mazzanti*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Ising 与自旋玻璃模型映射到受限玻尔兹曼机（Restricted Boltzmann Machine, RBM）统一框架下，利用退火重要性采样（Annealed Importance Sampling, AIS）结合基于网络权重的平均场初始化，在强涨落与复杂能量景观下高效、准确地估计配分函数、磁化强度并检测相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变与临界现象（critical phenomena）的传统蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）方法在强涨落和复杂能量景观区域效率受限，本文旨在探索 AIS 作为数值工具的可行性与优势，并寻求可大规模并行、可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 把 Ising 与自旋玻璃（spin-glass）等物理系统直接映射为 RBM，并扩展至合成与已训练架构；在统一 RBM 框架下将 AIS 与标准蒙特卡罗方法对比，并提出由网络权重导出的启发式平均场（mean-field）AIS 初始化方案以提升采样效率与精度。

Result: 经恰当初始化的 AIS 能够可靠估计热力学可观测量（thermodynamic observables），包括配分函数（partition function）与磁化强度（magnetization），并在多种系统尺寸下准确检测顺磁—铁磁（paramagnetic-ferromagnetic）与顺磁—自旋玻璃（paramagnetic-spin-glass）两类相变。

Conclusion: 统一 RBM 表述支持大规模并行采样，配合平均场 AIS 初始化，在所有考察系统中均提升采样效率与准确性，表明 AIS 可作为检测与刻画离散自旋及类自旋系统相变的有效工具。

Abstract: We We explore the use of the Annealed Importance Sampling (AIS) algorithm as a numerical tool to study phase transitions and critical phenomena. By mapping physical systems such as Ising and spin-glass models directly onto Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs), and extending the analysis to both synthetic and trained architectures, we use a unified RBM framework to compare the performance of AIS against standard Monte Carlo methods in regimes characterized by strong fluctuations and complex energy landscapes. The unified RBM formulation allows for massively parallel sampling, offering a scalable and precise alternative to traditional Monte Carlo methods. Building on a simple, heuristic mean-field AIS initialization derived from the weights of the network, the proposed scheme enhances sampling efficiency and accuracy across all systems considered, proving its effectiveness as a tool to detect and characterize phase transitions in discrete spin and spin-like systems. Our results show that a properly initialized AIS reliably estimates thermodynamic observables, including the partition function and magnetization, while accurately detecting both paramagnetic-ferromagnetic and paramagnetic-spin-glass phase transitions across various system sizes.

</details>


### [91] [A variational phase-field theory of cohesive fracture at finite deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41662)
*Ye Feng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在有限变形下兼具运动学与变分一致性的相场（phase-field）内聚断裂理论，通过引入独立的柔度-损伤（compliance-damage）场解耦裂纹几何描述与材料退化，并在能量最小化下自然产生Dirac型集中的强位移不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相场断裂模型要求相场同时承担裂纹几何刻画与材料退化两项职能，导致强位移不连续难以自然涌现、内聚律与体相材料律相互耦合。本文旨在解耦这一双重负担，使尖锐裂纹内聚区模型（cohesive zone model）的体相—界面结构在相场框架下精确重现。

Method: 引入独立的柔度-损伤场，允许其呈弥散分布或奇异集中；对变形梯度（deformation gradient）作秩一加性分解（rank-one additive split），以独立的裂纹应变场与参考法向场显式表达分离；全部场量由单一最小势能原理（principle of minimum potential energy）统一控制，一致耦合非线性体相变形、裂纹分离与裂纹面旋转。

Result: 一维能量比较表明，最小化倾向于柔度损伤与裂纹应变的Dirac型集中，从而在有限相场长度下无需预设即自然出现强位移不连续，而相场本身保持连续；集中能量精确恢复尖锐裂纹内聚区的体相—界面结构。名义牵引—分离律（traction–separation law）不受体相本构与相场长度影响；二维、三维算例验证了在大拉伸、有限转动及复杂裂纹路径下的有效性，且内聚区外近尖端Cauchy应力与Long等（2011）的1/r裂纹尖端渐近解高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该理论实现了裂纹几何与材料退化的解耦，使通用客观超弹体相势与容许混合型内聚律可独立给定；弥散损伤可与集中裂纹共存，在裂纹萌生阶段保留体相能量松弛的变形模式，为有限变形内聚断裂提供了变分一致且可验证的建模框架。

Abstract: This paper formulates a kinematically and variationally consistent phase-field theory for cohesive fracture at finite deformation. The theory introduces an independent compliance-damage field, which accommodates both diffuse distributions and singular concentrations. The phase-field is thereby freed from the dual burden of describing crack geometry and material degradation. A rank-one additive split of the deformation gradient makes separation explicit through independent crack-strain and reference-normal fields. All fields are governed by a single principle of minimum potential energy, which consistently couples nonlinear bulk deformation, crack separation, and crack-plane rotation. One-dimensional energy comparisons show that minimization favors Dirac-type concentration of compliance damage and crack strain. Strong displacement discontinuities thus emerge naturally at a finite phase-field length without being prescribed a priori, while the phase-field remains continuous. The concentrated energy recovers the exact bulk--interface structure of a sharp-crack cohesive zone model. General objective hyperelastic bulk potentials and admissible mixed-mode cohesive laws can therefore be prescribed independently, with the nominal traction--separation law unaffected by the bulk law or phase-field length. Diffuse damage can coexist with concentrated cracks, retaining deformation modes for bulk-energy relaxation during nucleation. Two- and three-dimensional examples validate the theory across large stretches, finite rotations, and complex crack paths. Notably, the computed near-tip Cauchy stress closely matches the analytical 1/r crack-tip asymptotic solution of Long et al. (2011) outside the cohesive zone.

</details>

### [92] [Hamiltonian dynamics with nonconservative forces on extended phase space: An analytical mechanics derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41635)
*Joseph T. A Peterson,Manoranjan Majji,John L. Junkins*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 该文在扩展相空间（extended phase space）框架下，给出非保守（nonconservative）且非自治（nonautonomous）动力系统的完整哈密顿运动方程（Hamiltonian equations of motion），重点处理与时间共轭的动量坐标与广义力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非保守力与扩展相空间在哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics）中各自常见，但二者结合的研究鲜有显式展开，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 沿用经典分析动力学（analytical dynamics）框架，全部推导以任意辛坐标（symplectic coordinates，即正则坐标）或其函数表述，并特别关注时间对应的动量坐标与广义力（generalized force）。

Result: 导出了非保守、非自治动力系统在扩展相空间上的完整哈密顿运动方程，包含时间共轭动量坐标与相应广义力的显式处理。

Conclusion: 将非保守力纳入扩展相空间哈密顿形式是可行且表述简洁的，所得方程在任意正则坐标下均成立，为相关动力学分析提供统一框架。

Abstract: Although nonconservative forces and extended phase space are individually common topics in Hamiltonian dynamics, their combination is seldom developed explicitly. This note derives the full Hamiltonian equations of motion for nonconservative and nonautonomous dynamical systems formulated on extended phase space. Particular attention is given to the momentum coordinate and generalized force associated with time. The classic analytical dynamics framework is used throughout, with all developments formulated in terms of arbitrary symplectic coordinates (i.e., canonical coordinates) or functions thereof.

</details>
