### [93] [Quantum Cosmology at the Cusp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40763)
*Luis A. Anchordoqui,Alek Bedroya,Muldrow Etheredge,Dieter Lüst,Severin Lüst,Alexander Westphal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明在紧致空间中axion动量量子化后，cusp区半经典态的axion动能会通过Wheeler-DeWitt方程把晚期演化驱动至axion支撑的标度解（axion-supported scaling cosmology），并给出转变时刻上限及与跨普朗克审查猜想（trans-Planckian censorship conjecture）的矛盾约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自上而下量子引力（top-down quantum gravity）构造中，saxionic方向在模空间（moduli space）产生尖点（cusps），标量势与axion衰变常数呈指数渐近行为；需要厘清在空间紧致宇宙中axion动量量子化对晚期宇宙学演化的影响。

Method: 在d维时空、约化普朗克单位下，对具有局域化axion的cusp半经典态使用Wheeler-DeWitt方程分析axion动能的量子化贡献，并与axion固定情形的标度解对比。

Result: 当(d-2)α(α+2γ)>4(d-1)时，axion动能驱动晚期演化趋向axion支撑的标度解，且导出了转变时间（transition time）的上界；长期加速膨胀要求2γ>(d-2)α，而跨普朗克审查猜想要求相反不等式2γ≤(d-2)α，后者亦可由axion磁荷膜（brane）须重于哈勃尺度独立得到。

Conclusion: 长期加速膨胀与跨普朗克审查猜想/磁荷膜质量约束在参数空间上互斥，构成对saxionic cusp相关量子引力构造的现象学限制。

Abstract: In top-down quantum gravity constructions, saxionic directions typically lead to cusps in moduli space, where the scalar potential and the axion decay constant asymptotically behave as $V\sim e^{-αφ}$ and $f\sim e^{-γφ}$ in reduced Planck units. Such cusps admit axion-supported scaling cosmologies that expand faster than the corresponding solutions with the axion held fixed, for the same scalar potential. In spatially compact universes, axionic momentum is quantized, and any semiclassical state in the cusp with a localized axion carries nonzero axionic kinetic energy. We show using the Wheeler-DeWitt equation that this contribution drives the late-time evolution towards the axion-supported scaling solution whenever $(d-2)α(α+2γ)>4(d-1)$ in $d$ spacetime dimensions. We derive an upper bound on the transition time. Long-lived accelerated expansion requires $2γ>(d-2)α$. The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture requires the opposite inequality, $2γ\leq(d-2)α$, which also follows independently from requiring the brane magnetically charged under the axion to remain heavier than the Hubble scale.

</details>


### [94] [Guaranteed Slater Conditions for the Numerical Conformal Bootstrap: Fixing Instabilities in SDPB, Navigator Methods, and Skydiving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40764)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出"Slater tools"工具集，用于诊断并修复共形自举（conformal bootstrap）、S-矩阵自举与量子力学自举所对应半定规划（semidefinite program, SDP）中违反原始/对偶 Slater 条件（Slater condition）的问题，并在 3d Ising 混合关联子及 current-scalar 自举中复现、解释并消除已知失败类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自举 SDP 通常由原始-对偶内点法（primal-dual interior-point method）求解，其收敛保证依赖于两侧均存在严格可行点（Slater 条件）；许多自举 SDP 违反该条件，导致收敛缓慢、结论不确定乃至 Skydive 算法失效，亟需诊断与补救手段。

Method: 引入属于 SDPB 可接受 PMP 类（PMP class）的"Slater tools"：（1）诊断工具，度量 Slater 余量（Slater margin），当余量消失时提取揭示证书（exposing certificate）以定位退化；（2）两类独立修复：对原 SDP 目标给出严格界、并在恰当参数下返回精确值的变换（transform），以及利用揭示证书直接移除退化的面约化（facial reduction）。

Result: 在混合关联子 3d Ising 设定中复现并解决了全部已知失败类型；证明 X-Slater 条件（SDPB 的原始侧）在 feasibility 模式下于每个不允许点失效、在所有测试过的 navigator 问题中成立、且可由导航函数（navigator function）上的一个附加条件保证；说明"twist gap 稳定性补救"实为移除正性条件端点处的退化，此类退化常源于块前因子（block prefactor）提取错误，也可在等/不等算子对混合的通道中合法出现；并分类并移除已发表 current-scalar 自举的全部退化，包括 $x=\infty$ 处由同一矩阵块内多项式次数不等引起的、任何 gap 假设均无法治愈的退化。

Conclusion: Slater 条件的违反是自举 SDP 失败的可诊断、可分类、可修复的根源；所提工具在保持 SDPB 兼容性的前提下提供严格界与精确修复，并澄清 twist gap  remedies 的作用机制。

Abstract: The semidefinite programs (SDPs) of the numerical conformal bootstrap, and of S-matrix and quantum-mechanics bootstraps, are solved with primal-dual interior-point methods whose convergence guarantees rest on primal and dual Slater conditions: the existence of strictly feasible points on both sides of the duality. Many bootstrap SDPs violate these. The consequences range from slow convergence to inconclusive verdicts and the breakdown of the Skydive algorithm. We introduce "Slater tools", all acting within the PMP class accepted by SDPB. Diagnostics measure the Slater margins and, when a margin vanishes, extract the exposing certificate that identifies the degeneracy. Two independent cures follow. Transforms that provide rigorous bounds on the original SDP objective and that for the right parameter choices return the exact value. Facial reductions that use the exposing certificate to remove the degeneracies themselves. We reproduce, explain and resolve all known types of failures in a mixed-correlator 3d Ising setup. We show that the $X$-Slater condition (SDPB's primal) fails at every disallowed point in feasibility mode, holds in every navigator problem we tested, and can be guaranteed by one additional condition on the navigator function. We show that the folklore "instability remedy" of a twist gap works by removing degeneracies at the endpoints of the positivity conditions. These often stem from a mistake in factoring out the block prefactor but can also arise legitimately in channels that mix equal and unequal operator pairs. Finally we classify and remove all degeneracies of the published current-scalar bootstrap, among them one at $x=\infty$, thus incurable by any gap assumption, caused by unequal polynomial degrees within one matrix block.

</details>


### [95] [The running of Weyl gauged gravity: asymptotic freedom, its obstruction, and a way out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40769)
*Diego Buccio,Fabrizio Chicconi,Luca Parente,Omar Zanusso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Weyl共形几何（Weyl conformal geometry）中计算了二次引力（quadratic gravity）的单圈贝塔函数（one-loop beta functions），指出Weyl张量耦合与Weyl规范耦合渐近自由，但$\widehat{R}^2$耦合在物理区域内无固定点，构成紫外完备性的障碍；足够大的Weyl不变四导数标量耦合可消除该障碍并给出全方向紫外吸引的固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于检验以Weyl共形几何表述的二次引力是否可实现紫外完备（ultraviolet completion），即其耦合流是否存在物理上可接受的固定点（fixed point）。

Method: 采用背景场方法（background field method）以图解方式提取发散，并针对规范群（gauge group）的半直积结构（semidirect product structure）设计相应的规范固定（gauge fixing）程序，从而计算单圈贝塔函数。

Result: Weyl张量耦合（Weyl-tensor coupling）与Weyl规范耦合（Weyl gauge coupling）渐近自由（asymptotically free），后者跑动独立于其他耦合及任何二阶导数物质内容；障碍来自Weyl协变曲率标量平方$\widehat{R}^2$，其耦合流在物理区域无固定点，唯一实固定点要求解耦的Weyl玻色子与快子标量模（tachyonic scalar mode）。

Conclusion: 该障碍在包含标准模型（Standard Model）任意非最小Higgs耦合及一般二阶导数动能物质时依然存在；但具有足够大$\widehat{R}^2$耦合的Weyl不变四导数标量可将其移除，产生物理区域内全方向紫外吸引且Weyl规范耦合非零的固定点。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop beta functions of quadratic gravity in Weyl conformal geometry, the gauge theory of diffeomorphisms and dilatations with a dynamical Weyl gauge boson. The divergences are extracted diagrammatically in the background field method, with a gauge fixing procedure adapted to the semidirect product structure of the gauge group. The Weyl-tensor coupling and the Weyl gauge coupling are asymptotically free, and the running of the latter is special, being independent of the other couplings and of any two-derivative matter content. The obstruction to an ultraviolet completion comes from the square of a Weyl-covariant curvature scalar, denoted $\widehat{R}{}^2$: the flow of the corresponding coupling has no fixed point in the physically interesting region and becomes trivial, the only real fixed points require a decoupled Weyl boson and a tachyonic scalar mode. This also holds when including the Standard Model with any nonminimal Higgs coupling and, more generally, for any matter with two-derivative kinetic terms. Weyl-invariant four-derivative scalars with a sufficiently large coupling to $\widehat{R}{}^2$ can remove the obstruction, producing a fixed point in the physical region that is ultraviolet attractive in all directions and has a nonvanishing Weyl gauge coupling.

</details>


### [96] [DiscKosky: Efficient Genealogical Constraints from Sector-By-Sector Critical Point Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40772)
*Giulio Crisanti,Luke Lippstreu,Andrew J. McLeod,Maria Polackova*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: DiscKosky 是一个新的 Mathematica 程序包，通过追踪费曼积分临界点方程的解来生成关于顺序不连续性的谱系约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效判定费曼积分（Feynman integral）的顺序不连续性（sequential discontinuity）对理解其解析结构很重要，而现有方法需要自动化工具支持；本文旨在提供一个可系统生成谱系约束（genealogical constraints）的程序包。

Method: DiscKosky 追踪与费曼积分相关的临界点方程（critical point equations）的解，识别在给定运动学极限下哪些临界点不再存在，从而将每个朗道奇点（Landau singularity）与一组在壳扇区（on-shell sectors）关联；再依据层级原理（hierarchical principle）排除那些与不相容在壳传播子集合相关联的顺序不连续性。

Result: 在少数情形下，算法因出现高维临界簇（higher-dimensional critical varieties）而无法给出预测，但该现象有时可用于识别最多只能计算一次的非重复不连续性（non-repeating discontinuities）。该程序包被应用于广泛类别的费曼积分，包括带一条和两条大质量外线的两圈五点积分，以及超出当前计算前沿的三圈示例。

Conclusion: DiscKosky 可有效生成费曼积分的顺序不连续性约束，并进一步适用于更一般的欧拉积分（Euler integrals）类，如宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）中出现的若干示例所示。

Abstract: We present DiscKosky, a new Mathematica package for efficiently generating genealogical constraints on the sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals. DiscKosky works by tracking the solutions to the critical point equations associated with a Feynman integral; by identifying which of these critical points cease to exist in a given kinematic limit, we can associate each Landau singularity with a set of on-shell sectors. The hierarchical principle then forbids sequential discontinuities with respect to singularities that are associated with incompatible sets of on-shell propagators. In rare cases, this algorithm fails to make predictions due to the appearance of higher-dimensional critical varieties---nevertheless, we show that this phenomenon can sometimes be used to identify non-repeating discontinuities, which can be computed at most once in a Feynman integral. We illustrate the power of DiscKosky by applying the package to a broad class of Feynman integrals, including two-loop five-point integrals with one and two massive legs, and three-loop examples that go beyond the current computational frontier. DiscKosky can also be used to probe the analytic properties of more general classes of Euler integrals, which we illustrate by considering several examples that arise in the context of cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [97] [Giant Gravitons in AdS$_5\times$SE$_5$ and the Superconformal Index of Toric Quiver Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40773)
*Evan Deddo,Sabarenath Jayaprakash,James T. Liu,Leopoldo A. Pando Zayas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过量子化 $\mathrm{AdS}_5\times SE_5$ 背景中的探针 D3-膜，直接导出巨引力子对超共形指数（superconformal index）的贡献，并在 $T^{1,1}$ 与 $Y^{p,q}$ 情形下与对偶环面四元规范理论的预测相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称微态计数传统上依赖对偶场论完成，本文旨在探索在超引力一侧直接由 D3-膜自由度恢复巨引力子展开（giant-graviton expansion）项的内在引力框架。

Method: 对 $\mathrm{AdS}_5\times SE_5$（$SE_5=T^{1,1},Y^{p,q}$）背景中的最大巨引力子（maximal giant graviton）探针 D3-膜进行量子化，确定其标量、矢量与费米子涨落并提取 BPS 谱（BPS spectra），进而得到单字母指数（single-letter index）。

Result: 所得单字母指数与对偶环面四元规范理论（toric quiver gauge theory）的巨引力子展开预测一致，表明有限 $N$ 超对称计数函数中的单巨引力子项可由量子化 D3-膜自由度直接恢复。

Conclusion: 结果指向巨引力子展开在超引力一侧具有普适的世界体（worldvolume）起源，并被视为构建类似开-闭-开对偶（open-closed-open triality）、以引力为本体的框架来处理超对称微态计数问题的一步。

Abstract: We derive giant-graviton contributions to superconformal indices by quantizing probe D3-branes in type IIB backgrounds $\mathrm{AdS}_{5}\times SE_{5}$. For $SE_{5}=T^{1,1}$ and $Y^{p,q}$, we determine the scalar, vector, and fermionic fluctuations of maximal giant gravitons and extract their BPS spectra. The resulting single-letter indices agree with those predicted by the giant-graviton expansions of the dual toric quiver gauge theories. These results show that the single-giant terms in finite-$N$ supersymmetric counting functions can be recovered directly from quantized D3-brane degrees of freedom and point toward a universal worldvolume origin of giant-graviton expansions directly on the supergravity side. We view the results as a step in an intrinsically gravitational framework, much like the open-closed-open triality, for addressing supersymmetric microstate-counting problems that are conventionally outsourced to the dual field theory.

</details>


### [98] [On Boundary State Screening via Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40777)
*Vivek Chakrabhavi,Babak Haghighat,Jonathan J. Heckman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出基于孤子/扭结剖面诱导的边界态跃迁，对大质量量子场论边界数据进行粗分类，并引入“边界屏蔽群（boundary screening group）𝔹”，其可计算目标空间缺陷群（defect group）的世界面类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量量子场论（massive QFT）的边界数据缺乏粗粒化的分类框架；同时希望建立世界面（worldsheet）与目标空间（target space）缺陷结构之间的对应，并刻画界面/结（interface/junction）能否完全消失的问题。

Method: 从边界态的格（lattice of boundary states）出发，以孤子/扭结剖面（soliton/kink profiles）所实现的边界态跃迁定义等价关系，取其等价类构成边界屏蔽群 𝔹；在几何上实现相互作用 QFT 的弦论背景中，将 𝔹 解释为目标空间缺陷群 𝔻 的世界面类比；对界面情形分析孤子吸收/透射所对应的守恒荷，并将结论推广到 QFT 的结及弦世界面 CFT 的结。

Result: 𝔹 非零时可作为目标空间存在非正规化模式（non-normalizable modes）的诊断判据；界面情形下存在与孤子吸收/透射相关的非平凡守恒荷，意味着界面不能完全溶解/消失，该结论对结亦成立，并在弦世界面 CFT 情形下译为弦论之间的结。文中以若干非几何二维 𝒩=(2,2) Landau-Ginzburg 模型及其自然提升到四维 𝒩=1 Wess-Zumino 模型为例予以说明。

Conclusion: 边界屏蔽群 𝔹 提供了大质量 QFT 边界数据的粗分类工具，并与目标空间缺陷群、非正规化模式及界面的不可消解性相联系；该框架在非几何 Landau-Ginzburg 与 Wess-Zumino 模型中得到示例性验证。

Abstract: We present a coarse classification scheme for the boundary data of massive QFTs based on transitions of boundary states via soliton / kink profiles. Starting from the lattice of boundary states, we introduce the boundary screening group $\mathbb{B}$: the equivalence classes of boundary states which cannot be connected by such transitions. In string theory backgrounds which geometrically engineer an interacting QFT in the target space, $\mathbb{B}$ computes the worldsheet analog of the target space defect group $\mathbb{D}$. When $\mathbb{B}$ is non-zero, it provides a diagnostic for having a target space with non-normalizable modes. For interfaces between QFTs, similar considerations imply the existence of a non-trivial conserved charge associated with soliton absorption / transmission, which means that the interface cannot fully dissolve / disappear. Similar considerations hold for junctions of QFTs, which in the special case of string worldsheet CFTs translate to junctions of string theories. We illustrate these general considerations with a number of non-geometric 2D $\mathcal{N} = (2,2)$ Landau-Ginzburg models, as well as their natural lifts to 4D $\mathcal{N} = 1$ Wess-Zumino models.

</details>


### [99] [Conformal boundaries and the photon sphere of AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40778)
*Raúl Arias,Valentino Di Pierro,Silvia Georgescu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究带共形边界条件的四维Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的线性化引力扰动，证明在光子球（全脐边界）处问题可精确分离，并对二阶及以上球谐模证明无指数增长模式，同时追踪近视界Rindler流体模向外过渡为渐近AdS₄引力准正规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力扰动研究多采用Dirichlet等固定边界条件，而有限时间类边界上的共形边界条件允许边界Weyl模涨落，其对稳定性的影响尚不清晰；光子球半径处边界的全脐性为分离问题提供了特殊几何契机。

Method: 在共形边界条件（固定诱导度规的共形类与平均曲率，允许边界Weyl模涨落）下分析线性化引力扰动，利用光子球处静态边界的全脐性质将标量问题精确分解为体（bulk）与边界（boundary）两个独立扇区；结合解析证明与数值追踪流体模随边界外移的演化，并考察AdS₄黑膜（black brane）情形。

Result: 对二阶及以上球谐，标量与矢量模均无指数增长；大角动量不稳定性随边界趋近全脐性而被抑制。近视界处恢复普适Rindler谱（含声（sound）与剪切（shear）激发），数值上这些流体模在边界外移至渐近AdS₄边界时趋近标准引力准正规模；对大黑洞，其渐近端点对应对偶三维共形场论（CFT）的声与剪切极点。黑膜情形下亦发现流体模的类似延拓，但无中间全脐边界。

Conclusion: 共形边界条件下Schwarzschild-AdS₄黑洞在光子球全脐边界处具有可分离结构与稳定性，流体模与渐近准正规模之间存在连续过渡，并在大黑洞极限下与对偶三维CFT的输运极点相对应，为边界条件与全息流体描述的联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of four-dimensional Schwarzschild-AdS black holes with finite timelike boundaries subject to conformal boundary conditions. These fix the conformal class of the induced metric and the mean curvature, while allowing the boundary Weyl mode to fluctuate. The photon sphere plays a distinguished role in this problem: a static boundary at this radius is totally umbilic, and the scalar problem separates exactly into independent bulk and boundary sectors. We prove the absence of exponentially growing scalar and vector modes for spherical harmonics of degree two and higher. We determine how large angular momentum instabilities are suppressed as the boundary approaches umbilicity. Near the horizon, we recover the universal Rindler spectrum, including sound and shear excitations. We numerically follow these fluid modes as the boundary is moved outwards and find that they approach standard gravitational quasinormal modes at the asymptotic AdS$_4$ boundary. For large black holes, these asymptotic endpoints correspond to the sound and shear poles of the dual three-dimensional conformal field theory. We also study perturbations of the AdS$_4$ black brane, where we find an analogous continuation of the fluid modes in the absence of an intermediate umbilic boundary.

</details>


### [100] [Monopole-Fermion Scattering from Twisted Conformal Boundary States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40779)
*Clay Córdova,Diego García-Sepúlveda,Lian-Tao Wang,Yen-Chieh Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 从扭曲扇区的共形边界态出发推导单极子-费米子散射关联函数，证明Callan-Rubakov边界条件给出模为1的入/出射两点函数，并补全s波幺正性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷守恒要求Callan-Rubakov效应中存在出射激发，其关联函数在普通局域费米子层面会消失，需借助拓扑对称线扭曲的边界态框架加以刻画。

Method: 构造N个自由Dirac费米子在U(1)^N保持边界条件下的扭曲边界态，将电荷格描述推广为移位格（shifted lattices）；利用集团分解（cluster decomposition）与开-闭对偶（open-closed duality）确定系数并相对未扭曲边界态固定归一化，再经规范协变渐近态联系到四维分波散射。

Result: Callan-Rubakov边界条件给出入射费米子与出射缺陷算符（defect operator）间模为1的两点函数，即便对应普通局域费米子关联函数为零；并识别出幺正性关系中缺失的s波贡献。

Conclusion: 扭曲通道（twisted channel）以恰好所需的归一化提供缺失的s波贡献，表明拓扑对称线扭曲扇区是理解单极子催化散射幺正性的关键要素。

Abstract: We derive monopole-fermion scattering correlators from conformal boundary states in twisted sectors. The key ingredient is that symmetry-preserving boundary conditions contain sectors twisted by topological symmetry lines. Their overlaps determine correlators of operators attached to these lines, including the outgoing excitations required by charge conservation in the Callan-Rubakov effect. We construct the twisted boundary states of $N$ free Dirac fermions with $U(1)^N$-preserving boundary conditions, extending the familiar description in terms of charge lattices to shifted lattices describing twisted sectors. Using cluster decomposition and open-closed duality, we determine their coefficients and fix their normalization relative to the untwisted boundary state. We find that the Callan-Rubakov boundary condition yields a two-point function between an incoming fermion and an outgoing defect operator with a coefficient of unit modulus, even when the corresponding correlators of ordinary local fermions vanish. Finally, we relate these correlators to four-dimensional partial-wave scattering using gauge-covariant asymptotic states. We identify the missing $s$-wave contribution to the unitarity relation and argue that the twisted channel supplies it with precisely the required normalization.

</details>


### [101] [Umbilic Observatories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40783)
*Dionysios Anninos,Raúl Arias,Damián A Galante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本工作以嵌入四维闵可夫斯基时空的三维de Sitter超曲面作为脐点边界（umbilic boundary）的最简范例，研究其引力动力学，发现共形与Dirichlet两种边界条件下均不存在虚频随角动量增长的线性模式，脐点条件使边界动力学线性稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在类时边界（timelike boundary）的情形下定义引力动力学一直存在困难，例如渐近闵可夫斯基时空的空间无穷（spatial infinity）结构；脐点超曲面（诱导度规与外在曲率成比例）可能提供更优的出发设定，故需检验其线性稳定性。

Method: 在四维广义相对论（general relativity）中考察嵌入四维闵可夫斯基世界的三维de Sitter超曲面，分别施加共形边界条件（conformal boundary conditions）与Dirichlet边界条件，分析线性化模式的频率随角动量的行为，并与空间无穷的结构作类比。

Result: 两类边界条件下均未发现虚部频率随角动量增长的线性模式。共形边界条件除体激发（bulk excitations）外还允许动力学边界自由度；脐点性质使边界Weyl模与体动力学线性解耦，动力学线性稳定。Dirichlet问题则不存在边界模。

Conclusion: 脐点超曲面因线性稳定并且能自然容纳边界自由度，可能成为在类时边界存在时定义引力动力学的改进出发点；分析同时建立了与渐近闵可夫斯基时空空间无穷结构及其有限化实现的对应关系。

Abstract: We study gravitational dynamics for the simplest example of an umbilic boundary in four-dimensional general relativity --- a three-dimensional de Sitter hypersurface embedded in a four-dimensional Minkowski world. Generally speaking, an umbilic surface has the property that its induced metric is proportional to its extrinsic curvature. We consider the case of both conformal boundary conditions, as well as Dirichlet boundary conditions imposed at the timelike hypersurface. For both cases, we find that there are no linearised modes whose imaginary frequency grows with angular momentum. The conformal boundary conditions allow for a dynamical boundary degree of freedom, in addition to the bulk excitations. The umbilic property linearly decouples the boundary Weyl mode from the bulk dynamics, yielding linearly stable dynamics. In contrast, the Dirichlet problem has no boundary mode. The umbilic hypersurfaces may thus constitute an improved starting point for defining gravitational dynamics in the presence of a timelike boundary. Throughout our analysis, we draw parallels to the structure of spatial infinity for asymptotically Minkowski spacetimes and a more finite realisation thereof.

</details>


### [102] [All Symmetries and Boundaries of the Two-Dimensional Free Scalar from Continuous Flat-Gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40818)
*Yichul Choi,Ho Tat Lam,Brandon C. Rayhaun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出二维共形场论的“远程可探测性判据”，据此完整分类了SU(2)_1 WZW模型与非紧致玻色子理论的幺正共形边界和幺正拓扑线缺陷，并发现自由标量场存在4维拓扑线缺陷模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立判别工具，用以说明已知边界与线缺陷模空间是否已穷尽二维共形场论中所有幺正共形边界（conformal boundary）和幺正拓扑线缺陷（topological line defect）。

Method: 提出远程可探测性判据（remote detectability criterion）：左右移动Virasoro代数乘积之外的任何局部算符均可被某拓扑线缺陷探测；结合与共形网（conformal net）文献结果的对照，并利用对U(1)对称性做“平坦规范化（flat-gauging）”从SU(2)_1得到非紧致玻色子理论。

Result: 论证SU(2)_1 WZW模型的SU(2)边界模空间与SO(4)拓扑线缺陷模空间已完备；自由标量场拓扑线缺陷存在4维模空间（多为此前未知），并给出全部边界与线的拉格朗日描述、边界态、区间Hilbert空间、圆环配分函数的开闭对偶验证及融合规则等。

Conclusion: 所提判据支持上述分类的完备性，并通过显式构造与配分函数检验予以佐证，从而扩展了对二维共形场论边界与拓扑线缺陷的认识。

Abstract: We propose a remote detectability criterion for two-dimensional conformal field theories, which asserts that any local operator outside of the product of the left- and right-moving Virasoro algebras can be detected by some topological line defect. We discuss its relationship to existing results in the conformal net literature. We then use this criterion to argue that the known $SU(2)$ moduli space of unitary conformal boundary conditions and the $SO(4)$ moduli space of unitary topological line defects of the $SU(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten model completely capture all unitary conformal boundaries and unitary topological lines of the theory. Invoking the fact that the non-compact boson conformal field theory can be obtained from $SU(2)_1$ by "flat-gauging" a $U(1)$ symmetry, we are also able to completely classify the unitary conformal boundaries and unitary topological line defects of the two-dimensional free scalar field. In particular, we find a 4-dimensional moduli space of topological line defects, most of which were not known previously. We support our proposed classifications by writing down concrete Lagrangian descriptions of all of the boundaries and lines of the non-compact boson. For every boundary condition, we calculate the corresponding boundary state, and for each pair of boundary conditions, we calculate the corresponding interval Hilbert space. We verify that the annulus partition functions of the theory respect open/closed duality. We also calculate the fusion rules of the topological line defects, their action on local operators, their action on the boundary conditions by parallel fusion, and their corresponding twisted-sector Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [103] [Proof of the entropic $c$-theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40874)
*Jonah Kudler-Flam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在二维量子场论中利用类空分离楔形区域间的互信息构造 c-函数（c-function），并借助相对熵（relative entropy）证明熵的 c-定理（entropic c-theorem），全程无需引入截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为c-定理提供无需截断、量均有限且仅依赖代数量子场论（algebraic quantum field theory）标准公理的证明，从而改进 Casini–Huerta 原有证明中依赖纠缠熵强次可加性（strong subadditivity）的做法。

Method: 通过相对熵定义类空分离楔形区域间的互信息（mutual information），由之构造 c-函数；证明思路沿袭 Casini 与 Huerta，但以互信息差分的单调递增替代纠缠熵的强次可加性，并在连续统中直接由分裂性质（split property）与相对熵的标准性质加以证明。

Result: 确立了互信息差分的单调性，从而得到 c-定理；该证明的假设仅为代数量子场论标准公理、跨能隙有限互信息的分裂性质（由模 p-核性（modular p-nuclearity）蕴含）以及所得 c-函数的有限紫外界（ultraviolet bound）。

Conclusion: 相对熵方法的优势在于所有量均有限、无需任何截断；此外，形式上 c-函数正比于某一态的最小平均零能量（minimal averaged null energy），该态复现两楔形区域间的真空关联（vacuum correlations）。

Abstract: We prove an entropic $c$-theorem in two dimensions by constructing a $c$-function from the mutual information between spacelike separated wedges, defined through the relative entropy. The proof follows Casini and Huerta's, with strong subadditivity of entanglement entropy replaced by increasing differences of the mutual information, which we prove directly in the continuum from the split property and standard properties of relative entropy. The benefit of relative entropy is that every quantity we use is finite and no cutoff ever needs to be introduced. The assumptions of the theorem are only the standard axioms of algebraic quantum field theory, the split property with finite mutual information across a gap (both implied by modular $p$-nuclearity), and a finite ultraviolet bound on the resulting $c$-functions. We also note that, formally, the $c$-function is proportional to the minimal averaged null energy of a state that reproduces the vacuum correlations between the two wedges.

</details>


### [104] [Hilbert space in the flat limit of AdS/CFT. II. Massless particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40878)
*Kevin Nguyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作为系列Part I的续作，本文证明固定维度Δ、任意整数自旋s的共形群酉最低权表示D(Δ,s)经由Inönü–Wigner收缩与无穷大推动（infinite boost）的联合操作，可收缩为无质量庞加莱粒子（massless Poincaré particle）表示，并在Carroll基下自然浮现无质量散射态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Part I已描述有质量散射态（massive scattering states）的涌现，本文意在完成对应的一半：说明共形表示的同一框架如何产生无质量粒子表示，从而统一理解共形对称性向庞加莱对称性收缩时的有质量与无质量谱。

Method: 将李代数收缩so(2,3)→iso(1,3)与无穷大推动相结合（infinite boost），考察酉最低权表示D(Δ,s)在Carroll基（carrollian basis）下的极限行为，并逐情形分析内积（inner product）与零态（null states）的收敛性质。

Result: 当Δ>s+1时，D(Δ,s)收缩为所有自旋s'≤s的无质量粒子直和；在幺正界（unitarity bound）Δ=s+1处，低自旋态变为零态，D(s+1,s)收缩为携带两个最大螺旋度J=±s的单一无质量粒子表示。两种情形下内积仅需一次与螺旋度无关的重标度（rescaling）即收敛到庞加莱不变内积。

Conclusion: 整个收缩过程保持幺正性（unitarity），表明共形最低权表示与无质量庞加莱粒子表示之间存在连续、幺正且螺旋度结构清晰的对应关系，为共形场论（conformal field theory）谱与无质量散射的关联提供了代数层面的依据。

Abstract: Building on Part I of this series, where we described the emergence of massive scattering states, we demonstrate how unitary lowest-weight representations $\mathcal D(Δ,s)$ of the conformal group with fixed dimension $Δ$ and arbitrary integer spin $s$ contract to massless Poincaré particle representations. This requires combining the Inönü--Wigner contraction $\mathfrak{so}(2,3) \to \mathfrak{iso}(1,3)$ with an infinite boost, in which case massless scattering states naturally emerge in a carrollian basis. For $Δ>s+1$, we find that $\mathcal D(Δ,s)$ contracts to the direct sum of massless particles of all spins $s'\leq s$. At the unitarity bound, lower-spin states become null so that $\mathcal D(s+1,s)$ contracts to a single massless particle representation carrying the two maximal helicities $J=\pm s$. In both cases, the inner product converges to the Poincaré-invariant one after a single helicity-independent rescaling, so that unitarity is preserved throughout the contraction.

</details>


### [105] [Facets of Gravity: A journey exploring spacetime from Planckian to cosmological scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40908)
*Agustín Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文围绕“量子化引力（quantum gravity）意味着什么、量子时空理论能预测什么”这一问题，采用三种互补的非微扰（non-perturbative）路径——因果动力学三角剖分、泛函重整化群与渐近安全、纯仿射引力的格点统计方法——发展刻画量子时空几何与连续极限的观测量与计算工具，并尝试将基础引力理论与可观测宇宙学相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在回应量子引力领域的核心概念问题：量子化引力的含义及其可预言内容。由于强涨落几何中常规微分几何工具失效、紫外临界面难以刻画、度规是否应作为基本变量亦存疑，需要发展非微扰且互补的研究手段。

Method: 在因果动力学三角剖分（Causal Dynamical Triangulations）中构造观测量以研究有效拓扑、统计均匀性与曲率，并在二维模型中检验粗粒化（coarse-graining）行为；在泛函重整化群（Functional Renormalization Group）与渐近安全（Asymptotic Safety）框架下发展流不变量并引入流不变量神经网络（Flow Invariant Neural Networks）重建紫外临界面，同时研究标量-张量引力中紫外一致性对红外相互作用的约束；此外提出纯仿射引力（purely affine gravity）的格点统计方法，以联络为基本变量、度规为演生量，并用蒙特卡罗模拟与四维均匀各向同性构造进行探索。

Result: 二维模型测试揭示了不同的粗粒化行为，并提供探测熟悉几何性质演生的新途径；流不变量方法可刻画紫外临界面，且在标量-张量引力中得到与宇宙学观测相容的暴胀（inflation）情形；二维纯仿射引力的蒙特卡罗模拟给出探索性结果，四维均匀各向同性构造则提供类德西特（de Sitter）几何的初步证据，并支持曲率关联的研究。

Conclusion: 论文通过多种互补路径为研究量子时空、检验其可能的连续极限（continuum behaviour）并将基础引力理论与可观测物理相联系提供了工具，同时指出了仍存在的概念性与计算性挑战。

Abstract: What does it mean to quantize gravity, and what can a quantum theory of spacetime actually predict? This thesis explores these questions through complementary non-perturbative approaches, from the geometry of quantum spacetime to the emergence of cosmological physics.
  Within Causal Dynamical Triangulations, we develop observables to investigate effective topology, statistical homogeneity, and curvature in strongly fluctuating geometries, where conventional differential-geometric tools are unavailable. Tests in two-dimensional models reveal distinct coarse-graining behaviours and provide new ways to probe the emergence of familiar geometric properties.
  We then turn to the Functional Renormalization Group and Asymptotic Safety, developing flow invariants to characterize ultraviolet-critical surfaces and introducing Flow Invariant Neural Networks as a computational method for reconstructing them. In scalar-tensor gravity, we investigate how ultraviolet consistency conditions can constrain infrared interactions, obtaining an inflationary scenario compatible with cosmological observations.
  Finally, we question whether the metric must be the fundamental variable of quantum gravity. We formulate a lattice statistical approach to purely affine gravity, in which the connection is fundamental and the metric, when it exists, is emergent. Monte Carlo simulations provide exploratory results in two dimensions, while a four-dimensional homogeneous and isotropic construction yields preliminary evidence for de Sitter-like geometry and allows the study of curvature correlations.
  Together, these investigations develop complementary tools for studying quantum spacetime, testing its possible continuum behaviour, and connecting fundamental gravitational theories with observable physics, while identifying the conceptual and computational challenges that remain.

</details>


### [106] [$h/2e$ Flux Trapping without Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41058)
*Will Lancer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文为普通金属（ordinary metal）中阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆磁通俘获（Aharonov-Bohm flux trapping）现象提供解析解释，并说明 h/2e 磁通俘获无需凝聚体或配对即可出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Komargodski 与 Popov 近期发现，穿有阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆螺线管（Aharonov-Bohm solenoid）的普通金属仅俘获 0 或 h/2e 磁通；h/2e 通常被视为超导（superconducting）特征量，其在无凝聚体的普通金属中出现，构成需要解释的反常现象。

Method: 采用解析方法（analytic approach）分析磁通俘获机制，并将结论推广至有限温度（finite temperature）、时间反演不变势（time-reversal invariant potential）以及格点正则化（lattice regularization）等情形。

Result: 解析分析复现了 0 与 h/2e 的磁通俘获值；在有限温度、时间反演不变势与格点正则化下，磁通仍被束缚于 0 和 h/2e。

Conclusion: h/2e 磁通俘获并非超导专属，普通金属在不含凝聚体或配对（pairing）的条件下亦可呈现；该结论对温度、势的对称性及格点实现具有稳健性。

Abstract: Recently, it was shown by Komargodski and Popov [arXiv:2601.09847] that ordinary metals threaded with an Aharonov-Bohm solenoid exclusively trap either 0 or h/2e flux. The latter value is familiar in the superconducting setting, yet we find that it holds in ordinary metals as well, with no condensate or pairing necessary. We provide an analytic explanation for the flux trapping, and extend the results to the cases of finite temperature, time-reversal invariant potentials, and lattice regularization, where we find that the flux remains trapped at 0 and h/2e.

</details>


### [107] [Induced Lorentzian geometry from the fermionic quantum metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41138)
*Patrick Copinger,Pablo Morales*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明背景电磁场中狄拉克费米子的量子度规（quantum metric）可在位形空间诱导出有效洛伦兹度规（Lorentzian metric），其扰动由量子度规与背景场强决定，并导出描述标量粒子在诱导量子几何中运动的有效理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）通常定义于参数空间或动量空间，与位形空间几何无必然联系；本文旨在探究费米子量子度规能否生成位形空间中的几何结构。

Method: 采用洛伦兹协变相空间世界线路径积分（worldline path integral）构造，对自旋自由度应用绝热定理（adiabatic theorem），在世界线作用量中于 $O(\hbar)$ 得到贝里相位（Berry phase）、于 $O(\hbar^2)$ 得到量子度规；并在 3+1 与 2+1 维考察费曼及推迟/超前传播子（Feynman and retarded/advanced propagators），涉及 QED/QED$_3$ 与凝聚态狄拉克理论。

Result: 对动量自由度积分后得到有效理论，在位形空间中产生涌现的洛伦兹度规，其相对平直度规的扰动依赖于量子度规与背景场强；除编码于贝里相位的自旋自由度外，有效理论描述标量粒子在诱导量子几何中的运动。

Conclusion: 量子度规可视为位形空间诱导几何的来源；并借助带隙三维狄拉克材料中的谱完整群（spectral holonomy）探讨了该协变几何的唯象应用。

Abstract: Quantum geometry is conventionally understood in parameter or momentum space and need not be related to a geometry in configuration space. Here we show for Dirac fermions in a background electromagnetic field that an induced Lorentzian geometry is generated from the fermionic quantum metric. We study a Lorentz covariant phase space worldline path integral construction, whose application of the adiabatic theorem onto spin degrees of freedom leads to the appearance of the Berry phase at $O(\hbar)$, and the quantum metric at $O(\hbar^2)$ in the worldline action. Both the Feynman and retarded/advanced propagators are examined in 3+1 and 2+1 dimensions, whose different on-shell implementations lead to related quantum metrics; QED/QED${}_3$ and condensed matter Dirac theory are considered. An effective theory is found by integrating out the momenta degrees of freedom, which gives rise to an emergent Lorentzian metric in configuration space, whose perturbation to the flat metric is dependent on the quantum metric and the background field strength. Apart from the spin degrees of freedom encoded into the Berry phase, the effective theory describes a scalar particle moving in the induced quantum geometry. Phenomenological applications to the covariant geometry are further studied using spectral holonomy in a gapped 3-dimensional Dirac material.

</details>


### [108] [Reconstructing Meson Parton Distributions from Mellin Moments with Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41189)
*Pietro Dall'Olio,Cristian Del Gobbo,Khépani Raya,José Rodríguez-Quintero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将介子部分子分布函数（PDF）/分布振幅（PDA）的重建视为病态逆问题，通过第一性原理硬约束与可微惩罚正则化，并以MLP与KAN两种神经核参数化对比，发现基于B样条的KAN重建更精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定能标下由有限个Mellin矩（Mellin moments）反演介子PDF/PDA在Hadamard意义上是病态的：矩不能唯一、稳定地确定逐点的x依赖，数据小扰动会导致重建结果大幅变化。

Method: 以第一性原理支持的约束（支撑区间x∈[0,1]、端点消失、单位归一化、π介子的精确x↔1−x对称性）限制可容许函数类，并辅以可微惩罚（differentiable penalties）抑制伪多峰与中心凹性变化；未知函数分别用多层感知机（MLP）与Kolmogorov–Arnold网络（KAN）参数化。

Result: KAN重建目标分布更准确，在非对称的K介子情形尤为显著；该优势归因于KAN所依赖的B样条基（B-spline basis）的局部性，可在矩匹配中局域化修正并抑制MLP易产生的全局振荡；使用十个和五个输入矩的合成目标基准测试均支持该结论。

Conclusion: 将硬约束与可微惩罚结合的正则化策略可有效处理Mellin矩反演的病态性；相比MLP，具有局部基的KAN是更优的参数化选择，尤其适用于非对称分布的重建。

Abstract: We address the inverse problem of reconstructing a meson parton distribution function (PDF) or parton distribution amplitude (PDA) at a fixed scale from a finite set of Mellin moments. At finite moment order this map is ill posed in the Hadamard sense: the moments do not determine a unique, stable pointwise $x$-dependence, and small data perturbations can produce large changes in the reconstruction. We regularize the problem by restricting the admissible function class through constraints justified by first principles---support on $x\in[0,1]$, endpoint vanishing, unit normalization, and, for the pion, the exact $x\leftrightarrow 1-x$ symmetry---and by complementing these hard constraints with differentiable penalties that suppress the spurious multiple maxima and central concavity changes typical of ill-posed inversions. We parameterize the unknown function with two interchangeable neural cores, a multilayer perceptron (MLP) and a Kolmogorov--Arnold network (KAN), and find that the KAN reconstructs the target distributions more accurately, most markedly in the asymmetric (kaon) case. We attribute this advantage to the locality of the B-spline basis underlying the KAN, which localizes corrections during moment matching and damps the global oscillations to which the MLP is prone. Benchmarks against synthetic targets, using both ten and five input moments, support these conclusions.

</details>


### [109] [Longitudinal electric corner modes in continuum quantum gauge theory with matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41240)
*Goncalo Araujo-Regado,Philipp A. Hoehn,Alok Laddha,Bilyana Tomova*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究连续量子场论中区域边界上的角模式（corner modes），为标量量子电动力学（scalar QED）的区域柯西片代数提出一种新的角算符，并构造其微扰GNS态以完成量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论（gauge theories）的子区域会产生边界上的边缘模式（edge modes）与角荷（corner charges），这在经典情形与量子格点规范理论中已较清楚；但在连续量子场论中，角模式因具有分布性质（distributional nature）而命运不明，尚缺乏理解。

Method: 在闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中，考察由标量QED的局部规范不变可观测量生成的区域柯西片代数（regional Cauchy slice algebra）；提出新角算符，其由子区域外物质源出的纵向电场（longitudinal electric field）给出，从而构成区域代数的中心（centre）。将Fock真空拉回到该片，并通过重整化（renormalising）因缺乏时间抹平（temporal smearing）而产生的发散，构造微扰GNS态。

Result: 存在一个微扰GNS态可在该代数上量子化此模式；引入新中心模式要求在区域角点（corner）进行进一步重整化，从而在物理上区分该角点并破坏态的平移不变性（translation invariance）。

Conclusion: 局域性（locality）、可重整性（renormalisability）与正定性（positivity）的要求共同对支持角模式作为真实量子算符的连续量子场论施加了强约束；文末还讨论了区域代数的冯·诺依曼完备化（von Neumann completion）及重整化真空态的可能物理解释。

Abstract: Subregions in gauge theories give rise to edge modes and corner charges supported on their boundaries. While this is well-established classically and in quantum lattice gauge theories, the fate of corner modes remains poorly understood in continuum quantum field theories owing to their distributional nature. In this paper, we revisit this challenge and propose a new corner operator for a regional Cauchy slice algebra generated by local gauge-invariant observables of scalar QED in Minkowski spacetime. The corner mode is given by the longitudinal electric field sourced by the matter outside the subregion and thus constitutes a centre for the regional algebra. Our main goal is to show that there exists a perturbative GNS state on this algebra that quantises this mode. Pulling back the Fock vacuum to the slice, we construct this state by renormalising the divergences arising from the lack of temporal smearing. We clarify that the inclusion of the new centre mode necessitates a further renormalisation on the corner of the region, thereby physically distinguishing it and breaking the translation invariance of the state. Our work shows how the requirements of locality, renormalisability, and positivity together put strong constraints on the existence of a continuum quantum field theory which supports corner modes as genuine quantum operators. We finish with comments about the von Neumann completion of the regional algebra, as well as possible physical interpretations of the renormalised vacuum state.

</details>


### [110] [Gravitational Memory from AdS States in the Flat-Space Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41247)
*David A. Lowe,Juanyi Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在全局 AdS$_4$ 中研究含动力学标量物质的线性化引力重构：外部标量源制备激发态并修改全息 Ward 恒等式，导出含物质修正的电四极主方程，并从推迟引力演化中提取 Bondi 剪切与 news，数值验证超平移记忆与能量再分配一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作集中于固定边界度规下的真空 Einstein 微扰，缺乏对标量物质被外源激发后引力响应及其对全息 Ward 恒等式影响的分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将体物质源响应与真空微扰分离，在 Einstein 约束要求下推导含物质依赖修正的电四极（electric quadrupole）主方程；并检验柱形边界积分（cylindrical boundary integral）逐模重现 Regge--Wheeler 主场。

Result: 柱形边界积分逐模复现 Regge--Wheeler 主场，但不等同于完整的全局 Fefferman--Graham 重构；对平空间极限下无质量的弱相互作用标量，所得超平移记忆（supertranslation memory）与出射能量再分配及边界功的四极矩数值吻合。

Conclusion: 在有序平空间极限下，后续边界控制不改变首个事件的波形，尽管在有限 AdS 半径下会抵消残余引力模，提示 AdS 曲率对记忆效应可观测性的影响。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational reconstruction in global AdS$_4$ with dynamical scalar matter, building on vacuum Einstein perturbations with fixed boundary metric and nontrivial normalizable data. External scalar sources prepare an excited state and modify the holographic Ward identities, while subsequent evolution can proceed without boundary driving. Separating the bulk matter-sourced response from vacuum perturbations, we derive an electric quadrupole master equation with the matter-dependent corrections required by the Einstein constraints. We also verify that a cylindrical boundary integral reproduces the Regge--Wheeler master field mode by mode, without identifying it with the full global Fefferman--Graham reconstruction. For weakly interacting scalars that become massless in the flat-space limit, we extract the Bondi shear and news from retarded gravitational evolution. The resulting supertranslation memory agrees numerically with the outgoing energy redistribution and the quadrupole moment of the boundary work. In the ordered flat-space limit, later boundary controls leave the first event's waveform unchanged, despite cancelling residual gravitational modes at finite AdS radius.

</details>
