### [111] [Fano Resonance of a Magnonic Ring Resonator with a Nano-Scatterer Embedded in a Magnonic Conduit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41816)
*Rajesh Kumar,Yat-Yin Au,Snorri Ingvarsson*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本研究通过微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulation）研究耦合于磁振子波导（magnonic waveguide）的纳米尺度磁振子环形谐振器（magnonic ring resonator），分别在波导中嵌入孔洞（H-NRR）与不嵌入孔洞（NRR）两种情形下，实现由洛伦兹型（Lorentzian）向法诺型（Fano）共振频谱线型（lineshape）的转变，并分析结构与材料参数对传输特性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将磁振子环形谐振器的洛伦兹型共振谱线转变为法诺型谱线，并改善原NRR系统在设计参数上的可控性与容差，为构建非常规磁振子集成电路（magnonic integrated circuits）提供基础。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟分析H-NRR与NRR的透射与反射谱（transmission and reflection spectra），考察嵌入孔直径、环形与波导间隙（gap）及阻尼因子（damping factor）等参数；并用包含前向与后向两条传播路径的双轨道解析模型（two-tracks analytical model）对模拟结果进行佐证。

Result: 模拟结果显示，H-NRR系统的谱透射幅度变化量（spectral transmission magnitude variation）对间隙与阻尼等设计参数呈单调依赖（monotonic dependence），相较原始NRR系统具有更好的工程可控性与容差。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构与研究有望用于传感（sensing）、数据处理系统与计算等非常规磁振子集成电路的构建。

Abstract: We present micromagnetic simulation of a nanoscale magnonic ring resonator coupled to a magnonic waveguide, with and without a hole embedded in the waveguide (H-NRR and NRR respectively), for realizing transformation into a Fano resonance frequency spectral lineshape from a Lorentzian one. Transmission and reflection spectra of the H-NRR and NRR have been analyzed for different structural parameters (embedded hole diameter and gap between the ring and the waveguide) and material parameters (damping factor of the ring) to achieve maximum spectral transmission magnitude variation. The obtained micromagnetic simulation results are supported by an analytical model that consists of two-tracks of wave propagation, forwards and backwards. Our findings show that H-NRR system demonstrates a monotonic dependence of spectral transmission magnitude variation against the aforementioned design parameters (gap and damping) which should provide better engineering controllability and tolerance when compared with the original NRR system. Overall, our proposed structure and study may help in building unconventional magnonic integrated circuits for sensing, data processing systems and computing.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Co--dsorption of terrylene and tetrapentacontane on hexagonal boron nitride - towards a two-dimensional Shpolskii matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40733)
*Rong Sun,Tolibjon Abdurakhmonov,Jan-Niklas Heidkamp,Julian Schröer,Paul Weinbrenner,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Tobias Korn,Oliver Kühn,Friedemann Reinhard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过在溶液相中共沉积 terrylene 荧光团与长链烷烃 tetrapentacontane，在六方氮化硼（hBN）上构建类 Shpolskii 超薄二维主客体基质，实现从系综到孤立单分子的发光体密度调控，并显著抑制扩散、提升光稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机分子发光体（organic molecular emitters）是量子技术的有前景构筑单元，但其在二维界面上的应用受限于扩散（diffusion）、聚集（aggregation）与光漂白（photobleaching），因此需要一种能在二维界面上稳定隔离分子的宿主基质方案。

Method: 采用溶液相共沉积工艺，将 terrylene 荧光团与长链 tetrapentacontane 共同沉积于 hBN 上，形成约一纳米厚的烷烃层类 Shpolskii 主客体基质；并结合时间序列共聚焦显微（time-lapse confocal microscopy）、自相关分析（autocorrelation analysis）与光漂白后荧光恢复（FRAP）等手段表征扩散与发光体位置稳定性，另以上层 hBN 封装测试光稳定性。

Result: terrylene 被分散于约 1 nm 厚的烷烃层中且未检测到相分离（phase separation），发光体密度可从系综调控至孤立发光体，后者表现出符合单分子荧光（single-molecule fluorescence）特征的闪烁（blinking）行为；在多个样品中观察到扩散被抑制、发光体位置稳定；顶部 hBN 封装进一步提升光稳定性，发光体在高光子剂量下仍具长寿命。

Conclusion: 孤立发光体与烷烃基质的成功共吸附是将 Shpolskii 型分子隔离（Shpolskii-type molecular isolation）拓展至 hBN 的重要里程碑，并为构建稳定、界面集成（interface-integrated）的分子量子比特（molecular qubits）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Organic molecular emitters are promising building blocks for quantum technologies, but their use at two-dimensional interfaces is limited by diffusion, aggregation, and photobleaching. We present work towards constructing an ultrathin two-dimensional Shpolskii-like host-guest matrix on hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) by co-depositing terrylene fluorophores with the long-chain tetrapentacontane in a solution-phase process. We demonstrate that this approach disperses terrylene in a roughly one-nanometer-thin alkane layer without detectable phase separation and allows tuning the emitter density from ensembles to isolated emitters showing characteristic blinking behavior consistent with single-molecule fluorescence. Time-lapse confocal microscopy, autocorrelation analysis, and fluorescence recovery after photobleaching show that suppressed diffusion and stable emitter positions can be achieved in several samples with a tetrapentacontane matrix. Encapsulation with hBN from the top side further improves photostability, with emitters demonstrating long lifetimes despite high photon doses. Successful co-adsorption of isolated emitters and alkane matrix is a milestone towards extending Shpolskii-type molecular isolation to hBN and provides a route towards stable, interface-integrated molecular qubits.

</details>


### [113] [Subsonic terahertz plasmon instability in space-time plasmonic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40880)
*H. Terças,J. L. Figueiredo,P. Cosme*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究受行波周期调制作用下的二维电子流体中等离子体波（plasma wave）的集体动力学，揭示行波调制等效为移动的等离子体晶体（plasmonic crystal），其布里渊区（Brillouin zone）边缘Bragg带隙闭合形成奇异点（exceptional point），并触发Krein型不稳定性，从而实现亚声速漂移下的波自放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探索行波周期调制对电子流体中等离子体波色散与稳定性的影响，并寻找无需超声速电子漂移即可实现波放大的机制。

Method: 采用流体力学描述（hydrodynamic description）结合Floquet-Bloch理论，在共动参考系中以有效漂移速度刻画能带结构；解析推导不稳定性阈值与增长率，并用非线性流体力学方程数值模拟加以验证。

Result: 布里渊区边缘Bragg带隙的闭合产生奇异点，通过正能与负能模式的耦合引发Krein型不稳定性（Krein-type instability）；解析结果表明，即使在实验室参考系中电子漂移保持亚声速（subsonic），仍可发生自放大，数值模拟确认该预测并显示系统演化至由相干Floquet-Bloch模式主导的饱和状态。

Conclusion: 行波等离子体晶体（travelling plasmonic crystal）为电子流体中的波放大与非厄米能带动力学（non-Hermitian band dynamics）提供了新的平台。

Abstract: We investigate collective plasma-wave dynamics in a two-dimensional electron fluid subected to a travelling periodic modulation of the effective plasma velocity. Using a hydrodynamic description combined with Floquet-Bloch theory, we show that the travelling modulation acts as a moving plasmonic crystal whose band structure is governed by an effective drift velocity in the comoving frame. We demonstrate that the closure of the Bragg gap at the edge of the Brillouin zone gives rise to an exceptional point and triggers a Krein-type instability through the coupling of positive- and negative-energy modes. Analytical expressions are derived for the instability threshold and growth rate, revealing that self-amplification can occur even when the electron drift remains subsonic in the laboratory frame. Numerical simulations of the nonlinear hydrodynamic equations confirm the theoretical predictions and show the evolution towards a saturated regime dominated by a coherent Floquet-Bloch mode. These results identify travelling plasmonic crystals as a novel platform for wave amplification and non-Hermitian band dynamics in electronic fluids.

</details>


### [114] [The spin-transfer inverse Faraday effect in Au/YIG thin film bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41061)
*Victor H. Ortiz,Daniel Russell,Fengyuan Yang,Roland K. Kawakami*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要报告了在Au/YIG双层结构中通过逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect, IFE）实现自旋转移的研究，采用超快时间分辨磁光实验手段，探索金属层中IFE驱动的自旋极化向磁性绝缘体注入磁振子（magnon）的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金属/铁磁绝缘体（NM/FMI）双层中，低于绝缘体带隙的光子无法直接激发磁振子，而金属层中IFE驱动的自旋极化可能提供替代路径；然而这一自旋转移机制此前大多未被探索。

Method: 采用超快时间分辨磁光实验（ultrafast time-resolved magneto-optical experiments），在Au/YIG双层中研究IFE诱导的自旋转移行为。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验测量数值或定量结论，仅指出研究聚焦于Au/YIG双层中通过IFE实现自旋转移的物理机制。

Conclusion: 阐明该机制具有多重意义：揭示光角动量与埋入界面处集体自旋模式之间的基本耦合、实现纯自旋流（pure spin current）的全光产生与操控，并为利用绝缘体长磁振子寿命的THz频率自旋电子器件奠定基础。

Abstract: The inverse Faraday effect (IFE) (the generation of an effective magnetic field in a material upon irradiation with circularly polarized light) offers a pathway to ultrafast, non-thermal control of spin dynamics. In metallic/ferromagnetic-insulator (NM/FMI) bilayers, IFE-driven spin polarization in the metallic layer can be an alternative to generate magnon excitations in the magnetic insulator which otherwise would be inaccessible with photon energies below the insulator bandgap, but this avenue has remained mostly unexplored. In this work, we investigate the regime of spin-transfer via IFE in Au/YIG bilayers, by means of ultrafast time-resolved magneto-optical experiments. Understanding the mechanism of this spin-transfer IFE in these NM/FMI heterostructures is crucial for several reasons: it elucidates the fundamental coupling between light's angular momentum and collective spin modes at buried interfaces, it enables all-optical generation and manipulation of pure spin currents, and it paves the way toward THz-frequency spintronic devices leveraging the insulator's long magnon lifetimes.

</details>


### [115] [Anisotropic Boltzmann transport in a tight-binding superlattice: Exact results for power-law energy-dependent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41101)
*Sankar Das Sarma,Yuqing Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立了电子超晶格面内与面外电导率的完整玻尔兹曼输运理论，揭示在隧穿耦合t≪E_F、开放波纹柱状费米面条件下，各向异性金属电导率的温度依赖高度反常，且仅由微带结构与能量相关弹性散射即可导致维德曼-弗朗兹定律的强烈破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对由二维抛物带层沿超晶格轴以最近邻隧穿耦合构成的电子超晶格，需在物理相关区间（t≪E_F，费米面为开放波纹柱面）系统理解其面内（σ_xx）与面外（σ_zz）输运行为，尤其是温度依赖与洛伦兹比是否遵循维德曼-弗朗兹定律。

Method: 在弛豫时间近似（relaxation-time approximation）下，假定散射时间为能量幂律依赖τ(E)∝E^p，对p=0,±1,±3/2,±2七个模型计算玻尔兹曼输运，并作为每层二维电子密度n与温度T的函数进行系统研究，含洛伦兹比与维德曼-弗朗兹（Wiedemann-Franz, WF）定律的检验。

Result: 对p=-2（面内）与p≤-1（面外），微带底部长寿命载流子在有限温度下可主导输运；p=-2时在T~E_F处使σ_zz(T)出现显著峰。各向异性电导率的温度依赖随t、T、p高度反直觉，无普适的单调增减。WF定律在T→0时对固定t的所有模型严格成立，有限温度下出现各向异性修正；带边载流子先通过同一能带内分离能区的热占据使层间洛伦兹比升至低温值的数倍，随后在高温度经能量过滤（energy filtering）被抑制。

Conclusion: 微带结构与能量相关弹性散射本身即可引起维德曼-弗朗兹定律的强烈违反，无需额外机制；超晶格输运的温度行为与洛伦兹比修正均由电流载流子的能量方差决定。

Abstract: We develop a complete Boltzmann transport theory for the in-plane ($σ_{xx}$) and out-of-plane ($σ_{zz}$) conductivities of an electronic superlattice consisting of a periodic stack of two-dimensional (2D) parabolic-band layers coupled along the superlattice axis by nearest-neighbor tunneling $t$, in the physically relevant regime $t\ll E_F$ where the Fermi surface is an open, corrugated cylinder. Within the relaxation-time approximation with a power-law energy-dependent scattering time $τ(E)\propto E^{p}$, we study the seven models $p=0,\pm1,\pm3/2,\pm2$ as functions of the 2D electron density $n$ per layer and temperature $T$. For $p=-2$ (in plane) and $p\le -1$ (out of plane), long-lived carriers at the miniband bottom can dominate transport at finite temperature. For $p=-2$, these carriers produce a pronounced peak in $σ_{zz}(T)$ at $T\sim E_F$. We find that the temperature dependence of the anisotropic metallic dc conductivity can be highly counterintuitive depending on $t$, $T$, and $p$, with no generic increase or decrease with temperature. The Lorenz ratio is determined by the energy variance of the current-carrying electrons; the Wiedemann-Franz (WF) law is exact as $T\to0$ for all models at fixed $t$, with anisotropic corrections at finite temperature. Band-edge carriers first enhance the interlayer Lorenz ratio up to several times its low-temperature value through thermal occupation of separated energy ranges within the same electron band and then suppress it at high temperature by energy filtering. Strong violations of the Wiedemann-Franz law can therefore arise from miniband structure and energy-dependent elastic scattering alone.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum-Geometric Subgap Second-Harmonic Generation in PT-Symmetric Antiferromagnetic Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41167)
*Shakeel Ahmad,Fei Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文识别出 PT 对称反铁磁体中由能带间厄米联络实部主导的内禀二次谐波产生，其源于纯虚能带间跃迁，在双层 MnBi₂Te₄ 中随相位与奈尔序呈现可探测的信号变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）在电子输运与光学响应中已有明确地位，但时间反演奇（time-reversal-odd）黎曼联络（Riemannian connection）对完全亚带隙（subgap）响应的贡献仍研究不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过理论分析识别 PT 对称反铁磁体（PT-symmetric antiferromagnets）中的内禀二次谐波产生（intrinsic second-harmonic generation），指出其主要由能带间厄米联络（interband Hermitian connection）的实部控制，并完全来自虚能带间跃迁（virtual interband transitions）。

Result: 在双层 MnBi₂Te₄ 中，该响应在平庸相（trivial phases）中持续存在，在层陈相（layer-Chern phase）中增强，并随奈尔序（Néel order）反转符号。

Conclusion: 该效应可作为量子几何的探针，并在无需可动载流子（mobile carriers）或真实激发的情况下实现反铁磁序的相位敏感读出。

Abstract: Although quantum geometry plays an established role in electronic transport and optical responses, the contribution of the time-reversal-odd Riemannian connection to fully subgap responses remains less explored. Here we identify intrinsic second-harmonic generation in PT-symmetric antiferromagnets, predominantly governed by the real sector of the interband Hermitian connection and arising entirely from virtual interband transitions. In bilayer MnBi$_2$Te$_4$, the response persists in trivial phases but is enhanced in the layer-Chern phase and reverses sign with Néel order, offering a probe of quantum geometry and phase-sensitive readout of antiferromagnetic order without mobile carriers or real excitations.

</details>


### [117] [Berry curvature quadrupole induced third-order anomalous Hall effect in altermagnetic $\mathrm{KV_2Se_2O}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41274)
*Jiacheng Yao,Zhuocheng Lu,Zhichao Guo,Hua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作针对d波交错磁体（altermagnet）中贝里曲率（Berry curvature）偶极被对称性禁止的情形，指出贝里曲率四极矩（Berry curvature quadrupole, BCQ）可贡献按τ²标度的非线性霍尔响应，并建立规范协变（gauge-covariant）的Wannier插值第一性原理计算框架，以KV₂Se₂O为例给出动量分辨的BCQ分布与非线性电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体兼具补偿磁序与动量依赖的自旋劈裂，是研究非线性量子几何输运（quantum-geometric transport）的对称性丰富平台；但所考察的d波体系中TC₄z反酉对称性使动量积分贝里曲率归零、反演对称性禁止有限贝里曲率偶极，常规非线性霍尔机制失效，需寻找更高阶的量子几何量作为响应来源。

Method: 推导了规范协变的Wannier插值（Wannier interpolation）公式，可直接由第一性原理Wannier紧束缚哈密顿量高效计算BCQ；并将其应用于KV₂Se₂O，计算BCQ的动量空间分布及化学势依赖的非线性电导率。

Result: BCQ对非线性霍尔响应的贡献按τ²标度（τ为弛豫时间）。动量分辨分析表明主导贡献来自自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）诱导的避免交叉（avoided crossings），从而将能隙结构与变号的非线性响应直接关联。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究交错磁体中高阶量子几何响应的一般性第一性原理路径，为下一代自旋电子学（spintronics）与磁电子学（magnetoelectronics）器件中的非线性霍尔效应探索提供了方法基础。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated magnetic order with momentum-dependent spin splitting, providing a symmetry-rich platform for exploring nonlinear quantum-geometric transport. In the d-wave altermagnet considered here, the antiunitary symmetry operation $TC_{4z}$ enforces a vanishing momentum-integrated Berry curvature, while inversion symmetry forbids a finite Berry-curvature dipole. Consequently, the Berry curvature quadrupole (BCQ) can provide a contribution to the nonlinear Hall response, scaling as $τ^2$, where $τ$ denotes the relaxation time. We derive a gauge-covariant Wannier interpolation formula that enables efficient evaluation of the BCQ directly from first-principles Wannier tight-binding Hamiltonians. Applying this framework to $\mathrm{KV_2Se_2O}$, we calculate the momentum-space distribution of the BCQ and the associated chemical potential dependent nonlinear conductivity. The momentum-resolved analysis reveals that the dominant contribution originates from spin-orbit coupling induced avoided arossings, directly linking the gap structure to the sign-changing nonlinear response. Our work establishes a general first-principles route to higher-order quantum-geometric responses in altermagnets, opening avenues for exploring nonlinear Hall effects in next-generation spintronic and magnetoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [118] [Spin-conserving and spin-flip magnetic scattering in the altermagnetic spin-splitter effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41414)
*Yunxi Jiang,Hao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在交变磁体（altermagnet）中，等总强度的磁涨落可对自旋分束比（spin splitter ratio）产生相反方向的影响；前向自旋翻转（forward spin flip）以准粒子散射率（quasiparticle rate）使两自旋能带布居趋于平衡，二维下该速率随关联长度（correlation length）线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界磁涨落（critical magnetic fluctuations）虽强烈散射电子，但因以小角度散射为主而对电荷电流退化很小；交变磁体中电场还驱动自旋电流，几乎前向的自旋翻转（spin flip）以近乎不变的速度把电子移到另一自旋能带，其对自旋输运的影响缺乏定量理论描述。

Method: 建立玻尔兹曼理论（Boltzmann theory）计算自旋分束比，保留准静态磁涨落的完整碰撞算符（full collision operator）及其随磁有序（magnetic order）的变化，并与输运时间近似（transport time approximation）对比。

Result: 等总强度涨落对各比例自旋翻转给出相同电阻率，却可使自旋分束比反向移动；自旋守恒涨落以接近电荷弛豫率松弛自旋流，而前向翻转以准粒子速率平衡等能的两自旋布居（二维下随关联长度线性增长）。分带能保持由占据态能带位移（band shift）携带的自旋流部分，能带劈裂在费米线间距超过关联长度倒数时阻断前向翻转；翻转主导时仍保留四分之一到二分之一的晶格能带比。

Conclusion: 输运时间近似能再现电阻率的初始增长，但只恢复自旋分束比初始下降的四分之一到二分之一；固定约化温度（reduced temperature）下的无序级数（disorder series）可在与有序幅度（order amplitude）无关的前提下测量该下降。

Abstract: Critical magnetic fluctuations scatter electrons strongly near an ordering transition, but they barely degrade the charge current, because they scatter mostly through small angles. In an altermagnet the electric field also drives a spin current, and a nearly forward spin flip moves an electron to the other spin band at almost unchanged velocity. We develop a Boltzmann theory of the spin splitter ratio that keeps the full collision operator of quasistatic magnetic fluctuations and its change with the magnetic order. Fluctuations of equal total strength give the same resistivity for every fraction of spin flips, yet they can move the spin splitter ratio in opposite directions. Spin conserving fluctuations relax the spin current at about the charge relaxation rate, whereas forward flips equalize the two spin populations at equal energy at the quasiparticle rate, which in two dimensions grows linearly with the correlation length. Energy conservation on the split bands protects the part of the spin current carried by the band shift of the occupied states at every scattering strength, and the splitting blocks forward flips once the spin Fermi lines lie farther apart than the inverse correlation length. When flips dominate, a quarter to a half of the lattice band ratio survives. The transport time approximation reproduces the initial growth of the resistivity but recovers only a quarter to a half of the initial fall of the ratio, and a disorder series at fixed reduced temperature measures this fall independent of the order amplitude.

</details>


### [119] [Spin conservation protects the altermagnetic spin-splitter current from critical scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41419)
*Yunxi Jiang,Hao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在交错磁体（altermagnet）中，自旋流（spin current）仅当临界涨落（critical fluctuations）保持自旋守恒时才享有与电荷流类似的保护；前向自旋翻转（forward spin flips）会以准粒子（quasiparticle）速率平衡两自旋布居，使自旋分裂比（spin splitter ratio）在相同电阻率下可朝相反方向变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性转变附近临界涨落强烈散射电子，但长波涨落几乎不改变电子速度，电荷流仍可存活；而在交错磁体中电场还通过自旋分裂效应（spin splitter effect）驱动自旋流，因此需厘清自旋流是否同样受临界涨落保护。

Method: 求解包含完整碰撞算子（collision operator）及其随磁序变化的玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation），分析自旋守恒与非守恒涨落对自旋流的弛豫作用，并引入固定约化温度（reduced temperature）下的无序序列测量。

Result: 前向翻转在等能量处按准粒子速率平衡两自旋布居，该速率在二维中随关联长度（correlation length）线性增长；自旋守恒涨落则以与电荷弛豫速率相当的速率弛豫自旋流。能量守恒保护跟随占据态能带移动的自旋流部分；当自旋费米线间距超过关联长度倒数时，能带分裂阻止前向翻转。实验上，该比值初始下降斜率与磁电阻率（magnetic resistivity）相关且与序参量振幅无关，斜率向转变点增长可标识自旋翻转临界涨落。

Conclusion: 自旋流的临界保护是有条件的，取决于涨落是否自旋守恒及自旋费米线间距与关联长度的相对大小；自旋分裂比初始斜率随温度趋近转变点的增长可作为识别自旋翻转临界涨落的判据。

Abstract: Near a magnetic transition, critical fluctuations scatter electrons strongly, but the charge current survives, because long wavelength fluctuations barely turn the electron velocity. In an altermagnet the electric field also drives a spin current, the spin splitter effect, and a nearly forward scattering event that flips spin moves an electron to the other spin band at almost unchanged velocity. We show that the spin current shares the protection of the charge current only when the fluctuations conserve spin. Solving the Boltzmann equation with the full collision operator, including its change with the magnetic order, we find that forward flips equalize the two spin populations at equal energy at the quasiparticle rate, which in two dimensions grows linearly with the correlation length, whereas spin conserving fluctuations relax the spin current at a rate comparable to the charge relaxation rate. Fluctuations with the same resistivity can thus move the spin splitter ratio in opposite directions. Energy conservation protects the part of the spin current that follows the band shift of the occupied states, and the splitting blocks forward flips once the spin Fermi lines lie farther apart than the inverse correlation length. A disorder series at fixed reduced temperature measures the initial fall of the ratio with the magnetic resistivity independent of the order amplitude, and the growth of this slope toward the transition identifies spin flip critical fluctuations.

</details>


### [120] [Potentials of individual atoms by convergent beam electron diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41513)
*Tatiana Latychevskaia,Colin Robert Woods,Yi Bo Wang,Matthew Holwill,Eric Prestat,Sara Mustafi,Sarah J. Haigh,Kostya S. Novoselov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究提出在二维材料（2D materials）的会聚束电子衍射（CBED）中，单个CBED斑点内的平均强度按单原子散射参数呈现规律性依赖，从而可由零阶与高阶斑点强度之比直接反演原子的高斯型散射势参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBED斑点内的强度分布可反映探测区域的局部原子排布，但如何从中直接、定量地获取单原子散射参数（scattering parameters）尚缺乏简便途径，本文旨在建立强度与散射参数间的直接对应关系以实现直接测量。

Method: 将单个原子的散射势（scattering potential）近似为高斯函数（Gaussian function），以其标准差（standard deviation）和最大相移（maximal phase shift）为待定参数；通过CBED图样中零阶与高阶斑点的强度比（ratio of intensities）反演上述参数，并用模拟CBED图样（未施加弱相位物体近似，weak phase object approximation）验证，最后应用于石墨烯（graphene）与扭转石墨烯-hBN结构的实验图样。

Result: 模拟结果表明，即使对相移高达1.5 rad的强散射材料（如钨，tungsten），所提方法仍能给出准确结果；由石墨烯与扭转石墨烯-hBN结构实验CBED图样恢复的散射参数与理论值吻合良好。

Conclusion: CBED斑点平均强度可作为单原子散射参数的直接探针，该方法无需弱相位物体近似，适用于大相移材料，并在二维材料实验中得到验证。

Abstract: In convergent beam electron diffraction (CBED) on two-dimensional (2D) materials, the intensity distributions within the individual CBED spots map the local atomic arrangements within the probed region. In this study we demonstrate that the average intensities within the CBED spots essentially depend on the scattering parameters by a single atom, thus, offering the possibility of the direct measurement of such parameters. Scattering potential of an individual atom can be approximated by the Gaussian function and its parameters, the standard deviation and the maximal phase shift, can be recovered from the ratio of the intensities in the zero- and higher orders spots in CBED pattern. In order to demonstrate this, we simulated CBED patterns and extracted the atomic scattering parameters from such patterns. In the simulated examples, no weak phase object approximation is applied and the proposed method provides accurate results even for materials with large phase shifts up to 1.5 rad, as for example tungsten. The scattering parameters recovered from the experimental CBED patterns of graphene and twisted graphene-hBN structure show good agreement with the theoretically obtained values.

</details>


### [121] [Hierarchy of Electromagnetic Gauge Field Quantum Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41610)
*Bin Wang,Fuming Xu,Lei Zhang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将常规Bloch量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor, QGT）推广到规范场空间，建立电/磁多极量子几何层级，并显式构造连接k±q/2的流-流QGT以描述非局域电流响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QGT仅描述空间均匀微扰下的电偶极几何与竖直Bloch跃迁，无法涵盖非局域电磁响应与非竖直跃迁，故需更广泛的几何框架。

Method: 把QGT置于电、磁多极量子几何层级中；以场依赖投影算符唯一确定各通道QGT，将响应曲率与量子度规配对；并推广至有限动量转移，构造k±q/2间的流-流QGT，在加倍布里渊区中分析切向与法向导数。

Result: 各电磁通道的本征反对称绝热响应为其共轭场空间中的Berry曲率；有限动量曲率支配本征非局域Hall响应，而对称光吸收的逆频率矩探测指标对称的度规扇区。

Conclusion: 该框架将动量空间QGT多极与有限动量几何统一，并在q二阶建立二者联系，为局域与非局域电磁响应的几何描述提供一致基础。

Abstract: The conventional Bloch quantum geometric tensor (QGT) describes electric-dipole geometry for spatially uniform perturbations and vertical Bloch transitions. We develop a broader framework linking quantum geometry to local and nonlocal electromagnetic response in gauge-field space, placing this tensor within a hierarchy of electric and magnetic multipole quantum geometries. The intrinsic antisymmetric adiabatic response in every electromagnetic channel is a Berry curvature in the space of its conjugate fields. The field-dependent projector uniquely fixes the QGT for that channel, pairing the response curvature with a quantum metric. This construction extends to spatially nonuniform perturbations, whose finite momentum transfer induces nonvertical Bloch transitions. We explicitly construct the current--current QGT connecting Bloch states at $\mathbf{k}\pm\mathbf{q}/2$, providing a unified metric and curvature description of nonlocal current response. In the doubled Brillouin zone, tangential derivatives generate conventional momentum-space QGT multipoles, whereas normal derivatives connect finite-momentum geometry to uniform electric- and magnetic-multipole-pair QGTs. We derive this connection through second order in $\mathbf{q}$. The finite-momentum curvature governs the intrinsic nonlocal Hall response, while an inverse-frequency moment of symmetric optical absorption probes the index-symmetric metric sector.

</details>


### [122] [Evaluating Instrument-Centered AI Agents for Table-Top Science Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41717)
*Jiaqi Cai,Siyu Cheng,William S. Huxter,Li-Qiao Xia,Shuwen Sun,Shuai Yuan,Yifan Zhao,Naiyuan Zhang,Weijie Li,Muxian Xu,Ilija Zeljkovic,Xiaodong Xu,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出 instrMCP 与 q-devBench 组成的评估栈（evaluation stack），用于桌面试科学实验（table-top science experiments）中面向仪器的 AI 智能体（AI agents）的可复现评测，并揭示当前系统操作能力较强而科学分析能力明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实仪器难以大规模重置与共享，且仪器状态性（statefulness）与安全约束使评测复杂化，因此缺乏对桌面试实验中 AI 智能体的可靠评估手段。

Method: instrMCP 通过模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol）将编码智能体（coding agents）接入科学仪器，作为仪器访问框架（instrument-access harness）；q-devBench 将凝聚态物理（condensed-matter physics）中 37 项真实实验任务重构为虚拟 QCoDeS 仪器，涵盖量子电子输运（quantum electronic transport）、扫描探针成像（scanning probe imaging）与校准流程（calibration workflows）。

Result: 多种商用编码智能体配置在测量相关评分细则（rubric criteria）上的满足率为 70-83%，但科学分析环节性能降至 28-46%；在仅分析消融实验（analysis-only ablation）中，提供干净参考数据集后分析表现显著提升，弥合了测量与分析得分差距的约三分之二。

Conclusion: 分析能力缺口在很大程度上源于测量会话上下文（包括工具使用开销、更长的上下文、测量规划决策与可能的采集错误），但仍存在残余的科学推理（scientific-reasoning）差距；该评估栈为实验室 AI 框架（laboratory AI harnesses）的基准测试、诊断与改进提供实用沙箱，并建立了从受控虚拟评估走向未来真实硬件验证的路径。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of AI agents for table-top science experiments has remained elusive because live instruments are hard to reset and share at scale, while instrument statefulness and safety constraints complicate evaluation. We introduce instrMCP and q-devBench, an evaluation stack for instrument-centered AI agents in table-top experiments. instrMCP connects coding agents to scientific instruments through the Model Context Protocol, serving as the instrument-access harness. q-devBench reconstructs 37 real experimental tasks from condensed-matter physics as virtual QCoDeS instruments covering quantum electronic transport, scanning probe imaging, and calibration workflows. q-devBench exposes reproducible failure modes in laboratory-agent systems while preserving realistic instrument-control workflows. Across a range of commercial coding-agent configurations, current systems already show substantial operational competence, satisfying 70-83% of measurement-related rubric criteria. The main bottleneck is scientific analysis, where performance falls to 28-46%. In an analysis-only ablation in which agents receive clean reference datasets without running the virtual measurement session, analysis performance improves substantially, closing roughly two thirds of the difference between the measurement and analysis scores. This suggests that much of the deficit arises from the measurement-session context, including tool-use overhead, longer context, measurement-planning decisions, and possible acquisition errors, while a residual scientific-reasoning gap remains. Together, instrMCP and q-devBench provide a practical sandbox for benchmarking, diagnosing, and improving laboratory AI harnesses, and establish a route from controlled virtual evaluation to future live-hardware validation.

</details>


### [123] [Dipolar Exciton Polarons in a van der Waals Homobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41730)
*Yuxin Duan,Hongming Zhang,Yaofeng Zhu,Hang Zhou,Xinyue Zhang,Yan Liu,Song Luo,Long Zhang,Zhanghai Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在意向电调控的范德华同质双层（van der Waals homobilayer）中实现偶极激子极化子（dipolar exciton polaron），其激子-电子结合能较常规层内激子极化子提升四倍以上，并证明费米海层极化可调控多体杂化与取向选择性缀饰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米极化子（Fermi polaron）是多体相互作用产生准粒子的范例；在电子掺杂二维半导体中，激子极化子（exciton polaron）为费米极化子物理提供光学探测与电学可调性，但获得更强且更可控的激子-电子相互作用仍是关键挑战。

Method: 在电学可调的范德华同质双层中构建偶极激子极化子：层间激子（interlayer exciton）的电子与空穴空间分离，作为携带永久电偶极矩的量子杂质（quantum impurity）；并通过费米海层极化（Fermi-sea layer polarization）引入额外调控自由度。

Result: 偶极极化子表现出显著增强的激子-电子相互作用，结合能（binding energy）超过常规层内激子极化子的四倍；费米海的存在诱导取向相反的偶极极化子之间发生杂化（hybridization），而将费米海限制于单层则抑制该杂化，产生取向选择性的多体缀饰（orientation-selective many-body dressing）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了偶极极化子物理的实验平台，其中偶极取向与费米海层极化共同支配多体相互作用，为电学可调相互作用以及偶极极化子介导的巡游电子（itinerant electron）间吸引提供途径。

Abstract: Fermi polarons exemplify how many-body interactions give rise to emergent quasiparticles, providing a fundamental framework for understanding interacting quantum matter. In electron-doped two-dimensional semiconductors, exciton polarons offer optical access to Fermi-polaron physics and electrical tunability, yet achieving stronger and more controllable exciton-electron interactions remains a key challenge. Here, we realize dipolar exciton polarons in an electrically tunable van der Waals homobilayer, where interlayer excitons with spatially separated electrons and holes act as quantum impurities carrying permanent electric dipole moments. These dipolar polarons exhibit substantially enhanced exciton-electron interactions, with binding energies more than four times those of conventional intralayer exciton polarons. We further demonstrate that Fermi-sea layer polarization provides an additional degree of freedom for controlling many-body interactions. In particular, the presence of a Fermi sea induces hybridization between oppositely oriented dipolar polarons, whereas confining the Fermi sea to a single layer suppresses this hybridization and produces orientation-selective many-body dressing. Our findings establish an experimental platform for dipolar-polaron physics in which dipole orientation and Fermi-sea layer polarization jointly govern many-body interactions, opening a route towards electrically tunable interactions and dipolar-polaron-mediated attraction between itinerant electrons.

</details>


### [124] [Andreev-Enhanced Conductance in a Bilayer Graphene Quantum Point Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41915)
*Emma Horgan,Bernd Aichner,Nadine Omar,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Luca Banszerus*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双层石墨烯量子点接触（bilayer graphene quantum point contact）与铝超导电极耦合的器件中观测到安德烈夫增强电导（Andreev-enhanced conductance）；增强幅度随模式占据呈非线性透射依赖，有效透射率最高达0.9，偏压窗口约140 μV，接近铝膜的近邻诱导超导能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点接触（quantum point contact）为探测依赖模式的安德烈夫反射（Andreev reflection）提供平台，本文旨在研究门控双层石墨烯量子点接触与超导体耦合时的模式分辨安德烈夫反射行为。

Method: 采用门定义（gate-defined）双层石墨烯量子点接触，与超导铝电极耦合；通过静电门控实现模式的顺序占据，并用正常态电导量子化（conductance quantization）加以验证；在超导态下测量电导平台内外增强的差异，并在面内与垂直磁场下考察增强的演化。

Result: 超导态下电导平台内增强、平台间减弱，符合安德烈夫反射的非线性透射依赖；有效透射概率最高约0.9，增强偏压窗口约140 μV，与铝膜能隙接近。面内磁场下增强持续至临界场，偏压窗口随Abrikosov-Gor'kov理论预言的谱隙变化；垂直磁场则使增强自零场起近似线性抑制，偏离均匀对破坏（pair breaking），提示磁通穿透（flux penetration）。

Conclusion: 结果展示了门控双层石墨烯量子点接触中模式依赖的安德烈夫增强电导，并表明该体系可用于探测近邻诱导超导能隙及磁场下的对破坏机制；垂直场下与均匀对破坏的偏离反映磁通穿透的影响。

Abstract: Quantum point contacts provide a platform for probing mode-dependent Andreev reflection. Here, we demonstrate Andreev-enhanced conductance in a gate-defined bilayer graphene quantum point contact coupled to a superconducting aluminum electrode. Electrostatic gating enables sequential mode occupation, verified by normal-state conductance quantization. In the superconducting state, the conductance is enhanced within the conductance plateaus, while the enhancement decreases between plateaus, consistent with the nonlinear transmission dependence of Andreev reflection. The enhancement yields effective transmission probabilities up to 0.9, and extends over a bias window of approximately 140 $μ$V, which we associate with a proximity-induced superconducting gap, close to the gap of the aluminum film. For in-plane fields, the enhancement persists up to the critical field, and the bias window follows the spectral gap predicted by Abrikosov-Gor'kov theory. In contrast, perpendicular fields suppress the enhancement approximately linearly from zero field, deviating from homogeneous pair breaking and indicative of flux penetration.

</details>


### [125] [Observation of light-induced Haldane gap in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42115)
*Fei Wang,Xuanxi Cai,Wanying Chen,Xiao Tang,Jinze Li,Benshu Fan,Tianshuang Sheng,Jinxi Lu,Jiansong Li,Hongyun Zhang,Pu Yu,Peizhe Tang,Wenhui Duan,Angel Rubio,Zhiyuan Sun,Shuyun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用时间与角分辨光电子能谱（TR-ARPES）在单层石墨烯（graphene）中首次直接观测到圆偏振光驱动下打开的光诱导狄拉克能隙（Dirac gap），并借助Floquet理论将其归因于光诱导Haldane质量，揭示出非平庸的Floquet贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与陈拓扑（Chern topology）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料的对称性与拓扑由电子波函数编码，通常由平衡态哈密顿量决定；强光场可在超快时间尺度上重塑哈密顿量，使对称性与拓扑成为可动态调控的自由度。石墨烯中长期被预言圆偏振光可动态破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry, TRS）并在狄拉克点打开拓扑非平庸能隙，但该能隙这一关键光谱学特征始终缺乏直接实验证据。

Method: 采用时间与角分辨光电子能谱（time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）对单层石墨烯进行测量，考察能隙是否随光场同步出现且仅在圆偏振驱动下产生；并结合Floquet理论对实验结果进行理论分析。

Result: 实验直接观测到光诱导狄拉克能隙：该能隙与光场同时出现，且仅存在于圆偏振驱动条件下，从而将其打开直接关联于动态TRS破缺。结合Floquet理论，该能隙被指认为光诱导的Haldane质量，并揭示出相应的非平庸Floquet贝里曲率与陈拓扑。

Conclusion: 在典型非磁性材料中演示动态TRS破缺，确立光驱动作为对称性与拓扑超快工程（ultrafast engineering）的途径，为超越平衡态的动态可调量子物态（dynamically tunable quantum matter）提供了可能。

Abstract: Symmetry and topology, encoded in electronic wavefunctions, play a central role in the electronic properties and emergent phenomena of quantum materials. While these properties are conventionally determined by the equilibrium Hamiltonian, strong optical fields offer a fundamentally different route by reshaping the Hamiltonian on ultrafast timescales, turning symmetry and topology into dynamically controllable degrees of freedom. Graphene provides a paradigmatic platform for realizing this concept, where circularly polarized light has long been predicted to dynamically break time-reversal symmetry (TRS) and open a topologically nontrivial gap at the Dirac point. Despite extensive experimental efforts, direct observation of this Dirac gap-the defining spectroscopic signature of the dynamical TRS-breaking induced topological phase-has remained missing. Here we directly observe this light-induced Dirac gap in monolayer graphene. Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy reveals that the gap emerges simultaneously with the optical field and exclusively under circularly polarized driving, directly linking its opening to dynamical TRS breaking. Together with Floquet theory, we identify the gap with a light-induced Haldane mass and reveal the associated nontrivial Floquet Berry curvature and Chern topology. By demonstrating dynamical TRS breaking in a prototypical non-magnetic material, our results establish optical driving as a route to ultrafast engineering of symmetry and topology, opening opportunities for dynamically tunable quantum matter beyond equilibrium.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Pair density waves via interband pairing --- a perspective from superfluid weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41687)
*Yi-Jian Hu,Yi-Fan Jiang,Wen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文以超流权重（superfluid weight, SW）为诊断量，系统论证了不依赖量子几何（quantum geometry）、仅由带间配对（interband Cooper pairing）驱动的配对密度波（pair density waves, PDW）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期部分研究强调量子几何在多带体系中稳定PDW序的作用，本文旨在系统考察另一种与几何无关的替代机制，即纯粹由带间库珀配对驱动PDW序的可能性。

Method: 以SW为诊断工具研究两个典型模型：一是受塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting）场作用的s波超导体经典Fulde-Ferrell模型，实现两条刚性平移能带间的纯带间配对；二是自旋无极化蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）最近邻f波配对模型，其修正版本近期在密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization-group）模拟中显示PDW不稳定性。

Result: 两模型在平均场层面SW均无量子几何贡献，但SW可被大幅压低甚至转为负值。第一模型中SW随塞曼场增强而持续受抑，并在出现大量无能隙激发（即Bogoliubov费米面）时急剧转负；蜂窝f波模型中解析与数值均表明量子几何SW为零且总SW恒为负。

Conclusion: 结果确立SW作为PDW序倾向的定性指示量，并为带间配对驱动的PDW不稳定性提供了微观机理依据。

Abstract: Pair density waves (PDW) are superconducting states that exhibit a spatially modulated pairing order parameter, corresponding to Cooper pairs carrying finite center-of-mass momentum. While some recent studies have emphasized the role of quantum geometry in stabilizing PDW order in certain multiband systems, we here present a systematic investigation of an alternative, geometry-independent mechanism: PDW order driven purely by interband Cooper pairing. Using the superfluid weight (SW) as a diagnostic, we study two canonical models. The first is the classic Fulde-Ferrell model of an $s$-wave superconductor subject to a Zeeman splitting field, which realizes pure interband pairing between two rigidly shifted bands; the second is a spinless honeycomb-lattice model with nearest-neighbor $f$-wave pairing, a modified version of which was recently found to exhibit PDW instability in density-matrix renormalization-group simulations. In both models the SW receives no quantum geometric contribution at the mean-field level, yet it can be greatly suppressed or even turn negative, signaling a tendency toward pairing with spatial phase modulations. For the first model, we establish that the SW is increasingly suppressed as the Zeeman field grows, drastically turning negative upon the emergence of extensive gapless excitations, i.e., the Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces. For the honeycomb $f$-wave model, we demonstrate, both analytically and numerically, vanishing quantum-geometric SW and an invariably negative total SW. Our results thus establish the SW as a qualitative indicator of the tendency toward PDW order, and provide a microscopic rationale for the interband-pairing-driven PDW instability.

</details>


### [127] [Creating functional nanostructures for vortex manipulation in superconducting Bi$_\text{2}$Sr$_\text{2}$CaCu$_\text{2}$O$_\text{8+x}$ thin films via focused He ion beam irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41827)
*Bernd Aichner,Christoph Schmid,Sandra Keppert,Reinhold Kleiner,Edward Goldobin,Johannes D. Pedarnig,Dieter Koelle,Wolfgang Lang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用30 keV聚焦氦离子束（focused helium ion beam, He-FIB）在最优掺杂Bi-2212超薄薄膜中制备间距100 nm的柱状缺陷周期阵列，作为人工涡旋钉扎中心（vortex pinning centers），并通过磁输运测量证实了显著的涡旋匹配效应（vortex matching effects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bi-2212因层状结构呈强各向异性并具有独特涡旋动力学，但对超薄Bi-2212薄膜的精确纳米尺度图案化（nanoscale patterning）仍具挑战，制约了对涡旋钉扎（vortex pinning）的可控研究。

Method: 采用30 keV聚焦氦离子束辐照，在最优掺杂Bi-2212薄膜中写入周期柱状缺陷（columnar defects）阵列（间距100 nm），构建人工钉扎中心，并开展磁阻与临界电流的磁输运测量及角度依赖测量。

Result: 磁输运测量显示明显的涡旋匹配效应：在匹配磁场（commensurate magnetic fields）处磁阻出现极小值、临界电流出现峰值；磁阻与临界电流的角度依赖可用有效磁场（effective magnetic field）标度，表明饼状涡旋（pancake vortices）沿辐照诱导轨迹被强束缚。

Conclusion: 结果表明He-FIB纳米图案化可有效调控Bi-2212薄膜的超导性质，为涡旋物质（vortex matter）基础研究提供平台，并推进基于该类强各向异性超导体的器件应用前景。

Abstract: Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+x}$ (Bi-2212) is a highly anisotropic high-temperature superconductor exhibiting unique vortex dynamics due to its layered structure. Precise nanoscale patterning of ultrathin Bi-2212 films remains challenging, limiting controlled studies of vortex pinning. Here, we employ 30 keV focused helium ion beam (He-FIB) irradiation to fabricate periodic arrays of columnar defects with 100 nm spacings in optimally-doped Bi-2212 thin films, thereby creating artificial vortex pinning centers. Magnetotransport measurements reveal pronounced vortex matching effects, evidenced by minima in magnetoresistance and peaks in critical current at commensurate magnetic fields. The angular dependence of magnetoresistance and critical current scale with an effective magnetic field, indicating strong confinement of pancake vortices along irradiation-induced tracks. These findings demonstrate the efficacy of He-FIB nanopatterning in modulating the superconducting properties in Bi-2212 thin films, providing a platform for fundamental studies of vortex matter and advancing prospects for device applications based on this highly anisotropic superconductor.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Tracy Widom law for sample covariance matrices under quadratic form concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41416)
*Guangchen Chen,Xing Lyu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在仅假设二次型集中不等式的条件下，为坐标可相依的独立同分布各向同性随机向量所构成的样本协方差矩阵建立边缘普适性（edge universality），并得到了最大特征值的 Tracy–Widom 律（Tracy–Widom law）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于对数凹（log-concave）样本协方差矩阵系综的边缘普适性结果依赖无条件性假设（unconditionality assumption）；本文旨在去除该假设，回答最初驱动该研究的悬而未决问题，并将结论推广至对数凹类之外。

Method: 研究对象为由 i.i.d. 中心化各向同性随机向量 q_1,…,q_N ∈ ℝ^M 构成的样本协方差矩阵，其坐标允许相依；核心假设是二次型（quadratic forms）q_i^T A q_i 满足集中不等式（concentration bound），在此假设下进行适当的中心化与尺度化后分析最大特征值。

Result: 在二次型集中假设下建立了边缘普适性，并在适当中心化与尺度化后得到样本协方差矩阵最大特征值的 Tracy–Widom 律。

Conclusion: 结果去除了现有对数凹系综边缘普适性工作中的无条件性假设，解决了最初的问题动机，同时因集中假设更弱而将适用范围扩展到对数凹类之外。

Abstract: We study sample covariance matrices formed from i.i.d., centered, isotropic random vectors $q_1, \dots, q_N \in \mathbb{R}^M$, whose coordinates are allowed to be dependent. We assume the random vectors satisfy a concentration bound for their quadratic forms $q_i^T A q_i$. Under this assumption, we establish edge universality and obtain the Tracy--Widom law for the largest eigenvalue of the sample covariance matrix after appropriate centering and scaling. In particular, our result removes the unconditionality assumption in existing edge universality results for log-concave ensembles~\cite{BX}, resolving the question that originally motivated our work. The concentration assumption also allows us to extend the result beyond the log-concave class.

</details>


### [129] [Rapid mixing of Potts Glauber dynamics throughout uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42059)
*Antonio Blanca,Reza Gheissari,Heehyun Park,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了铁磁 q 态 Potts 模型（ferromagnetic q-state Potts model）的单点 Glauber 动力学（single-site Glauber dynamics）在高温唯一性区间内于随机正则图与 Z^d 环面上均可快速混合，且阈值是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 Potts 模型采样是统计物理（statistical physics）与近似计数（approximate counting）中的基本问题；尽管最坏情形下计算困难，但在高温唯一性区（uniqueness regime）内自然动力学预期高效，而缺乏相关性衰减（correlation decay）界时难以界定混合时间。

Method: 引入新的二分原理（dichotomy principle）：在高温区间内，任一初始自旋分布要么其钉扎（pinning）保证良好的相关性衰减，要么 Glauber 动力学可快速逃离该分布。并将该原理推广至随机簇动力学（random-cluster dynamics）。

Result: 对任意 Δ≥3，随机 Δ-正则图上当 β<β_u(q,T_Δ) 时混合时间为 n·polylog(n)；对任意 d≥2，Z^d 中的立方体或环面上当 β<β_c(q,Z^d) 时逆谱隙（inverse spectral gap）为 n^{1+o(1)}；两阈值之上动力学指数级变慢，说明界是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 在无需谱独立（spectral independence）或强空间混合（strong spatial mixing）的条件下获得了尖锐的快速混合界；作为应用，证明了随机 Δ-正则图上 Swendsen-Wang 动力学在唯一性区间内以 O(log n) 时间混合。作者说明结果在 ChatGPT 5.6 与 6 的协助下得出。

Abstract: Sampling from the ferromagnetic $q$-state Potts model is a fundamental problem in statistical physics and approximate counting. Although computationally hard in the worst case, sampling is expected to be efficient throughout the high-temperature uniqueness regime of the model using the natural single-site Glauber dynamics. We establish fast mixing bounds for every $q$, in the full high-temperature regimes in the two central graph families on $n$ vertices.
  (1) For every $Δ\ge 3$, the Potts Glauber dynamics mixes in $n\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ steps with high probability on a uniformly random $Δ$-regular graph for all $β<β_u(q,\mathbb T_Δ)$ where $β_u(q,\mathbb T_Δ)$ is the uniqueness threshold on the $Δ$-regular tree;
  (2) For every $d\ge 2$, the Potts Glauber dynamics has inverse spectral gap $n^{1+o(1)}$ on cubes or tori in $\mathbb Z^d$ for all $β<β_c(q,\mathbb Z^d)$ where $β_c(q,\mathbb Z^d)$ is the critical point on $\mathbb Z^d$.
  Both temperature thresholds are sharp as the Glauber dynamics slows down exponentially above them. These results overcome the challenge of bounding mixing times without access to correlation decay bounds in the presence of pinnings (i.e., without spectral independence or strong spatial mixing). For this purpose, our proof introduces a new dichotomy principle: throughout the high-temperature regime, any initial spin distribution is either such that its pinnings ensure good correlation decay, or Glauber dynamics escapes from that distribution quickly. We expect this kind of dichotomy to have further applications. As a demonstration, we also develop its analog for random-cluster dynamics and use it to prove that Swendsen-Wang dynamics on random $Δ$-regular graphs mixes in $O(\log n)$ time throughout uniqueness.
  The results in this paper were arrived at with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.6 and 6.

</details>


### [130] [Large deviations, gradient flows and fluctuating hydrodynamics for fractional diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18182)
*Julien Barre,Hiroshi Horii*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该工作研究大尺度由分数扩散（fractional diffusion）描述的粒子系统的涨落（fluctuations），推导经验测度的大偏差原理（Large Deviation Principle），并求解涨流水动力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics）的随机偏微分方程（SPDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是逐步建立对分数扩散型粒子系统涨落的一般性理解，弥合大尺度分数扩散描述与微观粒子涨落之间的理论空白。

Method: 对带 Lévy 型噪声（Lévy-type noise）的平均场相互作用粒子（mean-field interacting particles）的经验测度（empirical measure）形式化推导大偏差原理；在独立粒子情形揭示大尺度下的广义梯度流（generalized gradient flow）结构；并推导、数值求解描述该系统的涨流水动力学 SPDE。

Result: 获得了经验测度的大偏差原理与广义梯度流结构，并给出涨流水动力学 SPDE 的数值解；结果与粒子动力学的直接数值积分吻合良好。

Conclusion: 大偏差与涨流水动力学框架可刻画分数扩散粒子系统的涨落，且数值上得到验证，为理解此类系统的大尺度涨落提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This work is a step towards a general understanding of fluctuations for systems of particles described at large scale by fractional diffusion. First, we derive formally a Large Deviation Principle for the empirical measure of mean-field interacting particles with a L{é}vy-type noise. In the case of independent particles, this unveils a generalized gradient flow structure at large scales. Second, we derive and solve numerically a stochastic Partial Differential Equation describing the fluctuating hydrodynamics of this system. The results compare well with direct numerical integration of the particles' dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Moiré metals and continuum models beyond a single valley](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40761)
*Matthew Albert,Andrew Klekovkin-Hardy,Valentin Crépel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出“环境分辨双中心近似”，可从均匀堆垛的公度双层中无拟合地学习莫尔耦合及其谐波，构建在整个单层布里渊区有效的连续模型，并在扭转NbSe₂中展示莫尔效应集中于范霍夫奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有莫尔连续模型多局限于单一谷，无法描述费米面大、跨谷的金属体系，故需覆盖整个单层布里渊区（monolayer Brillouin zone）的连续模型。

Method: 引入环境分辨的双中心近似（environment-resolved two-center approximation）：跃迁振幅同时依赖轨道间距与其在莫尔胞内的位置；以两层错配度设定局域环境，从均匀堆垛的公度双层（commensurate bilayers）中学习莫尔耦合及全部谐波，无需拟合。

Result: 该方法与含数百原子的莫尔超胞从头算（ab initio）能带结构高度一致；应用于扭转NbSe₂时得到的连续模型中，莫尔效应集中于范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可用于研究莫尔调控的超导、电荷密度波不稳定性（charge-density-wave instabilities）及其竞争的单粒子起点，且方法无需拟合、适用范围覆盖全布里渊区。

Abstract: We derive continuum models of moiré materials valid over the full monolayer Brillouin zone, as required for metals whose large Fermi surfaces are not confined to a single valley. To this end, we introduce an environment-resolved two-center approximation, in which each hopping amplitude depends not only on the distance between the orbitals it connects but also on its position within the moiré cell. Taking the local environment to be set by the disregistry of the two layers alone, these moiré couplings and their full set of harmonics can be learnt from commensurate bilayers at uniform stackings, without fitting. The method shows excellent agreement with ab initio band structures on moiré cells containing a few hundred atoms. Applied to twisted NbSe$_2$, it yields a continuum model in which moiré effects concentrate at the van Hove singularities, a single-particle starting point for studying moiré-tuned superconducting, charge-density-wave instabilities and their competition.

</details>


### [132] [Gapless and Non-Equilibrium Topology in Blockaded Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40771)
*Ramanjit Sohal,Hannes Bernien,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出多腿 PXYP 模型，用 Rydberg 型阻塞（Rydberg-like blockade）在压缩空间（squeezed space）中诱导关联，为非平衡与平衡态下的非平凡弦序（string order）提供统一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探寻一个最小模型设定，以刻画约束（constraint）诱导的巡游自由度（itinerant degrees of freedom）与内部自由度（internal degrees of freedom）之间的交织（intertwinement）。

Method: 以多腿 PXYP 模型为研究对象，论证 Rydberg 型阻塞在压缩空间中诱导关联；采用张量网络模拟（tensor network simulations）与经典随机近似（classical stochastic approximation）验证淬火（quench）后动力学。

Result: 在两腿几何中给出内禀无能隙拓扑基态（intrinsically gapless topological ground state），其可终止于超对称多临界点（supersymmetric multicritical point）；淬火后弦序在参数尺度长时间内保持。三腿几何中阻塞导致额外的涌现 U(1) 对称性（emergent U(1) symmetry），形成多组分 Luttinger 液体（multicomponent Luttinger liquid）。

Conclusion: 该模型族可自然出现于 Rydberg 光镊阵列（Rydberg tweezer arrays）与光晶格中的偶极原子（dipolar atoms in optical lattices）等量子模拟平台。

Abstract: We propose a multi-leg PXYP model as a minimal setting to explore constraint-induced intertwinement between itinerant and internal degrees of freedom. We argue that the Rydberg-like blockade induces correlations in squeezed space, providing a unified mechanism for nontrivial string order both in and out of equilibrium. In a two-leg geometry, this leads to an intrinsically gapless topological ground state, which can terminate at a supersymmetric multicritical point. This mechanism also leads to the preservation of string order for parametrically long times after a quench, which we verify using tensor network simulations and a classical stochastic approximation to the dynamics. In a three-leg geometry, the blockade leads to an additional emergent $U(1)$ symmetry, resulting in a multicomponent Luttinger liquid where both itinerant and internal degrees of freedom are critical. This family of models arises naturally in a range of quantum simulator platforms such as Rydberg tweezer arrays and dipolar atoms in optical lattices.

</details>


### [133] [Singlet-Triplet Transition of Rotating Moiré Atoms in a Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40786)
*Conor Smith,Yubo Yang,Shiwei Zhang,Miguel A. Morales*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用满足磁周期边界条件、包含完整朗道能级混合并同时处理两个自旋分量的神经量子态（neural quantum states），计算了莫尔超晶格中双电子莫尔原子的基态随势阱深度与磁场强度的演化。强场下原子为自旋三重态的最大密度液滴并携带持续环流；加深势阱则触发向非旋转自旋单态的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）每个势阱极小值可束缚多个电子，形成少电子"莫尔原子"，但其在磁场与势阱深度共同调控下的基态性质（尤其是旋转与自旋构型）尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用神经量子态（neural quantum states）方法，施加磁周期边界条件（magnetic periodic boundary conditions），包含完整朗道能级混合（full Landau-level mixing）并同时处理两个自旋分量，对双电子莫尔原子基态做关于莫尔深度与磁场强度的参数扫描。

Result: 强场下每个原子为自旋三重态（spin-triplet）的最大密度液滴（maximum-density droplet），具有规范角动量⟨L_z⟩=-1与持续环流，被视为莫尔超晶格中"维格纳分子晶体（Wigner molecular crystal）"的旋转对应物；加深势阱驱动单态-三重态转变（singlet-triplet transition），转为仅含弱抗磁反向环流的非旋转自旋单态。随磁场增强，转变移至更深的势阱，因而深度与磁场共同开辟了"转子（rotors）"存在的窗口。转变处轨道磁矩随自旋极化翻转而变号，构成可被磁测量（magnetometry）捕捉的联合特征。

Conclusion: 该现象是双电子量子点（quantum dots）单态-三重态转变的集体类比。若通过磁邻近耦合（magnetic proximity coupling）等其他方式使原子极化，转子可在弱场下锁定为风车（pinwheel）图案的自发有序二聚体晶体。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices can bind several electrons to each potential minimum, forming few-electron "moiré atoms." Using neural quantum states that obey magnetic periodic boundary conditions, include full Landau-level mixing, and treat both spin sectors, we map the ground state of two-electron moiré atoms in a superlattice as a function of moiré depth and magnetic field strength. At strong field each atom is a spin-triplet maximum-density droplet with canonical angular momentum $\langle L_z\rangle=-1$ and a persistent circulating current: the rotating counterpart of the "Wigner molecular crystal" recently imaged in moiré superlattices. Deepening the wells drives a singlet-triplet transition to non-rotating spin-singlet atoms whose only current is a weak diamagnetic counter-circulation. The transition moves to deeper wells as the field grows, so depth and field together open a window for the formation of rotors. This is the collective analog of the singlet-triplet transition of two-electron quantum dots. At the transition, the orbital magnetic moments of the atoms reverse sign as their spin polarization switches, a joint signature accessible to magnetometry. If the atoms are instead polarized by other means, such as magnetic proximity coupling, the rotors lock at weak field into a spontaneously ordered crystal of dimers in a pinwheel pattern.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum Monte-Carlo study of spin-valley ferromagnetism in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40864)
*Michael Demos,Dongqi Li,Mainak Das,Tobias M. R. Wolf,Chunli Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作针对菱形堆叠石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）中实验观测到的居里温度以上大磁熵与蜂窝电子晶序，构建四分之一金属与半金属相的自旋-谷局域矩（spin-valley local-moment）哈密顿量，用约束Hartree-Fock确定耦合、以随机级数量子蒙特卡洛（stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo）求解有限温热力学。结果表明局域矩模型在无拟合条件下可再现实验温标与磁场响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层与三层菱形堆叠石墨烯的电容测量在居里温度（Curie temperature）以上报告了大磁熵，暗示涨落局域矩（fluctuating local moments）的形成；六层体系的扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）直接观测到随升温而增强、随后熔化的蜂窝电子晶序（honeycomb electron-crystal order），与Pomeranchuk效应相关。这些实验共同构成研究动机：需从理论上刻画相关相的有限温磁性。

Method: 针对四分之一金属（quarter metal）与半金属（half metal）相构建自旋-谷局域矩哈密顿量，耦合参数由约束Hartree-Fock（constrained Hartree-Fock）计算提取，并采用随机级数量子蒙特卡洛（stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo）求解其有限温（finite-temperature）热力学性质。

Result: 四分之一金属表现为连续二维伊辛相变（2D-Ising transition），Tc≈0.7 K，比Hartree-Fock估计（约30 K）小一个数量级，更接近实验的几开尔文尺度。半金属中，无邻近增强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）时，谷间Hund耦合（intervalley Hund's coupling）增强磁响应；强自旋轨道耦合（可由邻近过渡金属二硫属化物诱导）在零温给出标准的自旋倾斜基态（spin-canted ground state），升温时面内极化（in-plane polarization）较面外极化衰减更快。

Conclusion: 尽管电子具有巡游性质（itinerant nature），耦合由微观计算确定的局域矩模型无需任何拟合即可捕捉菱形堆叠石墨烯的关键有限温磁性质，为涨落局域矩与电子晶序图像提供了定量支持。

Abstract: Recent capacitance measurements in bilayer and trilayer rhombohedral graphene reported large magnetic entropy above the Curie temperature, suggesting the formation of fluctuating local moments. Further experimental support comes from scanning tunneling microscopy experiments in rhombohedral hexalayer graphene, which directly visualized honeycomb electron-crystal order that strengthens upon heating before melting, related to the Pomeranchuk effect. Motivated by these experiments, we construct spin-valley local-moment Hamiltonians for the quarter-metal and half-metal phases. We extract their couplings from constrained Hartree-Fock calculations, and solve their finite-temperature thermodynamics with stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo. For the quarter metal we find a continuous 2D-Ising transition at $T_c \approx 0.7$ K, an order of magnitude smaller than the Hartree-Fock estimate (~30 K) and closer to the experimental scale of a few kelvin. For the half metal, we study the field-induced magnetization as a function of temperature with and without proximity-enhanced spin-orbit coupling. When spin-orbit coupling is negligible, intervalley Hund's coupling enhances the magnetic response. Strong spin-orbit coupling, as can be induced by a neighboring transition-metal dichalcogenide, gives rise to the standard spin-canted ground state at zero temperature. Upon heating, the in-plane polarization decreases more rapidly than the out-of-plane polarization. Surprisingly, despite the itinerant nature of the electrons, local-moment models with microscopically determined couplings appear to capture key finite-temperature magnetic properties of rhombohedral graphene without any fitting.

</details>


### [135] [Triplon thermal Hall response in a honeycomb-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet with Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40910)
*Guilherme F. Figueiredo,E. Miranda,R. L. Doretto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究柱状价键固体（columnar valence-bond solid）中三聚体子（triplon）的拓扑与热霍尔输运，指出各孤立三聚体子能带的陈数（Chern number）虽为零，但能依赖的玻色权重仍可产生有限热霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在蜂窝格 J1-J2 海森堡反铁磁体中，由 J2^A≠J2^B 造成的三角子格不等价破坏键中心反演对称性，从而允许面内 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用；据此探讨三聚体子拓扑及热霍尔输运。

Method: 识别两种对称允许的面内 DM 矢量模式，重点给出平行于键并外加面外磁场的模式；采用谐振键算符（bond-operator）处理，计算三聚体子能带、贝里曲率（Berry curvature）、陈数与三聚体子热霍尔电导率，并界定稀薄三聚体子（dilute-triplon）适用区间。

Result: DM 相互作用与磁场产生非零贝里曲率，但数值积分得到每条孤立三聚体子能带的陈数均为零；然而正、负贝里曲率区域受能量依赖的玻色权重调制，在非零低温下给出有限热霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 陈数为零的能带并不直接限制价键固体的三聚体子响应；文中亦讨论了次近邻自旋间面外矢量型 DM 相互作用对柱状价键固体相的影响。

Abstract: We investigate triplon topology and the thermal Hall transport in a columnar valence-bond solid of a spin-$1/2$ honeycomb-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet. Inequivalent triangular sublattices, modeled by $J_2^A\neq J_2^B$, remove inversion symmetry at nearest-neighbor bond centers and allow in-plane Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interactions. We identify the two symmetry-allowed in-plane DM vector patterns and present detailed results for the pattern parallel to the bonds including an out-of-plane magnetic field. Within a harmonic bond-operator treatment, we determine the triplon bands, their Berry curvatures and Chern numbers, and the triplon thermal Hall conductivity. The DM interaction and magnetic field generate nonzero Berry curvatures, but the numerically integrated Chern number of every isolated triplon band vanishes. Nevertheless, the energy-dependent Bose weighting of positive and negative Berry-curvature regions produces a finite thermal Hall response at nonzero low temperatures. We delimit the dilute-triplon regime in which the harmonic transport calculation is expected to be reliable. These results show that a vanishing Chern number band does not directly constrain the triplon response of a valence-bond solid. We also comment on the effects on the columnar valence-bond solid phase of a DM interaction between next-nearest-neighbor spins characterized by an out-of-plane vector pattern.

</details>


### [136] [Exact Electron Dynamics in Linear and Chiral Cavities: Complete Field versus Single-Helicity Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40986)
*Patrick Barron,Kalman Varga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过三个二次型电子—腔模型（quadratic electron–cavity models）的精确解表明：手性腔（chiral cavity）本身并不产生合成磁场（synthetic magnetic field），真正决定电子呈弹道式（ballistic）还是类朗道轨道（Landau-like）运动的是腔的模内容（mode content），而非在 Power–Zienau–Woolley 变换后做单螺旋度投影这一操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：手性腔能否把自由电子变成类朗道轨道粒子（Landau-like orbiting particle）。作者意在检验“单螺旋度腔可产生合成规范场”这一直觉是否成立，并厘清该效应究竟源于投影操作还是腔的真实模结构。

Method: 在偶极近似（dipole limit）下求解三个二次型电子—腔模型的精确解，适用于任意耦合强度。比较单线偏振模、含两种圆偏振螺旋度（circular helicity）的完整横向场，以及仅保留单一螺旋度的两类模型，并讨论 Power–Zienau–Woolley 变换之前与之后约化的差异。

Result: 单线偏振模仅重整电子质量，表现为飞行延迟、波包（wavepacket）展宽变慢及电子—腔纠缠。完整横向场在任意耦合下保持两个对易的无能隙平移，电子保持弹道运动且不出现合成磁场。单螺旋度约化给出两个不可由规范变换互化的模型：变换前约化为弹道运动，变换后约化为类朗道交叉正交哈密顿量，二者相差均匀场 B=sλ²/(2ω_c)。

Conclusion: 在完整理论中，决定二者的是配对螺旋度的频率 ω_{-s} 相对于 g²=λ²/(2ω_c) 的大小：配对模较“硬”时电子弹道运动，仅在软配对点（soft-partner point）呈类朗道行为。该重组仅对活跃扇区成立，对电子—光子纠缠等可观察量不成立。因此 PZW 变换后的单螺旋度投影本身不构成合成磁机制，腔的模内容才是关键。

Abstract: Can a chiral cavity turn a free electron into a Landau-like orbiting particle? We answer with exact solutions of three quadratic electron--cavity models in the dipole limit, valid at arbitrary coupling. A single linearly polarized mode dresses the electron mass, which delays its flight, slows the spreading of a wavepacket and entangles the packet with the cavity. The complete transverse field, with both circular helicities, keeps two commuting, gapless translations at any coupling, mode number and helicity splitting, so the electron stays ballistic and no synthetic magnetic field appears. Keeping a single helicity gives two inequivalent models that are not related by a gauge transformation: reducing before the Power--Zienau--Woolley transformation gives ballistic motion, whereas reducing after it gives a Landau-like crossed-quadrature Hamiltonian. They differ by a real uniform field $B=sλ^2/(2ω_c)$ ($ω_c$ the mode frequency, $λ$ the coupling, $s=\pm1$ the helicity). In the complete theory the frequency $ω_{-s}$ of the partner helicity, measured against $g^2=λ^2/(2ω_c)$, decides between them: the electron is ballistic for a stiff partner, and Landau-like only at the soft-partner point, through an exact canonical reorganization that holds for the active sector but not for observables such as the electron--photon entanglement. Projecting onto one helicity after the PZW transformation is therefore not by itself a mechanism for synthetic magnetism; the mode content of the cavity is.

</details>


### [137] [Electron-electron interactions mediated by overdamped vibrations: Implications for chirality-induced spin selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40990)
*Leonardo Celada,Rocco Bellusci,D. K. Andrea Phan-Huu,Alessandro Chiesa,Stefano Carretta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）为动机，研究在快速弛豫的Peierls模式存在下，电子通过关联桥的迁移过程中的自旋极化；作者通过精确Lang-Firsov变换导出两体自旋-自旋相互作用，并在二阶微扰下推导含相干与耗散贡献的约化电子动力学，数值结果表明振动弛豫不排除关联桥上的自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）所驱动，旨在厘清分子电子转移中振动模式与电子关联如何共同影响自旋极化。核心关切是快速振动弛豫是否会使关联桥上的自旋极化消失。

Method: 针对四站点手性桥（four-site chiral bridge）采用精确Lang-Firsov变换（exact Lang-Firsov transformation），通过同时调制电子跃迁与自旋-轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling），导出各向同性、各向异性及Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya型交换相互作用。随后在电子-振动联合主方程（joint electron–vibration master equation）中引入快速振动弛豫，并在振动耦合二阶展开下推导约化电子动力学，保留相干与耗散贡献及振动响应的有限时间建立过程。

Result: 数值模拟验证了该推导：在无极化注入条件下，受主端（acceptor）出现自旋极化积累，且其随阻尼（damping）与桥填充（bridge filling）呈非单调依赖关系。

Conclusion: 在所设定的局域损耗模型（local-loss model）框架内，结果表明快速振动弛豫并不妨碍关联桥上的自旋极化产生，并为将振动介导相互作用与耗散动力学作为分子电子转移中CISS的构成要素进行评估提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Motivated by chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS), we investigate spin polarization during molecular electron transfer through a correlated bridge in the presence of a rapidly relaxing Peierls mode. By modulating both electronic hopping and spin--orbit coupling, the mode mediates two-body spin-spin interactions, including isotropic, anisotropic, and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya exchange. We derive these interactions using an exact Lang-Firsov transformation for a four-site chiral bridge. We then include fast vibrational relaxation in the joint electron--vibration master equation and derive the reduced electronic dynamics to second order in the vibronic coupling, retaining both coherent and dissipative contributions and the finite-time buildup of the vibrational response. We test our derivation by numerical simulations, finding spin polarization accumulated at the acceptor after unpolarized injection, with nonmonotonic dependence on damping and bridge filling. Within the postulated local-loss model, our results show that rapid vibrational relaxation does not preclude spin polarization through a correlated bridge and provide a framework for assessing vibration-mediated interactions and dissipative dynamics as ingredients of CISS in molecular electron transfer.

</details>


### [138] [Anomalies of TQFTs coupled to parameter spaces and constraints on ground-state degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42007)
*Takamasa Ando,Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文将反常表述为构造参数化族拓扑量子场论（TQFT）的障碍，并利用投影基态丛导出基态简并度 N 与挠反常 ω 的一般约束 N∫_W ω ∈ ℤ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局对称性反常（anomaly）可出现在长程纠缠（long-range entangled）量子多体系统中并约束其动力学，因此需要刻画 d 维 TQFT 与参数空间耦合时的反常及其对基态简并度的影响。

Method: 将反常形式化为构造 TQFT 参数化族的障碍，讨论反常分类对万有 U(1) (d-1)-形式对称性（(d-1)-form symmetry）上拓扑选择方式的依赖，并借助将 TQFT 在空间流形上紧化得到的投影基态丛（projective ground-state bundle）进行推导。

Result: 得到基态简并度 N 与挠反常 ω 之间的约束 N∫_W ω ∈ ℤ（W 为闭曲面上空间流形的丛），并将其应用于带 framing 与 1-形式对称性反常的 (2+1) 维 TQFT 的基态简并度，以及保持对称性的 TQFT 的存在性障碍。

Conclusion: 进一步讨论了该框架对配边生成元（bordism generators）的启示，并提出关于通过以曲面为底的纤维化流形进行反常探测的猜想。

Abstract: Anomalies of global symmetries can arise in long-range entangled quantum many-body systems and provide useful constraints on their dynamics. In this paper, we study anomalies of $d$-dimensional topological quantum field theories (TQFTs) coupled to parameter spaces and their implications for ground-state degeneracies. Specifically, we formulate anomalies as obstructions to constructing parametrized families of TQFTs. We discuss how the classification of these anomalies depends on the choice of topology on the universal $\mathrm{U}(1)$ $(d-1)$-form symmetry. In this framework, we use the projective ground-state bundle obtained by compactifying the TQFT on a spatial manifold to derive a general constraint relating its ground-state degeneracy $N$ to its torsion anomaly $ω$, expressed as $N\int_Wω\in\mathbb{Z}$ for bundles $W$ of spatial manifolds over closed surfaces. We apply this constraint to ground-state degeneracies of (2+1)-dimensional TQFTs with framing and 1-form symmetry anomalies and to obstructions to symmetry-preserving TQFTs. We also discuss implications for bordism generators and propose a conjecture on anomaly detection by manifolds fibered over surfaces.

</details>


### [139] [Frustration-Induced $1/6$-Vortex-Paired Phase in a Kagome XY Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41161)
*Tong-Yu Lin,Guang-Ming Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 kagome XY 模型中，通过调节反铁磁耦合 J₁ 与二次谐波耦合 K 的比值使两种竞争阻挫基态流形在 K/J₁=1/9 处简并，产生携带 1/6 涡旋的畴壁弯折，张量网络计算确认由此涌现出全新的 1/6 涡旋配对相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何阻挫（geometric frustration）可在二维 XY 体系中将涡旋分数化，但既有阻挫模型通常只承载单一类型的分数涡旋，缺乏在同一模型中实现多类型分数涡旋及新配对相的机制。

Method: 研究 kagome XY 模型，其中反铁磁耦合 J₁ 与二次谐波耦合（second-harmonic coupling）K 竞争出两种阻挫基态流形；通过基态映射（分别对应 kagome 三态 Potts 反铁磁体与 kagome Ising 反铁磁体）分析涡旋类型，并借助张量网络（tensor-network）计算考察有限温行为。

Result: K/J₁<1/9 时基态映射到 kagome 三态 Potts 反铁磁体并支持 1/3 涡旋；K/J₁>1/9 时映射到 kagome Ising 反铁磁体并支持 1/2 涡旋。在 K/J₁=1/9 简并点，两流形间畴壁（domain wall）的弯折（bends）携带 1/6 涡旋激发。张量网络计算显示，仅在 e^{6iθ} 具准长程有序（quasi-long-range order）的有限温相从该简并点涌现，分隔 1/3 与 1/2 涡旋配对相。

Conclusion: 该 1/6 涡旋配对相在两个母流形中均无对应物，表明竞争的阻挫流形（competing frustrated manifolds）可为新型分数涡旋配对相的出现提供途径。

Abstract: Geometric frustration can fractionalize vortices in two-dimensional XY systems, but a given frustrated model usually hosts only a single type of fractional vortex. Here we study a kagome XY model in which an antiferromagnetic coupling $J_1$ and a second-harmonic coupling $K$ favor two competing frustrated ground-state manifolds. For $K/J_1<1/9$, the ground states map onto those of the kagome three-state Potts antiferromagnet and support $1/3$ vortices; for $K/J_1>1/9$, they map onto those of the kagome Ising antiferromagnet and support $1/2$ vortices. At $K/J_1=1/9$, the two manifolds become degenerate, and bends in the domain walls between them carry $1/6$-vortex excitations. Tensor-network calculations show that a finite-temperature phase with quasi-long-range order only in $e^{6iθ}$ emerges from this degenerate point, separating the $1/3$- and $1/2$-vortex-paired phases. This $1/6$-vortex-paired phase has no counterpart in either parent manifold, showing that competing frustrated manifolds provide a route to new fractional-vortex-paired phases.

</details>


### [140] [Multiband-Induced Complex Magnetotransport in the Kagome Metal LuNb$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41409)
*Hongye Zhang,Xingyu Ma,Ying Zhu,Jingyang Zhu,Andreas Eich,Yuan Tang,Xiong Wang,Ningxi Yang,Aifeng Wang,Yisheng Chai,Xinrun Mi,Michael Merz,Quansheng Wu,Mingquan He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究报道了笼目金属（kagome metal）LuNb₆Sn₆在约80 K发生电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）转变时的电输运性质，其磁输运行为与AV₃Sb₅体系类似，可由高迁移率多带导电并结合CDW相关散射修正来理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LuNb₆Sn₆与已知笼目金属AV₃Sb₅（A = K, Rb, Cs）在CDW转变附近均表现出复杂磁输运行为，本文旨在厘清其电输运机理，判断异常霍尔信号是否源于本征多带导电或CDW相关效应。

Method: 测量LuNb₆Sn₆的纵向磁电导与霍尔电导（Hall conductivity）随磁场的依赖关系；通过扣除局域背景提取类异常霍尔（anomalous Hall-like）贡献，并采用迁移率谱分析（mobility-spectrum analysis）检验多带模型（multiband model）的适用性。

Result: 约1 T以下纵向磁电导近似呈线性场依赖，霍尔电导则表现出显著非线性及场致符号反转（sign reversal）；迁移率谱分析表明整体磁输运主要由高迁移率电子型与空穴型载流子的多带导电解释，但在T_CDW附近出现明显偏离多带模型的偏差。

Conclusion: 高迁移率多带输运与CDW引起的载流子散射修正共同主导LuNb₆Sn₆的复杂磁输运行为，T_CDW附近的模型偏差指向额外的CDW相关散射过程。

Abstract: We report the electrical transport properties of the kagome metal LuNb$_6$Sn$_6$, which undergoes a charge density wave (CDW) transition at $T_\mathrm{CDW}\approx80$ K. Similar to the kagome metals $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ ($A$ = K, Rb, Cs), LuNb$_6$Sn$_6$ develops complex magnetotransport behavior near and below the CDW transition. In particular, the longitudinal magnetoconductivity exhibits an approximately linear field dependence below about 1 T, whereas the field dependence of Hall conductivity displays pronounced nonlinearity and a field-induced sign reversal. Although an anomalous Hall-like contribution can be extracted by subtracting a local background from the Hall conductivity, mobility-spectrum analysis shows that the overall magnetotransport can be largely accounted for by multiband conduction involving high-mobility electronlike and holelike carriers. Nevertheless, sizable deviations from the multiband model emerge near $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$, pointing to additional CDW-related scattering processes. These results demonstrate that high-mobility multiband transport, together with CDW-induced modifications of carrier scattering, play important roles in the complex magnetotransport of LuNb$_6$Sn$_6$.

</details>


### [141] [Counting kinks and magnons with the lattice: magnetoelastic effects in Cs$_2$CoBr$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41619)
*V. Romerio,J. Nagl,K. Yu. Povarov,S. Gvasaliya,A. Zheludev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过低温膨胀测量（dilatometry）与超声测量，完整重建了受挫准一维反铁磁体 Cs$_2$CoBr$_4$ 的场诱导磁相图，并揭示了沿主链方向伸长与磁化强度的紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫准一维反铁磁体（frustrated quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnet）中存在级联场诱导磁相变，需要借助晶格探针来绘制完整相图并理解磁性与晶格的耦合机制。

Method: 采用低温膨胀测量与超声（ultrasound）测量两种晶格探针，覆盖全部场诱导磁相变，并在每个相边界处识别异常；同时联合分析超声与伸缩（striction）数据以量化磁弹性耦合（magnetoelastic coupling）强度。

Result: 晶格探针可完整恢复相图，各相边界均有明显异常；沿主链轴的伸长（elongation）在整个相图中与磁化强度高度一致，并以 Néel 结构中的扭结（kinks）及横向有序磁 BEC 相中的磁振子（magnons）各携带固定晶格畸变来解释。

Conclusion: 磁性与晶格自由度在 Cs$_2$CoBr$_4$ 中强关联，晶格测量可作为磁相图的有效探针，且磁弹性耦合强度已被定量给出。

Abstract: We report low-temperature dilatometry and ultrasound measurements on the frustrated quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnet Cs$_2$CoBr$_4$ across the entire cascade of field-induced magnetic phase transitions. The whole phase diagram can be recovered from these lattice probes, with distinct anomalies observed at each phase boundary. Elongation along the principal chain axis is found to closely track magnetization throughout the phase diagram. This behavior is interpreted in terms of kinks in the Néel structure and magnons in the transverse-ordered magnetic-BEC phases, each carrying a fixed lattice distortion. A simultaneous analysis of ultrasound and striction data allows us to quantify the strength of magnetoelastic coupling.

</details>


### [142] [Solvable coupled cluster states in $d=\infty$ with prescribed momentum distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41976)
*Izak Snyman,Serge Florens*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将耦合簇（coupled cluster）指数拟设与动力学平均场理论（DMFT）建立联系：在超立方晶格无限维极限下，对低秩簇振幅（cluster amplitudes）可精确求值耦合簇波函数的静态期望值，从而构造出DMFT的波函数对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合簇指数拟设与DMFT是多体理论中最成功的两类方法，但迄今各自独立发展；本研究旨在二者之间建立联系，并区分传统耦合簇理论中的求值误差（evaluation errors）与拟设本身表达能力的局限。

Method: 利用超立方晶格（hypercubic lattice）的无限维极限（$d\to\infty$），假设簇振幅具有低秩分解（low-rank decomposition）；证明腔构造（cavity construction）将格点环境精确约化为高斯Grassmann核，单体积不可约图形为格点对角（类比DMFT自能），由此得到仅涉及有限个Grassmann变量的“杂质问题”与静态自洽条件。

Result: 对限制为秩一双激发（rank-one doubles）的最简情形，得到闭式动量分布；任一动量分布均可由某个显式母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian）的唯一精确基态实现，该谱为无能隙单粒子隧穿谱。构造上变分性保证可恢复Hubbard模型的低阶微扰理论；在Mott转变附近强耦合区的失效揭示了耦合簇双激发（coupled cluster doubles）的根本性局限。该波函数族还包含零准粒子权重且动量分布在费米能处完全光滑的无能隙态。

Conclusion: 在无限维极限下，低秩耦合簇波函数可精确化为静态自洽问题，构成DMFT的波函数对应物，并将求值误差与表达能力限制相分离；同时给出该类态在Mott强关联区的适用边界诊断。

Abstract: Two of the most successful ideas in many-body theory, the exponential coupled cluster ansatz and DMFT, have to date developed separately. We establish a connection between them, by showing that the infinite dimensional limit of the hypercubic lattice allows the exact closed-form evaluation of static expectation values of coupled cluster wave functions, provided the cluster amplitudes admit a low-rank decomposition. As a result, evaluation errors -- a serious concern in conventional coupled cluster theory -- can be disentangled from limitations to expressivity of coupled cluster states. For this family of coupled cluster states, the cavity construction in $d\to\infty$ exactly reduces the environment of a lattice site to a Gaussian Grassmann kernel, and irreducible diagrams for one-body expectation values are found to be site-diagonal (in analogy to the DMFT self-energy). The resulting ``impurity problem" is an integral over a finite number of Grassmann variables rather than a dynamical quantum impurity problem, so that static observables derive exactly from a static self-consistency condition: a wave function counterpart of DMFT. For the simplest case of a cluster amplitude restricted to rank-one doubles, we obtain the momentum distribution in closed form. We show that any momentum distribution can be realized by a state that is the exact and unique ground state of an explicit parent Hamiltonian with a gapless single-particle tunnelling spectrum. By construction these coupled cluster states variationally recover low order perturbation theory for the Hubbard model. Their failure in the strong coupling regime near the Mott transition diagnoses a fundamental limitation of coupled cluster doubles. Intriguingly, our family of coupled cluster wave functions includes a gapless state with zero quasiparticle weight, with a momentum distribution that is completely smooth at the Fermi energy.

</details>


### [143] [Charge Pumping Leptogenesis from the Symmetric Mass Generation Phase on a Four-Leg Ladder Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42103)
*Runze Chi,Meng Zeng,Tao Xiang,Yi-Zhuang You,Bertrand I. Halperin,Juven Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用四腿梯形（four-leg ladder）的准二维密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）模拟，研究(1+1)d 3-4-5-0手征费米子模型单边界受相互作用调制时量子化电荷泵浦（quantized charge pumping）的稳定性，发现三种边界相中每磁通量子泵浦电荷量相同，但空间分布与输运机制不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔体系中量子化电荷泵浦是体拓扑性质，通常在无能隙手征边缘模（chiral edge modes）上表现为谱流；无反常边界可被对称质量生成（symmetric mass generation, SMG）相互作用对称性破缺地打开能隙，但这种相互作用能隙边界对量子化泵浦的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）模拟(1+1)d 3-4-5-0晶格手征费米子模型的四腿梯形准(2+1)d实现，在一条边界上引入六费米子相互作用；并辅以开边界条件下单费米子注入的传播分析。

Result: 弱耦合与强耦合下相互作用边界附近均存在无能隙模，中等耦合则出现完全能隙的SMG相；临界相互作用强度以上出现模型ℤ₂对称性的自发破缺。三种边界相均表现出每磁通量子相同的量子化泵浦电荷，但泵浦电荷的空间分布随相而异；SMG相中端点出现相互作用诱导的味混合流（flavor-mixing current），伴随独特的电荷重分布机制。

Conclusion: 量子化电荷泵浦对边界是否能隙化具有鲁棒性，而泵浦电荷分布与输运细节依赖边界相；该模型与(3+1)d标准模型中的拓扑轻子生成（leptogenesis）情景存在类比：3-4-5-0模型的无反常手征U(1)对称性类似于重子数减轻子数（B−L）对称性，泵浦可视为沿经典定义但量子反常方向的连续形变，同时保持离散子群为精确量子对称性。

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

</details>


### [144] [Resolving the spinon hierarchy of the Heisenberg antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42127)
*Ian P. McCulloch,Jesse J. Osborne*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在半整数自旋（spin-½）海森堡反铁磁体（Heisenberg antiferromagnet）的零温动力学结构因子（dynamical structure factor）中，直接于热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）解析出至六自旋子（six-spinon）的贡献，并证明前六个自旋子即可覆盖约99%的强度和第一频率矩（first-frequency-moment）求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域自旋涨落（local spin fluctuation）会产生偶数个分数化自旋子（fractional spinon）激发，而已知的两自旋子（two-spinon）贡献仅占72.89%，剩余谱权重（spectral weight）的归属与多粒子（multiparticle）激发的分布尚不明确，需要定量厘清更高自旋子扇区的贡献。

Method: 在热力学极限下直接解析计算零温动力学结构因子，逐扇区分解出两、四、六自旋子贡献；并推导各计算扇区中与动量无关（momentum-independent）的第一矩分数，以及非零动量下每个偶数n的上边界律（upper-edge law）S_n ∼ A_n(k)(ω_{n,u} − ω)^{(n²−3)/2}。

Result: 四自旋子响应约占总积分强度的21.90%，六自旋子再贡献4.3%，使总响应达到强度与第一频率矩求和规则（sum rules）的约99%。边缘指数（edge exponent）随n快速增大，说明不断扩张的多粒子带宽（multiparticle bandwidth）几乎不携带高能权重，而更高扇区在低扇区连续谱（lower-sector continua）范围内仍不可忽略。

Conclusion: 多自旋子激发在低维量子反铁磁体的谱权重中占据主导地位，六自旋子层面已基本满足求和规则；边缘指数增长与扇区权重的分层分布共同刻画了分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）的谱结构。

Abstract: A local spin fluctuation in the spin-$\tfrac12$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet creates an even number of fractional spinon excitations. We resolve its zero-temperature dynamical structure factor through six spinons directly in the thermodynamic limit. Beyond the established two-spinon contribution of $72.89\%$, the four-spinon response gives approximately $21.90\%$ of the integrated intensity and six spinons contribute a further $4.3\%$, bringing the response to about $99\%$ of both the intensity and first-frequency-moment sum rules. We derive momentum-independent first-moment fractions in the calculated sectors and a general upper-edge law $S_n\sim A_n(k)(ω_{n,u}-ω)^{(n^2-3)/2}$ for every even $n$ at nonzero momentum. The rapid growth of the edge exponent explains why the expanding multiparticle bandwidth carries very little high-energy weight, while higher sectors remain appreciable within the lower-sector continua.

</details>
