### [145] [Calorimetry as inference: the information content of particle showers and the limits of energy measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41894)
*Wonyong Chung*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文用量热计（calorimeter）记录的费雪信息（Fisher information）格结构，说明强子量热学的“极限”其实是可逐级攀爬的阶梯。作者在 Geant4 真值记录上测得全记录分辨约 5.5%/√E ⊕ 0.3%（20–100 GeV），比常规单泛函构造低 1.7–1.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量热计并不直接测能量，而是由吸收过程留下的记录反推能量，因此能量守恒（energy conservation）中不可见分量（E_B、E_nu、E_esc）使分辨率受限。论文旨在把“记录对缺失项约束多好”形式化，从而厘清强子量热学的“终极极限（ultimate limits）”究竟由何种信息因素决定。

Method: 在记录构成的格（lattice）上建立信息单调性框架：泊松记录稀释使费雪信息按保留比例精确缩放，聚集读出代价为 1/(1+cv²)，求和读出下限为 a/√E ⊕ b。随后在铜中强子簇射的 Geant4 真值记录上，从耗散能量和逐级测量到时间、位置、多重数分辨的记录，估计量在可精确求解的玩具模型上验证。

Result: 全记录分辨（模型条件性）约 5.5%/√E ⊕ 0.3%（20–100 GeV），同一批事件上 20 GeV 处为 1.29% 对 2.14%。改用重训练的高精度中子输运（neutron transport）后由 1.29% 变为 1.30%，耗散能量和改善 7%，时间结构增益由 0.28% 降为 0.17%。双读出（dual-readout）贡献信息份额最大，簇射形状次之且几乎全为纵向、在约一个相互作用长度处饱和，时间结构增益与簇射形状近似独立可加，停止μ子中微子损失给出 20 GeV 处约 0.99% 的估计下限。

Conclusion: 强子量热学的“终极极限”仅为阶梯的第一级，即不可见能量围绕单一相关可观测量的残余展宽；其上方各级可通过更丰富的记录维度继续测量。每个读出维度都有可测的信息交换率（information exchange rate），从而区分新硬件能购得的部分与更优算法可回收的部分。

Abstract: A calorimeter does not measure the energy of a particle, it infers it from the record the absorption process leaves behind. Energy conservation writes every event as an accounting ledger, E = E_dis + E_B + E_esc + E_nu, of which only the dissipated component contributes to signal, so resolution is limited by how well the record of the dissipation constrains the missing entries. We formalize this: records form a lattice on which information is monotone; thinning a Poisson record scales Fisher information exactly by the retention; clustered records read out as sums cost the 1/(1+cv^2); and the quadrature budget a/sqrt(E) (+) b is the floor of every summed readout. The "ultimate limits" of hadronic calorimetry are identified as the first rung of a ladder, the residual spread of the invisible energy around a single correlated observable. We measure the ladder above that rung on Geant4 truth records of hadronic showers in copper, from the dissipated-energy sum to time-, position- and multiplicity-resolved records, with estimators validated on exactly solvable toy models. The achieved full-record value, model-conditional, is about 5.5%/sqrt(E) (+) 0.3% over 20-100 GeV, a factor 1.7-1.9 below the canonical one-functional construction on the same events (1.29% vs. 2.14% at 20 GeV). Under retrained high-precision neutron transport it moves from 1.29% to 1.30%, the dissipated-energy sum by 7%, and the gain from time structure becomes 0.17% in place of 0.28%. Dual-readout supplies the largest share of the information. Shower shape is next, almost entirely longitudinal, saturating at about one interaction length. Time structure adds nearly as much with or without shower shape, and stopped-muon neutrino losses set an estimated floor of 0.99% at 20 GeV. Every readout dimension has a measured information exchange rate, separating what new hardware can buy from what better algorithms can recover.

</details>


### [146] [Challenges in the verification of MOSAIX ASIC for ALICE ITS3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41916)
*Simone Scarfi,Matteo Lupi,Adithya Pulli*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文介绍针对 ALICE 内径迹系统（ITS3）升级的晶圆级原型专用集成电路（ASIC）MOSAIX 的基于 UVM 的验证策略，共发现 260 个设计错误，并总结流片签核（sign-off）经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOSAIX 为晶圆级全功能原型芯片，集成 144 个独立供电像素矩阵、8 个 10 Gb/s 串行器和片上数据与电源分配，全芯片验证资源开销极大（超过 80 GB 内存、192,000 个测试），且需覆盖复杂上电时序与辐射条件，因而需要系统化的验证方法。

Method: 采用基于通用验证方法学（UVM, Universal Verification Methodology）的验证策略，针对 65 nm 拼接（stitching）工艺实现的芯片开展全芯片验证，并覆盖辐射环境条件（10^6 粒子 s^-1 cm^-2 通量与 4 kGy 总电离剂量 TID）。

Result: 该验证策略共识别出 260 个设计错误。

Conclusion: 论文总结了 ASIC 签核（sign-off）过程中的关键经验教训，为同类晶圆级、高集成度芯片的验证提供参考。

Abstract: The MOSAIX is a wafer-scale, full-functionality prototype ASIC for the ALICE Inner Tracking System (ITS3) upgrade, implemented in $65\,\text{nm}$ stitching technology. It integrates 144 independently powered pixel matrices, eight $10\,\text{Gb/s}$ serializers, and on-chip data and power distribution, making full-chip verification highly resource-intensive (over $80\,\text{GB}$ of memory and 192,000 tests). Verification also targets a sophisticated power-up sequence and radiation conditions of $10^{6}$\ particles $s^{-1} cm^{-2}$ and $4\,\text{kGy}$ TID. This work presents a UVM-based verification strategy that identified 260 design errors and summarizes key lessons from ASIC sign-off.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Fractional Differential Equations in Chemical Modeling: From Memory and Anomalous Transport to Mathematically Justified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41817)
*Ravshan Ashurov,Nigora Qutlimurotova,Ilyoskhuja Sulaymonov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该综述面向数学与分数阶分析领域读者，系统检视分数阶微分方程（fractional differential equations）在化学相关领域的代表性应用，核心主张是区分"基于机制的分数阶建模"与"唯象的分数阶化（phenomenological fractionalization）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶微分方程越来越多地用于刻画化学中的记忆效应与反常输运（anomalous transport），但将整数阶导数（integer-order derivative）形式地替换为分数阶导数并不能自动为分数阶动力学提供正当性。关键问题在于非局部性（nonlocality）能否由物理、随机、几何或约化机制导出，以及是否产生可实验检验的后果。

Method: 采用综述方式，讨论电化学、吸附与吸着（adsorption and sorption）、反应–亚扩散（reaction–subdiffusion）、化学与酶动力学、聚合物弛豫（polymer relaxation）及大气化学中的代表性应用，并着重辨析机制型分数阶模型与唯象分数阶化。

Result: 重点强调适定性（well-posedness）、非负性、守恒律、量纲一致性、参数可辨识性（parameter identifiability）与数值收敛性等数学问题；并通过电化学实验实例说明，需将真实的分数阶效应与模型失配或不可辨识性区分开来。

Conclusion: 提出若干将分数阶分析与以实验为基础的化学建模相联系的开放问题。

Abstract: Fractional differential equations are increasingly used to model memory and anomalous transport in chemistry and related fields. However, the formal replacement of an integer-order derivative by a fractional one does not by itself justify fractional dynamics. The essential issue is whether nonlocality can be derived from a physical, stochastic, geometric, or reduction mechanism and whether it leads to experimentally testable consequences.
  This review, written primarily for mathematicians and specialists in fractional analysis, discusses representative applications in electrochemistry, adsorption and sorption, reaction--subdiffusion, chemical and enzyme kinetics, polymer relaxation, and atmospheric chemistry. Particular attention is paid to distinguishing mechanism-based fractional models from phenomenological fractionalizations.
  Mathematical questions of well-posedness, nonnegativity, conservation laws, dimensional consistency, parameter identifiability, and numerical convergence are emphasized. Experimental electrochemical examples illustrate the need to distinguish genuine fractional effects from model mismatch or non-identifiability. Several open problems connecting fractional analysis with experimentally grounded chemical modeling are formulated.

</details>

### [148] [Gravitational Lensing Signatures of a Black Hole in Mimetic Euler-Heisenberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40669)
*Nashiba Parbin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在模仿欧拉-海森堡（mimetic Euler-Heisenberg）理论下带电黑洞的弱场极限中研究引力透镜（gravitational lensing），利用高斯-博内定理（Gauss-Bonnet theorem）计算有限距离观测者与光源情形下的光线偏折角，并推导爱因斯坦环（Einstein ring）半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察特定黑洞模型（模仿欧拉-海森堡理论中的带电黑洞）在弱场近似下的引力透镜特征，并评估模型参数对透镜观测量的影响。

Method: 针对渐近平直时空（asymptotically flat spacetime），在观测者与光源均位于距透镜有限距离的假设下，采用高斯-博内定理计算光线偏折角；并据此解析推导爱因斯坦环半径，同时分析放大率（magnification）、畸变参数（distortion parameter）以及引力透镜引起的时间延迟（time delay）。

Result: 解析得到了爱因斯坦环半径，并定性讨论了欧拉-海森堡参数（Euler-Heisenberg parameter）与电荷参数（charge parameter）对爱因斯坦环半径的影响；同时给出了弱引力极限下透镜效应的观测意义，以及放大率、畸变参数和时间延迟的分析结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，该黑洞的模型参数对引力透镜特征具有显著影响。

Abstract: The present study delves into the gravitational lensing phenomena in the weak field limit of a charged black hole in the mimetic Euler-Heisenberg theory. The deflection angle of light is computed for the specified asymptotically flat spacetime assuming that the observer and the light source are both located at finite distances from the lens using the Gauss-Bonnet theorem. Additionally, the Einstein ring radius is analytically derived as a direct implementation of the deflection angle. The relevant influence of the Euler-Heisenberg parameter and the charge parameter on the Einstein ring radius is briefly discussed. We also investigate the observational implications of lensing in the weak gravitational limit of the black hole. The magnification and distortion parameter are also analyzed in this manuscript. Furthermore, the time delay of light due to gravitational lensing is also briefly discussed. Our study reveals the significant influence of the model parameters of the specified black hole on the gravitational lensing signatures.

</details>


### [149] [Thin-shell wormholes from magnetic black holes in exponential electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40711)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文从指数非线性电动力学（exponential nonlinear electrodynamics）的磁黑洞构建对称薄壳（thin shell）磁虫洞，推导其平衡应力与线性径向稳定性判据，并研究零质量磁分支下的稳定性与零测地线成像。结论是：成像退化（image degeneracy）取决于保留的零测地线轨迹与发光壳层支撑，而非仅由视界存在决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明磁虫洞（magnetic wormhole）可与黑洞共享外部度规，而喉部（throat）响应由支撑物质控制，因此需要明确何种壳层物态能提供径向稳定喉部，以及这种结构在成像上能否与种子黑洞区分。

Method: 以指数非线性电动力学的磁黑洞为种子，构造对称薄壳并导出平衡应力与线性径向稳定性判据；将种子限制在零裸质量磁分支以固定质量族中的电荷与耦合。采用标定线性正压壳（barotropic shell）、可变幻影（phantom）与可变 Chaplygin 物态，并用独立、最小耦合光线与透明连接条件做数值零测地线成像（含 ISCO 截断的旋转薄盘与天体源）。

Result: 抽样中标定线性正压壳不稳定，而可变幻影与可变 Chaplygin 律存在局部稳定径向区段；显式半径依赖增强幻影恢复项，Chaplygin 响应抵消发散的逆失效（inverse lapse）贡献，使稳定区段可接近非极端种子视界。表面密度保持为负，但内部区间满足切向零能量与内禀强能量条件；在种子光子球（photon sphere）处两变律共享精确稳定阈值。径向平均零能量始终为负，即便固有时壳层能量趋于零。若喉位于种子光子球之下并采用规定 ISCO 截断，倾斜像与种子黑洞像重合；喉位于其上则移除部分返回贡献但保留大部分明亮主结构，照亮对侧外部会增添内盘弧，但中心暗区仍可存在。

Conclusion: 磁虫洞薄壳可在特定变律物态下获得局部稳定径向区段，但其能量条件与平均零能量仍呈非标准性质。成像上虫洞与黑洞的退化联合取决于保留的零测地线轨迹与发光支撑结构，而非仅由视界有无决定。

Abstract: Transparent magnetic wormholes can share a black hole exterior while their supporting matter controls the response of the throat. We construct symmetric thin shells from the magnetic black hole of exponential nonlinear electrodynamics and derive their equilibrium stresses and linear radial stability criterion. Restricting the seed to its zero-bare-mass magnetic branch ties the charge and coupling along the fixed-mass family. In sampled cases, calibrated linear barotropic shells are unstable, whereas variable phantom and variable Chaplygin laws admit locally stable radial sectors. Explicit radius dependence strengthens the phantom restoring term; the Chaplygin response cancels the divergent inverse lapse contribution and permits stable sectors close to a non-extremal seed horizon. The surface density remains negative, although an inner interval satisfies the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy conditions. At the seed photon sphere, the two variable laws share an exact stabilization threshold. The complete radial averaged null energy remains negative even when the proper shell energy tends to zero. Numerical null geodesic imaging uses independent, minimally coupled light and transparent junctions, with celestial sources and rotating thin disks truncated at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). For observer-side disk emission with the prescribed ISCO truncation and a throat below the seed photon sphere, inclined images coincide with the seed black hole image. A throat above that sphere removes selected returning contributions while preserving much of the bright primary structure. Illuminating the opposite exterior adds inner disk arcs, but a central dark region can survive because the disk does not illuminate every transmitted direction. Image degeneracy depends jointly on the retained null trajectories and the emitting support, rather than on the presence of a horizon alone.

</details>


### [150] [Strong-deflection scaling in nearly symmetric thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40715)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究近反射对称的静态薄壳虫洞（thin-shell wormhole）中，返回光线角向绕转（angular winding）增长与可允许碰撞族消失之间的竞争关系，给出受控误差的复合角向展开并刻画标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 返回光线的角向绕转可在可允许碰撞（admissible impacts）族消失的同时增长，二者在反射对称附近的竞争机制尚需刻画，尤其当喉距（throat distance）与质量差（mass contrast）取不同相对尺度时。

Method: 考虑连接质量略不同的两个 Schwarzschild 外部解的静态薄壳虫洞；以精确壳帧匹配（shell-frame matching）固定反射区间，并在每个非退化光子球极大值处用法向坐标构造复合角向展开，在紧致内部通道区间上控制误差；辅以独立求积（quadratures）检验标度律。

Result: 结果在喉距除以质量差趋于零、保持有限或发散时均成立；当喉距正比于质量分数差的某正幂时，宽度指数由一增至二后饱和，而对数绕转系数由三降至二；固定喉距情形保持有限距离振幅与系数三。参考扣除剖面连接中间区段与有限及消失的标度喉位，独立求积分辨出与尺度比变化相关的较慢收敛。

Conclusion: 收缩的立体角孔径（solid-angle aperture）直接由临界碰撞间隙导出；对轴向观测者与同一外部中的透明测试盘，返回相位条件筛选出允许交点，近对称构型使首个可用全局穿越移至更晚标签；孔径闭合与绕转增加通过可及径向范围相联系，盘的可及性由发射段累积相位决定。

Abstract: The angular winding of a returning light ray can grow while the family of admissible impacts disappears. We characterize this competition near reflection symmetry in a static thin-shell wormhole joining Schwarzschild exteriors of slightly different masses. Exact shell-frame matching fixes the reflected interval, and a normal coordinate at each nondegenerate photon-sphere maximum gives a composite angular expansion with controlled error on compact interior channel intervals. The result remains valid when the throat distance divided by the mass contrast tends to zero, stays finite or diverges. For a throat distance proportional to a positive power of the fractional mass difference, the width exponent increases from one toward two and then saturates, while the logarithmic winding coefficient decreases from three toward two. Fixed-throat configurations retain their finite-distance amplitude and coefficient three. Reference-subtracted profiles connect the intermediate sector to finite and vanishing scaled throat positions. Independent quadratures test the scaling laws and resolve the slower convergence associated with changing scale ratios. The shrinking solid-angle aperture follows directly from the critical-impact gap. For an axial observer and a transparent test disk confined to the same exterior, the return-phase condition selects the admitted intersections; sampled near-symmetric configurations shift the first available global crossing to a later label. The closing aperture and increasing winding are therefore linked by the accessible radial extent, with disk access determined by the phase accumulated on the emitting segment.

</details>


### [151] [Separation of variables for massive spin-2 fields on the Kerr black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40780)
*Oscar J. C. Dias,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明Kerr黑洞上的大质量自旋-2场（massive spin-2 fields）所满足的耦合偏微分方程组可通过两个对易的角向算符实现分离变量，最终约化为单一二阶径向方程，从而建立Teukolsky型方程（Teukolsky-type equation）的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量自旋-2场在Kerr黑洞背景上通常由耦合的偏微分方程组描述，缺乏可分离的Teukolsky型方程，构成对该类场微扰分析的障碍。本文旨在移除这一障碍。

Method: 构造作用在场分量、其旋度（curl）及线性化Weyl张量（linearised Weyl tensor）五个分量上的两个对易角向算符（commuting angular operators）实现分离；联合角向本征函数（joint angular eigenfunction）确定五种极化（polarisations）的相对剖面并留下一个二阶径向方程；分离常数（separation constants）满足由角向算符本身导出的代数关系。另借助径向输运恒等式（radial transport identity）与首次积分（first integral）分别消除障碍与附加径向解。

Result: 得到Kerr上大质量自旋-2场的常微分方程（ordinary differential equations），等价地亦给出Kerr黑洞弦（Kerr black string）的Kaluza-Klein微扰（Kaluza-Klein perturbations）所满足的方程。

Conclusion: 该分离变量构造为Kerr背景下大质量自旋-2场及Kerr黑洞弦的Kaluza-Klein微扰提供了可处理的常微分方程组，取代了原先无分离结构的耦合偏微分方程。

Abstract: Massive spin-2 fields on the Kerr black hole generally obey coupled partial differential equations. We show that these equations separate through two commuting angular operators acting on five components of the field, its curl and its linearised Weyl tensor. A joint angular eigenfunction fixes the relative profiles of the five polarisations and leaves one second-order radial equation. The separation constants satisfy an algebraic relation that follows from the angular operators themselves. The construction removes the obstruction to a Teukolsky-type equation by a radial transport identity, then eliminates the additional radial solution through a first integral. It provides ordinary differential equations for massive spin-2 fields on Kerr, and, equivalently, for Kaluza-Klein perturbations of the Kerr black string.

</details>


### [152] [Leaking Sound Waves: Gravitational Waves From Dark Sectors Weakly Coupled to the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40781)
*Marco Finetti,Helena Kolešová,Daniil Krichevskiy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 暗扇区一阶相变产生的声波引力波信号，在考虑暗流体与可见流体弱耦合后总体被抑制，且谱形被改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常假设相变产生的声波仅在暗扇区流体（dark-sector fluid）中传播、忽略可见扇区（visible sector）影响，本文检验该忽略是否合理，考察两流体弱耦合对引力波信号的作用。

Method: 采用双超相对论性流体（two ultra-relativistic fluids）的唯象流体力学描述，不指定微观模型而参数化其相互作用；聚焦耦合在相变期间可忽略、但在随后较长的声波传播期变得相关的参数区间，并借助声壳模型（sound-shell model）进行解析分析。

Result: 解析表明即使气泡膨胀最初只影响暗流体，声波随后仍可在可见扇区中被诱导产生；但两流体共同贡献的总引力波信号相比完全解耦暗扇区情形总体被抑制，且耦合强度越大、可见扇区相对论自由度（relativistic degrees of freedom）与暗扇区之比越大，抑制越明显。

Conclusion: 暗-可见扇区弱耦合不仅导致可见扇区出现次级声波，还普遍压低总引力波振幅并改变其频谱形状，故忽略可见扇区的处理会系统性高估信号。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in a dark sector can produce an observable stochastic gravitational-wave background, and many models predicting such signals in current and future experiments have been explored. For moderately strong transitions, hydrodynamical simulations suggest that sound waves generated by the expansion and collisions of true-vacuum bubbles are the dominant source of gravitational waves. It is often assumed that these sound waves propagate only within the dark-sector fluid, while the impact of the visible sector is neglected. In this paper, we investigate the effects of a weak coupling between the dark and visible fluids on the resulting gravitational-wave signal. We adopt a phenomenological hydrodynamical description of two ultra-relativistic fluids, parametrizing their interactions without specifying an underlying microscopic model. We focus on the regime in which the coupling can be neglected during the phase transition but becomes relevant during the subsequent, longer-lasting period of sound wave propagation. Within this framework, we analytically show that, even if the bubble expansion initially affects only the dark fluid, sound waves can subsequently be induced in the visible sector. Using the sound-shell model, we find that, despite the additional sound waves generated in the visible sector, the total gravitational-wave signal sourced by both fluids is generally suppressed compared to the case of a completely decoupled dark sector. This suppression becomes more pronounced for larger interaction rates and a larger ratio of relativistic degrees of freedom in the visible sector to those in the dark sector. The coupling also modifies the shape of the gravitational-wave spectrum.

</details>


### [153] [Relating approaches to interacting quantum clocks and systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40791)
*Artur Semião,Leonardo Chataignier*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明Smith、Ahmadi、Gemsheim与Rost等提出的方法以及Born-Oppenheimer方法可统一于单一的条件量子态（conditional quantum states）描述框架，并指出量子宇宙学中的Born-Oppenheimer方法对应一类相互作用的Page-Wootters形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述量子系统相对其所相互作用量子钟的动力学，对量子参考系（quantum reference frames）物理与量子引力（quantum gravity）均具有重要意义，而现有从时间无关哈密顿约束（Hamiltonian constraint）导出关系性含时薛定谔方程的方法彼此分立，缺乏统一表述。

Method: 在统一的框架下梳理并比较多种既有进路：Smith的方法、Ahmadi的方法、Gemsheim与Rost的方法，以及Born-Oppenheimer方法，将其纳入描述条件量子态的统一形式中，并分析其间对应关系。

Result: 上述若干方法可被统一于同一框架；量子宇宙学（quantum cosmology）中的Born-Oppenheimer方法被识别为一种相互作用的Page-Wootters形式（interacting Page-Wootters formalism）。文中还讨论了关系性动力学（relational dynamics）中幺正性（unitarity）与线性（linearity）是否存在或缺失的问题。

Conclusion: 关系性量子动力学可由单一条件量子态框架统一刻画，且其幺正性与线性并非必然成立；文末对后续发展作了简要展望。

Abstract: The problem of describing the dynamics of quantum systems relative to quantum clocks with which they interact is relevant to the physics of quantum reference frames and quantum gravity alike. There have been different approaches that derive a relational time-dependent Schrödinger equation for the system relative to the clock from a time-independent Hamiltonian constraint. We show that some of these approaches, due to Smith and Ahmadi, Gemsheim and Rost, as well as the so-called Born-Oppenheimer approach, can be unified in a single framework for describing conditional quantum states. In particular, the Born-Oppenheimer approach to quantum cosmology is seen to correspond to a type of interacting Page-Wootters formalism. We discuss the issue of unitarity and linearity, or lack thereof, in the relational dynamics, and we conclude with a brief outlook to further developments.

</details>


### [154] [Effects of Eddington-inspired Born-Infeld Gravity on Black Hole Accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40795)
*Merce Guerrero,Razieh Ranjbar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Eddington-inspired Born–Infeld（EiBI）引力下求解类ADAF吸积流方程，发现内区气体密度比标准Paczyński–Wiita结果高出数个量级，可作为强场引力的潜在观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞强场区域是检验广义相对论（General Relativity）偏离的理想场所，需明确修正引力（modified gravity）如何改变吸积流结构，以寻找可观测的区分特征。

Method: 采用Palatini表述下EiBI引力导出的Paczyński–Wiita型势描述盘引力场，求解类ADAF（advection-dominated accretion flow）方程，并与标准Paczyński–Wiita势下的径向剖面（radial profiles）对比，涵盖s=-1与+1两支解。

Result: 最大差异出现在气体密度：EiBI情形内区高数个量级，大半径处收敛于标准结果；比角动量（specific angular momentum）与温度偏低，径向速度主要在大半径处不同，角速度与盘厚度比H/r仅轻微变化。

Conclusion: EiBI引力能够改变强场区热吸积流（hot accretion flow）的密度结构，可能为区分引力理论提供观测判据。

Abstract: Black holes offer a unique setting to test gravity in the strong-field regime, where deviations from General Relativity are expected to be most pronounced. We study how modified gravity changes the structure of an advection-dominated accretion flow (ADAF) around a supermassive black hole. The gravitational field governing the disc is described by a Paczyński--Wiita-like potential derived in Eddington-inspired Born--Infeld (EiBI) gravity, formulated \textit{à la} Palatini. We solve the ADAF-like equations for this potential and compare the radial profiles of the flow with those obtained for the standard Paczyński--Wiita potential. The largest difference appears in the gas density, which is several orders of magnitude higher in the EiBI case near the inner region and converges to the standard result at large radii. The specific angular momentum and temperature are lower in EiBI gravity, while the radial velocity differs mainly at large radii for both solution branches, $ s=-1$ and $+1$. The angular velocity and the disk aspect ratio $H/r$ change only slightly. These results show that EiBI gravity can modify the density structure of hot accretion flows in the strong-field regime, potentially providing observational discriminators.

</details>


### [155] [Bounds on the minimum orbital period of general static spherical black holes: a sharp upper bound and a conditional lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40843)
*Yan Peng,Guohua Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维广义静态球对称黑洞附近最小轨道周期（minimum orbital period）的上下界，证明在外界物质能量密度非负条件下 T_min ≤ 6√3πM，且在切向压强条件 p_τ ≥ (κ/2)ρ（κ>1）下给出下界 T_min ≥ c(κ)M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小轨道周期（minimum orbital period）是黑洞时空几何的可观测特征，而此前对其在一般（非史瓦西）静态球对称黑洞中的普适约束尚不明确。本文旨在建立适用于广泛几何类别的上下界。

Method: 在四维广义静态球对称黑洞（具有事件视界、渐近平坦、视界外能量密度非负）的几何类中，采用解析证明推导周期上界；进一步引入视界外切向压强条件（tangential pressure condition）p_τ ≥ (κ/2)ρ（κ>1，对所有 r ≥ r_g）以推导下界。

Result: 证明上界 T_min ≤ 6√3πM，该界是紧的（sharp）且由史瓦西解（Schwarzschild solution）饱和；在下界方面，得到 T_min ≥ c(κ)M，其中 c(κ)=6√3π(κ−1)√(κ+2)/κ^{3/2}。

Conclusion: 两个界适用于同一几何类别，区别仅在于对物质的要求：非负能量密度即可保证上界，而下界需要额外的切向压强条件。结果表明最小轨道周期与黑洞质量之比受物质性质而非仅几何的约束。

Abstract: We investigate bounds on the minimum orbital period around four-dimensional general static spherical black holes. For all static spherically symmetric black holes with an event horizon, asymptotic flatness and non-negative energy density outside the horizon, we prove $T_{\min}\leq 6\sqrt{3}\,πM$. The bound is sharp and is saturated by the Schwarzschild solution. The lower bound requires an additional condition on the exterior matter: under the tangential pressure condition $p_τ\geq(κ/2)ρ$ with $κ>1$, imposed for all $r\geq r_{g}$, where $r_{g}$ is the largest radius at which $T(r)$ attains its global minimum, we prove $T_{\min}\geq c(κ)M$ with $c(κ)=6\sqrt{3}π(κ-1)\sqrt{κ+2}/κ^{3/2}$. In summary, both bounds apply to the same geometric class and differ only in what they demand of the matter: non-negative energy density suffices for the upper bound, while the lower bound is established under the additional tangential pressure condition.

</details>


### [156] [Global and asymptotic properties of self-gravitating nonlinear sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40853)
*Oleksandr S. Stashko,Valery I. Zhdanov,Mark P. Hertzberg*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用解析方法研究广义相对论（general relativity）及其D维推广中、含多个极小耦合标量场（minimally coupled scalar fields，由一般非线性sigma模型（nonlinear sigma model）描述）的静态球对称渐近平坦解，证明解在所有有限非零半径处正则、可向内延拓至中心裸奇点（naked singularity），并给出中心奇点附近普适渐近行为与最多一个不稳定光子球（photon sphere）的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含多标量场非线性sigma模型物质源的静态球对称时空，其整体与渐近性质（解的正则性、奇点位置及光子球数量）缺乏一般性解析结论，本文旨在在较弱且自然的假设下给出普适刻画。

Method: 假设目标空间（target space）为完备黎曼流形（complete Riemannian manifold）、标量势（scalar potential）非负且满足适当增长条件，对爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）及D维推广的静态球对称渐近平坦解进行解析分析。

Result: 证明解在每一个有限非零半径处保持正则，并可向内延拓至中心裸奇点，从而排除有限半径球壳状奇点（shell singularity）；同时证明所有解在中心奇点附近表现出普适渐近行为，且此类时空至多拥有一个光子球，若存在则必为不稳定。

Conclusion: 在完备目标空间与非负标量势且满足增长条件的框架下，含多标量场非线性sigma模型的静态球对称渐近平坦解具有统一的结构性质：奇点仅可能出现于中心且为裸奇点，渐近行为普适，光子球至多一个且不稳定。

Abstract: We present an analytical study of the global and asymptotic properties of static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat solutions in general relativity and its \(D\)-dimensional generalization with multiple minimally coupled scalar fields described by a general nonlinear sigma model. Assuming a complete Riemannian target space and a non-negative scalar potential satisfying suitable growth conditions, we prove that solutions remain regular at every finite nonzero radius and can be extended inward to a naked singularity at the center. In particular, this excludes singularities on spherical shells at finite radius. We further show that all solutions exhibit universal asymptotic behavior near the central singularity. Finally, we demonstrate that these spacetimes can possess at most one photon sphere, which, if present, is necessarily unstable.

</details>


### [157] [Dynamics of spinning test particles in the innermost stable circular orbit of an approximate Kerr-like spacetime with mass quadrupole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41036)
*Cristian Herrera-Chaves,Francisco Frutos-Alfaro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在含质量四极矩（mass quadrupole moment）二阶微扰的旋转时空中，研究自旋试验粒子（spinning test particle）运动，推导含自旋与四极矩参数的显式ISCO方程，并给出守恒轨道量的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注无自旋粒子或标准Kerr时空中的ISCO（innermost stable circular orbit），而自旋与四极矩变形对ISCO参数（半径、能量、角动量）的联合修正尚缺乏统一描述，故需加以刻画并与无自旋情形及Kerr基准对比。

Method: 在Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon框架下结合Euler--Lagrange形式与哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics），采用有效势（effective potential）技术，分别处理同向旋转（co-rotating）与反向旋转（counter-rotating）位形。

Result: 得到包含自旋与四极矩参数的显式一般ISCO方程及守恒轨道量的解析表达式；并通过对Schwarzschild、Lense--Thirring与Kerr极限的取法，建立新的ISCO方程与能量、角动量的紧凑表达式，将上述基本时空推广至自旋试验粒子情形。

Conclusion: 自旋与四极矩变形共同改变ISCO参数，所给推广框架可统一涵盖Schwarzschild、Lense--Thirring与Kerr等极限情形，为自旋试验粒子在变形旋转时空中的轨道分析提供解析基础。

Abstract: We study the motion of spinning test particles in a deformed, rotating spacetime described by a metric perturbed up to second order in the mass quadrupole moment. We examine how the parameters of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) --namely the radius, energy, and angular momentum-- are modified by the particle's spin, contrasting our findings with both the spinless case and standard Kerr benchmarks. By employing effective potential techniques, we consider both co-rotating and counter-rotating configurations, corresponding to parallel and antiparallel alignments of the angular momenta. Using the Euler--Lagrange formalism and Hamiltonian dynamics within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework, we derive an explicit general ISCO equation incorporating the spin and quadrupole parameters, alongside analytical expressions for the conserved orbital quantities. Furthermore, by taking the Schwarzschild, Lense--Thirring and Kerr limits within our generalized framework, we establish new ISCO equations and compact expressions for the energy and angular momentum that extend these fundamental spacetimes to the case of spinning test particles.

</details>


### [158] [More Eccentric, Not Always Less Detectable: High Mass Holds Its Ground, Low Mass Loses Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41229)
*Elwin K. Y. Li,Juno C. L. Chan,Alvin K. Y. Li,Otto A. Hannuksela*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 首个系统性 GstLAL 偏心致密双星搜寻研究，覆盖 $e_{15Hz}$ 至 0.99：高-mass 灵敏距离几乎不随偏心率变化，low-mass 在 $e_{15Hz}\approx0.75$ 附近下降约 50%，且波形匹配度会显著高估实际灵敏度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏心致密双星并合（eccentric compact binary coalescences）的引力波（gravitational waves）记录双星动力学形成历史，但多数探测依赖准圆轨道（quasicircular）模板的匹配滤波（matched-filtering）搜寻。需量化偏心导致的搜寻灵敏度（search sensitivity）损失，以评估族群完备性（population completeness）及是否需要专用偏心模板库（eccentric template bank）。

Method: 采用 GstLAL 搜寻流程，注入由 TEOBResumS-Dalí 波形模型生成的偏心信号，扫描 $e_{15Hz}$ 至 0.99 及啁啾质量（chirp mass）、质量比、倾角、对齐自旋（aligned spin）的广泛参数范围。测量恢复信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）、信号一致性统计量（signal-consistency statistics）、误报率（false-alarm rate）以及灵敏体积与灵敏距离（sensitive volume and distance）。

Result: 对高质量双星（$\mathcal{M}_c\gtrsim30\,M_\odot$），灵敏距离几乎与偏心率无关，表明更大偏心率未必削弱搜寻灵敏度。相反，低质量双星在 $e_{15Hz}\approx0.75$ 附近灵敏距离最多下降约 50%；尽管绝对可达距离损失有限，该相对降幅超过 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA 第二至第三观测运行之间的灵敏距离增幅，并与既有低质量研究一致。

Conclusion: 固定源参数下的波形匹配度（waveform match）会显著高估搜寻灵敏度的损失，因为流程的信号一致性检验与模板库最大化在一定程度上抵消了预期损失。因此需要端到端注入实验（end-to-end injection campaigns）来确定偏心双星的可探测性。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from eccentric compact binary coalescences trace dynamical binary formation, but matched-filtering searches responsible for most detections rely on quasicircular templates. Quantifying any sensitivity loss is crucial for assessing population completeness and the need for dedicated eccentric template banks. We present the first systematic GstLAL search study spanning eccentricities up to $e_{\rm 15Hz}=0.99$ and broad ranges of chirp mass, mass ratio, inclination, and aligned spin. Using TEOBResumS-Dalí waveforms, we inject eccentric signals and measure recovered signal-to-noise ratio, signal-consistency statistics, false-alarm rate, and sensitive volume and distance. For high-mass binaries ($\mathcal{M}_c\gtrsim30\,M_\odot$), sensitive distance is nearly independent of eccentricity, demonstrating that greater eccentricity does not necessarily reduce search sensitivity. In contrast, for low-mass binaries, sensitive distance falls by up to $\sim50\%$ near $e_{\rm 15Hz}\approx0.75$. Although the absolute loss in reach is modest given their low quasicircular sensitivity, this relative reduction exceeds the increase in sensitive distance between the second and third observing runs of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA collaboration and is consistent with previous low-mass studies. We also find that waveform matches at fixed source parameters substantially overpredict search sensitivity loss, meaning that the pipeline's signal-consistency test and maximization over its template bank mitigate much of the predicted sensitivity loss. This highlights the need for end-to-end injection campaigns to establish the detectability of eccentric binaries.

</details>


### [159] [Probing dark sector with decihertz gravitational wave observations of compact binary inspirals in IndIGO-D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41473)
*Divya Tahelyani,Abhishek Sharma,Anand S. Sengupta,Arpan Bhattacharyya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文评估分赫兹（decihertz）引力波观测对原子暗物质（atomic dark matter, aDM）及超轻暗光子中介相互作用的探测能力，基于IndIGO-D空间探测器对双中子星与中子星—黑洞系统的贝叶斯参数估计表明，暗光子静止能量可被约束至约10^-20 eV量级（在暗扇区信号可探测的前提下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双星并合波形可能携带暗扇区（dark sector）相互作用的印记，而分赫兹频段是现有地面与空间引力波探测器之间的观测空白，需评估其对新物理的探测潜力。

Method: 考虑中子星吸积带暗电荷的暗物质、经暗阿贝尔大质量矢量中介（dark Abelian massive vector mediator）产生长程汤川相互作用（Yukawa interaction）与偶极辐射（dipolar radiation）；波形修正由汤川耦合强度α、偶极辐射参数γ与中介质量相关参数μ刻画，包含领头-1后牛顿（post-Newtonian）偶极项与有限中介质量效应，并将修正映射到广义相对论（general relativity）的参数化检验框架；对模拟双中子星（BNS）与中子星—黑洞（NSBH）系统在IndIGO-D上开展贝叶斯参数估计（Bayesian parameter estimation）。

Result: 对类GW170817双中子星系统、取接近当前观测上限的非零aDM耦合时，暗光子静止能量可被推断至90%可信度不确定度O(10^-20) eV，且以暗扇区贡献可探测为前提；当α≲O(10^-8)且汤川耦合与偶极辐射贡献均可忽略时中介质量无法约束，但若偶极辐射足够强，即使汤川耦合可忽略仍可测量中介质量。

Conclusion: 未来分赫兹引力波天文台可作为检验暗扇区物理、通过精密引力波测量探测致密双星中暗物质相互作用的强有力实验室。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of decihertz gravitational-wave (GW) observations of compact-binary inspirals to probe atomic dark matter (aDM) and its interactions through an ultralight massive dark photon. We consider a scenario in which neutron stars accumulate DM charged under a dark Abelian massive vector mediator, leading to an additional long-range Yukawa interaction between compact objects and associated dipolar radiation. In this framework, the GW waveform acquires characteristic corrections parameterized by the Yukawa coupling strength $α$, the dipole-radiation parameter $γ$, and a parameter related to the mediator mass $μ$. These corrections include a leading $-1$ post-Newtonian dipole contribution and finite-mediator-mass effects. We relate the aDM corrections to existing parameterized tests of general relativity and perform Bayesian parameter-estimation analyses for simulated binary neutron star (BNS) and neutron star-black hole (NSBH) systems incorporating the modified binary inspiral in a proposed space-based decihertz detector, named IndIGO-D. For a GW170817-like BNS with nonzero aDM couplings chosen near current observational bounds, we find that the dark-photon rest energy can be inferred with a $90\%$ credible uncertainty of $O(10^{-20})\,\mathrm{eV}$, conditional on a detectable dark-sector contribution. Analyses with weaker dark-sector interactions $(α\lesssim O(10^{-8}))$ show that the mediator mass remains unconstrained when both the Yukawa coupling and dipole-radiation contribution are negligible, but can be measured even for a negligible Yukawa coupling if the dipole-radiation contribution is sufficiently strong. Our results demonstrate that future decihertz GW observatories can serve as powerful laboratories for testing dark-sector physics and probing dark matter interactions in compact binaries through precision GW measurements.

</details>


### [160] [Probing Realistic Neutron Star Equations of State via the Chameleon Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41505)
*Debadri Bhattacharjee,Grigorios Panotopoulos,Pradip Kumar Chattopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 采用真实核子状态方程研究变色龙机制（chameleon mechanism）对中子星（neutron star）结构性质的影响，发现标量-物质耦合参数 β 可显著改变最大质量与半径，且现有质量-半径观测约束对 β 给出严格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于变色龙机制对致密天体影响的研究多依赖简化或理想化状态方程，其在真实核子状态方程（nucleonic equations of state）下是否仍具显著效应尚不明确，因此需要系统检验。

Method: 在真实核子状态方程框架下对中子星结构进行数值计算，以标量-物质耦合参数（scalar-matter coupling parameter）β 为自由参量，比较变色龙模型与广义相对论（general-relativistic）对应解在中子星最大质量与半径上的差异。

Result: 数值结果表明，即使采用真实状态方程，变色龙机制仍能对中子星的宏观性质（macroscopic properties）产生显著影响；β 偏离广义相对论情形时，最大质量与恒星半径均发生改变。

Conclusion: 变色龙机制在中子星宏观结构上不可忽略，且已被充分确立的中子星质量与半径约束（constraints on masses and radii）可对耦合参数 β 的取值给出严格限制，为标量-张量（scalar-tensor）引力模型的参数空间提供观测筛选。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of the Chameleon mechanism on structural properties of neutron stars adopting realistic nucleonic equations of state. The scalar-matter coupling parameter $β$ controls the departure from the corresponding general-relativistic configurations, affecting both the maximum mass and the stellar radius. Our numerical results demonstrate that the chameleon mechanism can have a significant impact on the macroscopic properties of neutron stars, even when realistic equations of state are employed. Moreover, well established constraints on masses and radii of neutron stars put stringent limits on the allowed numerical values of the coupling.

</details>


### [161] [Novel thermodynamic topological subclass from inverse-temperature asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41585)
*Dejiang Yin,Qi-Qi Liang,Yu-Die Wan,Li-yun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究正则系综下定电荷带电 McGuigan–Nappi–Yost 黑洞的热力学拓扑，发现其缺陷结构不属于既有拓扑（子）类，而应归为新子类 $\breve W^{1+}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有热力学拓扑分类基于逆霍金温度（inverse Hawking temperature）的渐近行为，而该黑洞在视界趋于无穷大时逆温度趋于有限正值，这一特征可能使现有分类无法覆盖。

Method: 在固定电荷的正则系综（canonical ensemble）中，由广义离壳自由能（generalized off-shell free energy）构造热力学矢量场，以该矢量场的零点识别平衡黑洞解并计算其缠绕数（winding number）。

Result: 热力学相结构仅包含一个局部稳定分支，缠绕数为 $w=+1$；最内层小黑洞态在低温极限下局部稳定，随温度升高稳定分支延伸至最外层大黑洞区，超过有限上界霍金温度后不存在黑洞态。

Conclusion: 该缺陷结构的拓扑数虽与标准 $W^{1+}$ 类一致，但因逆温度具有有限非零的大视界极限而落在既有（子）类之外，故被识别为新子类 $\breve W^{1+}$，从而将分类推广至该渐近情形。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic topology of the charged McGuigan--Nappi--Yost black hole in the canonical ensemble at fixed electric charge, identifying equilibrium black hole solutions as zeros of the thermodynamic vector field constructed from the generalized off-shell free energy. The inverse Hawking temperature diverges as the extremal limit is approached and approaches a finite positive value in the large-horizon limit. The zero points of the thermodynamic vector field reveal a thermodynamic phase structure consisting solely of a locally stable branch with positive winding number ($w=+1$). The innermost small black hole state is locally stable in the low-temperature limit. As the temperature increases, the stable branch extends toward the outermost large black hole regime, while no black hole states exist in the high-temperature regime beyond the finite upper Hawking temperature. This distinct asymptotic behavior of the inverse temperature places the thermodynamic defect structure outside the established topological (sub)classes, although its topological number agrees with that of the standard $W^{1+}$ class. We therefore identify this defect structure as a new thermodynamic topological subclass, $\breve W^{1+}$. This finding extends the existing classification to include a finite, nonzero large-horizon limit of the inverse Hawking temperature and suggests that distinct asymptotic behaviors in other black hole families may reveal further topological (sub)classes and thermodynamic phase structures.

</details>


### [162] [Hyperbolic scaling of the small-exponent limit in magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42091)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,R. Martínez von Dossow,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究分数幂非线性电动力学（fractional-power nonlinear electrodynamics）中指数趋于零时磁时空（magnetic spacetime）趋近 Schwarzschild 而电磁特征保持奇异的现象，并给出外域电磁能量、轨道与散射的解析展开与收敛性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁时空在参数极限下可逼近 Schwarzschild 度规，但其电磁特性在同极限下仍呈奇异，二者分离的机制尚不清楚；需在固定渐近质量、磁荷与非线性耦合的条件下刻画这一分离。

Method: 取分数幂非线性电动力学的指数趋于零，分析非常偏振（extraordinary polarization）存在共同物理时间方向的边界（随指数四次方根倒数增长），并构造外域电磁能量的精确表示，导出轨道与碰撞参数的二阶修正，同时独立进行能量、轨道与散射计算以检验展开。

Result: 在该径向尺度上 lapse 趋于 1，但本构角向响应保留非退化势垒与有限标度临界数据；约束质量等于电磁自能可保持领头临界常数，但使逼近由四次方根修正转为平方根修正。强偏折对数系数被算出；弱场下本构弯曲按标度碰撞参数的四次方反比衰减，有限质量给出较慢的逆碰撞参数尾部，并确定二者竞争尺度及其在临界碰撞参数之上的可达性与两项近似精度。

Conclusion: 近乎真空的背景可通过非线性本构响应（nonlinear constitutive response）支撑一个独立的 exterior 光学极限；能量、轨道与散射三方面计算相互吻合，并给出依赖参数的收敛行为。

Abstract: A magnetic spacetime can approach Schwarzschild while its electromagnetic characteristics remain singular in the same parameter limit. We study this separation in fractional-power nonlinear electrodynamics as the exponent tends to zero with asymptotic mass, magnetic charge and nonlinear coupling independently fixed. The extraordinary polarization admits a common physical time direction only outside a boundary that grows as the inverse fourth root of the exponent. On this radial scale, the lapse tends to unity but the constitutive angular response retains a nondegenerate barrier and finite scaled critical data. An exact representation of the exterior electromagnetic energy yields complete second-order corrections to the orbit and impact, separating mass and charge contributions and their distinct cancellations. Constraining the mass to equal the electromagnetic self-energy preserves the leading critical constants while changing the approach from fourth-root to square-root corrections. Returning rays remain inside the admissible exterior and define a limiting scattering function with a calculated logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient. In the weak regime, constitutive bending decays as the inverse fourth power of scaled impact, whereas finite mass supplies a slower inverse-impact tail. We determine their competition scale, its accessibility above the critical impact, and the accuracy of the two-term approximation. Independent energy, orbit and scattering calculations check the expansions and resolve their parameter-dependent convergence. The analysis shows how a nearly vacuum background can support a distinct exterior optical limit through the nonlinear constitutive response.

</details>


### [163] [Teleparallel Renormalization of Gravitational Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31444)
*Martin Krššák,Michal Stano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在远平行等效广义相对论（teleparallel equivalent of general relativity）框架下，以史瓦西时空（Schwarzschild spacetime）为例澄清自旋联络（spin connection）作为表面项/抵消项（counterterm）正则化引力作用量的若干表观模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远平行等效广义相对论中，自旋联络通过表面项进入作用量并可起到抵消项作用，但该正则化程序存在多处表观模糊性（体作用量与准局域作用量的差异、标架选择的角色、正则化方案的唯一性），需要系统澄清。

Method: 以史瓦西时空为具体算例，比较体作用量与准局域作用量（quasilocal action），分析奇点（singularity）相关贡献，比较不同标架（tetrad）选择对应的自旋联络，并考察欧几里得（Euclidean）作用量与惠勒–德维特斑块（Wheeler–DeWitt patch）上的洛伦兹作用量。

Result: 体作用量与准局域作用量的表观差异源于时空奇点相关的贡献，准局域作用量与广义相对论（general relativity）的标准结果一致；恰当对角标架（proper diagonal frame）与正则标架（canonical frame）给出相同的欧几里得作用量，但只有恰当对角标架能在惠勒–德维特斑块上重现预期的洛伦兹作用量。

Conclusion: 正则化方案具有唯一性；对挠率张量（torsion tensor）施加适当的衰减行为可消除剩余模糊性，并唯一确定欧几里得作用量。

Abstract: We study the gravitational action in the teleparallel equivalent of general relativity, where the spin connection contributes to the action through a surface term and, when chosen appropriately, regularizes the action by effectively playing the role of a counterterm. We resolve several apparent ambiguities in this procedure using the Schwarzschild spacetime as an example. We show that the apparent discrepancy between bulk and quasilocal actions originates from contributions associated with spacetime singularities, and that the quasilocal action agrees with the standard results obtained in general relativity. We further clarify the role of the tetrad choice in determining the corresponding spin connection, showing that the proper diagonal and canonical frames give the same Euclidean action, while only the proper diagonal frame reproduces the expected Lorentzian action on the Wheeler--DeWitt patch. Finally, we establish the uniqueness of the regularization scheme and show that imposing an appropriate fall-off behavior of the torsion tensor removes the remaining ambiguity and fixes the Euclidean action uniquely.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Spherical law for optimal layouts of random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40965)
*Jakob Stubenrauch,Christian Keup*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文把弹簧电布局（spring electric layout）视为统计物理（statistical physics）问题，精确求解独立边随机图的 Gibbs 布局系综，发现最优布局对所有图渐近相同，形成"受挫液体"（frustrated liquid）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨随机图的节点能否被嵌入辅助空间 $\mathbb{R}^{M}$ 使连通性变得局部，并检验弹簧电布局这一标准算法的实际效力。

Method: 将弹簧电布局纳入 Gibbs 系综（Gibbs ensemble）框架，在节点数 $N$ 大、嵌入维数 $M$ 固定的极限下，对边相互独立（independent edges）的图进行精确求解。

Result: 节点位置服从球面律（spherical law），在 $M$ 维球内均匀分布；$M=2$ 时与 Girko 圆律（Girko's circular law）密切相关。基态能量（ground state energy）对具有该密度的所有布局是平坦的，即某图的最优布局对其他图也渐近最优。

Conclusion: i.i.d. 图的最优布局是受挫液体（frustrated liquid）：宏观受约束但微观不可优化。文中还讨论了其与图划分（graph-partitioning）、拉普拉斯特征映射（Laplacian eigenmaps）及降维（dimensionality reduction）的关联，并指出该结论对相关图（correlated graphs）会失效。

Abstract: Can the nodes of a random graph be placed in an auxiliary space $\mathbb{R}^{M}$ so that its connectivity becomes local? The spring electric layout is a standard algorithm to try to find such local graph layouts; we study it as a statistical-physics problem by considering the Gibbs ensemble of spring-electric layouts. For graphs with independent edges we solve the model exactly at large number of nodes $N$ and fixed embedding dimension $M$. The node positions obey a spherical law: they are uniformly distributed in an $M$-dimensional ball; for $M=2$ this is closely related to Girko's circular law. Crucially, the ground state energy is flat across all layouts with that density: an optimal layout for one graph is asymptotically optimal for any other. Optimal layouts of i.i.d. graphs are therefore frustrated liquids, macroscopically constrained but microscopically unoptimizable. We discuss relations to graph-partitioning, Laplacian eigenmaps, and dimensionality reduction, and outline how this harsh result breaks down for correlated graphs.

</details>


### [165] [Existence of the thermodynamic limit for the Hopfield model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41938)
*Adriano Barra,Fabrizio Durante*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在载荷（load）α∈(0,∞)任意取值、有限逆温度与均匀外场下，具有K∼αN个独立同分布对称Rademacher模式（Rademacher patterns）的Hopfield模型其淬火压强（quenched pressure）存在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit），且无需复制对称（replica symmetry）、序参量集中（order-parameter concentration）或非凝聚假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hopfield模型热力学极限的存在性通常依赖复制对称、序参量集中或非凝聚（non-condensation）等额外假设，而本文旨在在无这些假设的更一般条件下建立极限存在性，并将结论推广到Gaussian模式等情形。

Method: 借助Hubbard–Stratonovich表示（Hubbard–Stratonovich representation）提取隐藏坐标超过δ√N的有限多个模式，用集体插值（collective interpolation）将其余二值模式替换为Gaussian模式，误差为体积一致（uniform in the volume）的O(δ^2)，并以离散立方体上的凸集中（convex concentration on the discrete cube）控制依赖无序的保留模式选择；对混合模型建立体积一致且对任意Ising势成立的Gaussian隐藏自旋截断界（truncation bound），归约到Ho及Chen、Ho、Issa关于紧致自旋非凸向量自旋玻璃（non-convex vector spin glasses）的结果。

Result: 证明了热力学极限等于含r个二值模式、Gaussian背景的混合模型极限压强对r的上确界（supremum）；对Gaussian模式给出Camilli、Contucci与Mingione所宣布存在性结果的替代证明，并扩展至均匀外场与额外二值模式；两种情形下压强均具有指数小偏差概率（exponentially small deviation probabilities）并几乎必然收敛，内能与熵在极限可微处收敛；额外二值Mattis模式可作标量变分归约（variational reduction）。

Conclusion: 零场下Rademacher压强支配含额外二值模式的Gaussian压强，后者又支配纯Gaussian压强；论文未给出显式公式，且证明过程系在作者问题设定与指导下通过与AI模型Claude（Opus 5.5）与ChatGPT（Sol 6.1）的大量交互生成。

Abstract: We prove that the quenched pressure of the Hopfield model with $K\simαN$ i.i.d. symmetric Rademacher patterns has a thermodynamic limit at every load $α\in(0,\infty)$, finite inverse temperature and uniform external field, with no replica-symmetry, order-parameter concentration or non-condensation hypothesis required. The limit is the supremum over $r$ of limiting pressures of mixed models with $r$ binary patterns in a Gaussian bulk. Via the Hubbard--Stratonovich representation we extract the finitely many patterns whose hidden coordinates exceed $δ\sqrt N$; a collective interpolation replaces the other binary patterns by Gaussian ones with error $O(δ^2)$ uniform in the volume, and convex concentration on the discrete cube controls the disorder-dependent choice of retained patterns. For the mixed models we prove a Gaussian hidden-spin truncation bound uniform in the volume and in an arbitrary Ising potential, and convergence for Gaussian bulks with finitely many site-field types, reducing to results of Ho and of Chen, Ho and Issa on non-convex vector spin glasses with compact spins. For Gaussian patterns we give an alternative proof of the existence result announced by Camilli, Contucci and Mingione, extended to a uniform field and an extra binary pattern. In both cases the pressure has exponentially small deviation probabilities and converges a.s.; internal energy and entropy converge wherever the limit is differentiable. An extra binary Mattis pattern admits a scalar variational reduction. At zero field the Rademacher pressure dominates the Gaussian one with an extra binary pattern, which dominates the pure Gaussian one. No explicit formula is given. The proofs were generated through extensive interaction with the AI models Claude (Opus~5.5) and ChatGPT (Sol~6.1), based on the problem formulation and guidance of the authors.
